Only the arriving guest's contact was shown to cleaners, and the office saw the departing guest's only inside a collapsed disclosure.

- `buildCleanerDepartingGuest` in `lib/jobs/guest-summary.ts` builds the cleaner's view from the reservation: name, phone, check-out time (or check-out day), party size. As with the arriving guest, never the email, profile link or reservation code. A booking with neither a name nor a phone is not shown as a guest.
- `GET /api/jobs/[id]/form` selects only those reservation fields and returns `departingGuest`, and adds `departingGuestName`/`departingGuestPhone` to `contact`.
- Cleaner: the Call sheet lists "Departing guest" before "Arriving guest", each labelled; the property info drawer has a "Departing guest" block above "Arriving guest" (renamed from "Next guest").
- Office: the Estate job detail's Guests (iCal) card shows the departing guest's name, phone and email in the open, under a "Departing" badge; the remaining booking fields stay in the "Departing booking details" disclosure.
- The classic (`/admin`, `/cleaner`) job pages are unchanged.

### Cleaner workflow presentation and charging (2026-10)

The Estate cleaner heading includes the Sydney job date. The dashboard route is a compact next-stop link to the existing route workflow; totals refresh while visible. Today's brief groups secondary details into disclosures and explains charging evidence. The cleaner journey uses SVG step icons and identity/job-scoped return-stage memory, reset by a full refresh. Travel's Start driving opens the existing route with this assigned job selected; an already active route remains authoritative.

Property access appears in Set up only; setup reference images appear in Get there and Set up. The stale previous-laundry briefing is suppressed in favor of the existing authoritative laundry-cycle card. During an ongoing inventory-enabled job, Urgent stock opens the existing request workspace inside a lazy disclosure, locked to the current property; collapse retains its draft.

Effective Jackson Airbnb turnover forms add required Ring/Minut charging proof, reusing an unconditional existing device check where possible. Otherwise a charging outcome requires either proof or an absent/unable-to-charge explanation. Augmentation follows rotational filtering so every clean has its charging checks. The established Jackson property-name convention determines applicability; Jackson P3's owner-confirmed Ring removal remains excluded. Reworks and historical submissions are unchanged. Shared finite status animations extend to cleaner, client and laundry surfaces; reduced-motion settings remain respected.

**Migration completeness (2026-10).** `20261009120000_reconcile_schema_contract` closes historical differences between migration replay and the Prisma schema. It transactionally applies onboarding defaults, two declared indexes and foreign-key deletion rules: job QA assignments/submissions/media override requests restrict deletion, while shopping lines retain their values and clear their optional property reference. The schema also records existing billing/team indexes and the existing empty blog-tags default, preserving them. No tables, columns or rows are dropped. Deploy the new code/image, run `npx prisma migrate deploy` (or `npm run db:deploy`) once against the intended database before starting the new app, and regenerate Prisma Client when building. Do not put migration execution in each replica's startup. Future schema changes must include and validate forward migrations; `db push` is not a production deployment substitute.

**Jobs filter disclosure (2026-10).** The Estate Jobs filter panel remembers its last expanded/collapsed choice in browser-local storage, scoped to the current account/impersonation identity and Jobs view context. Native disclosure state survives data refreshes without being forced open by React rerenders, and the saved display preference survives returning to Jobs or refreshing the page. First use remains expanded; blocked storage leaves the disclosure usable. This preference does not change filters, saved views or team defaults, and needs no database migration.

**Live API authorization and Admin rights (2026-10).** Protected API handlers authorize locally through `requireRole`/`requireSession` or audited wrappers, including live account/role validation, impersonation identity and OPS feature enforcement. Middleware supplies trusted path/method headers but no longer repeats API authorization via an HTTP session callback. Effective ADMIN and accounts holding ADMIN have full feature access independently of manager packs or policy-store availability; this never gives an impersonated target the actor’s owner rights. Revoked/inactive accounts remain unauthorized. Read-only impersonation, retained identity validation and route-specific sensitive-action checks remain enforced. Page navigation and retained-account resolution still use the trusted `NEXTAUTH_URL_INTERNAL` transport (the standard runner defaults to loopback at its Next port); those callbacks reject redirects and time out after five seconds. The Estate Jobs error card distinguishes session, permission, service and malformed-payload failures.

### Invoices and pay: merged Money page, paged client invoices and bulk actions (2026-11)

Phase 0 and Money sub-phase (a) of `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`. No schema change and no change to invoice rules, numbering, money arithmetic, transitions or Xero push semantics.

**One page.** `/v2/admin/finance` is titled "Invoices and pay", with tabs Client invoices (default), Cleaner invoices, Pay runs, Adjustments and time, Overview. Old `?tab=` values still land on their content (`invoices`, `payroll`, `adjustments`, `time`, `overview`; `time` opens the time sub-view of Adjustments and time) and `cleaner-invoices` is new. `/v2/admin/payroll` and `/v2/admin/cleaner-invoices` redirect to their tabs and carry other query parameters (`lib/admin/finance-redirects.ts`); `/v2/admin/payroll/[id]` is unchanged. The header holds the Xero connection state (from `XeroConnection`; "last sync" only when a sync time is stored), "Billing schedule" (the draft cadence settings, in a dialog that stays mounted so unsaved ticks survive closing it) and "New invoice" (From finished jobs = the existing generator; Blank invoice and Credit note shown disabled, "Coming in a later step"). The old KPI strip moved into the tabs it describes: Payroll due and Last run on Pay runs, Revenue MTD and Outstanding on Overview.

**Navigation and badges.** The Finance, Payroll and Cleaner invoices sidebar entries are one "Invoices and pay" entry. Its badge (`AdminAttentionCounts.finance`) is client invoices still to send (DRAFT or APPROVED) plus cleaner invoices awaiting the office (SUBMITTED or PAID_CLAIMED); the two parts are exposed as `clientInvoicesToSend` and `cleanerInvoices` and badge their own tabs ("N to send", "N to review") from `getMoneyAttentionCounts()`. Before, the Finance badge counted only cleaner invoices.

**Operations managers.** The page's tabs follow the feature that owns their APIs: Cleaner invoices, Pay runs and Adjustments and time are `payroll`; Client invoices and Overview are `finance` (`FINANCE_PAYROLL_TABS` in `lib/rbac/ops-catalog.ts`; the tab is read for the page only, never for an API path). The page shows only the tabs a manager's packs allow, and a manager with payroll but not finance is sent to `?tab=cleaner-invoices` from the sidebar. Before the merge, `?tab=payroll|adjustments|time` were gated as finance even though their data routes were payroll.

**Client invoices list.** `GET /api/admin/invoices?paginated=1` pages, sorts and filters in the database through `lib/admin/list-query.ts` (`parseListQuery` refuses an unknown sort column, page or page size with 400) and `lib/billing/client-invoice-list.ts`. Sort keys: number, client, period (start), created (the Due column sorts by this), sent, total, status, xero (export time). Owing is shown but not sortable (it is not a column). Filters: status chip (needs action = DRAFT, APPROVED or a pending/failed Xero reservation; overdue = SENT/PART_PAID past the due day; each status; all), client, period (invoices whose period starts in the range, organisation timezone), Xero state (not in Xero / in Xero / pending or failed — a failed push leaves the reservation PENDING, so the two are indistinguishable) and text search (number, client name or email). The response carries `counts`, real grouped totals under the same non-status filters. Without `paginated` the route still returns the original `{ clients, properties, rates, invoices }` for the classic screen; `?view=context` returns clients and properties; `?view=stats` returns the four figures above the table (`lib/billing/client-invoice-stats.ts`): finished jobs on no non-void invoice (a count — no dollar figure, since charges depend on rates), drafts and their stored total, owing on SENT/PART_PAID with the overdue count and oldest issued invoice, and invoices whose PAID stamp falls in this calendar month (recorded here; since step (d) this includes payments recorded from Xero).

**Due date.** There is no stored due date. The PDF prints created + `invoicing.defaultPaymentTermsDays` (14 when unset), and the list uses that same rule, read from current settings, for the Due column and "overdue". Changing the terms changes both.

**Rows and actions.** Each row shows one action chosen from its state (pending/failed Xero → Retry Xero; Draft → Review; Approved → Send; Sent or Part paid → Record payment, or Send reminder when overdue; Paid → Payment record; Void → View PDF). Every other existing action is in the row menu with its existing confirmation: edit, PDF, approve, send, re-send (new on screen; the send route already accepted SENT and PART_PAID and keeps PART_PAID's status), record payment, mark paid, payment record, push to Xero, void, reverse, reconcile/void (PIN + VOID) and delete (PIN). Void and Reverse now ask for confirmation, explaining the difference. The editor modal lost "Force status" (the API refuses every override) and its duplicate "Push to Xero". Filter, sort and page live in the URL; reads are cancelled when superseded and a response for an older filter is never shown.

**Shared actions and bulk.** The PATCH, send and Xero push logic moved out of the route handlers into `lib/billing/client-invoice-patch.ts`, `client-invoice-send.ts` and `client-invoice-xero.ts`, unchanged; the single routes and `POST /api/admin/invoices/bulk` call them. Bulk takes `{ action: "approve" | "send" | "approve-and-send" | "xero-push", ids }` (1–50 ids), processes rows one at a time and answers `{ id, invoiceNumber, ok, outcome: done|refused|unchanged, reason? }` per row with a summary. Bulk send refuses already-sent invoices (Re-send stays a per-row choice). Approve and send approves, then sends only if approval succeeded. Already approved / already in Xero are reported as "no change", never as done. One `CLIENT_INVOICE_BULK_ACTION` audit row records the request; each row's own action writes what its single route writes. `GET /api/admin/invoices/export` returns CSV of selected ids or of the filtered list, refusing more than 5,000 rows instead of truncating; formula-leading cells are escaped.

**Building blocks.** `components/v2/ui/`: `EDataTable`, `EBulkBar`/`EBulkButton`, `EFilterChips`, `EFilterPopover`, `ERowMenu`, `EPagination`, `EPill`. Conventions are in `docs/style/operational-ui.md`. The 1,698-line `estate-invoices.tsx` is now a thin entry over `components/v2/admin/finance/invoices/` (list, filters, columns, row actions, bulk, dialogs, requests; the editor moved to its own page in sub-phase (b), below).

### Invoice editor page (2026-11)

Money sub-phase (b) of `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`. `/v2/admin/finance/invoices/[id]` is the full-page client invoice editor; it replaces the editor modal (deleted) and the read-only summary that route used to render for admin (`components/v2/shared/invoice-summary-page.tsx` is kept for the client portal). No schema change and no change to invoice rules, transitions, numbering, money arithmetic or Xero push semantics: every change goes through the existing routes (`PATCH /api/admin/invoices/[id]` → `patchClientInvoice`, `…/send`, `…/xero-push`, `…/reconcile`, `DELETE`, `…/started-work-review`).

**Access.** Same roles as the invoice API (`requireRole([ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER])`). An operations manager needs the `finance` feature to open it and `manage` on it to edit; with `read` the page is view-only ("Your access to invoices is view only"). ADMIN has full rights regardless of packs (`lib/billing/client-invoice-access.ts`). The read model is `GET /api/admin/invoices/[id]?view=editor` (`lib/billing/client-invoice-editor.ts`), used by the page after every change; reads are cancelled when superseded and checked after parsing.

**Links.** The list's Review / "Review and edit" and the invoice number open the page with `?back=<list query>`; the page's "← Client invoices" returns to the same filters, sort and page (only list keys are carried; the list re-validates them). Old links without `back` (global search, bookmarks) return to the plain list.

**Layout** (as the concept `InvoiceEditor.dc.html`): back link; one h1 with number and status; subtitle with client, period and save state; Preview PDF, Save draft (editable invoices only), the ONE primary action and a "more" menu. The primary action is the list's own choice (`primaryInvoiceAction`), except that the list's "Review" opens this page, so a draft leads with Approve; a void invoice has none (Preview PDF covers it). The menu holds every other existing action (Download PDF, send/re-send, record payment, mark paid, payment record, push/retry Xero, void, reverse, reconcile/void, delete) with their existing dialogs, PIN and typed-phrase rules. Then a read-only stage bar (`EStageBar`, `lib/billing/client-invoice-stage.ts`), the lines card, and on the right "Before you approve", Send, Xero and History. At narrow widths the right column stacks under the lines and the table scrolls in its own region.

**Stage bar.** Draft → Approved → Sent to client → In Xero → Paid, from stored state only. Approval is optional and not recorded once the status moves on, so past it the stage reads "passed, not recorded". "In Xero" is done only when `xeroInvoiceId` is set; a PENDING reservation (in flight or failed) reads "Pending or failed". A paid invoice that was never emailed shows "Sent to client" as skipped. PART_PAID shows Paid as current with "Part paid". VOID replaces the bar with a "Void" state.

**Editing.** Offered on DRAFT and APPROVED invoices with no payment evidence, no Xero invoice and no pending export (`invoiceEditLock` in `lib/billing/client-invoice-guards.ts`, which also supplies the PATCH's refusal messages, so the page states the server's own reason). Otherwise the page is read-only with that reason: void; payment evidence; in Xero; export pending; sent (reverse to draft first); view-only access. Line description, quantity and rate are edited in place; reviewed shopping lines keep amount and grouping locked (description only). Saving is explicit — "Save draft" sends one `updateLines` PATCH for every changed line — because the PATCH has no last-write guard, so autosave could silently overwrite another tab or a bulk action. Changed lines are tinted and marked "Unsaved"; amounts and totals stay the stored figures until saved (no money arithmetic in the browser). Adding a line, removing one (PIN or password verified first, as before), reordering, GST and a manual line's property grouping save on their own and never discard typed drafts. While a save runs every field is frozen. A refused save keeps the drafts with the server's message and a Retry (a refusal rolls the PATCH back, so retry is safe); a lost response to an add or remove is not retried — the invoice is reloaded so the admin can see whether it landed. Leaving with unsaved input asks first (browser unload and the back link). Approve, send, payment, Xero and corrections wait until edits are saved or discarded. "Group by property" shows the PDF's own grouping (`groupInvoiceLines`) with collapsible section rows, counts and subtotals; a drag reorders within a section (by pointer, or keyboard: Space on the handle, arrows, Space). Job lines link to the job; the line note the PDF prints is shown read-only.

**Before you approve.** Computed on the server (`lib/billing/client-invoice-checks.ts`) from the functions approval runs: a pending Xero reservation and the started-work review (`startedWorkRefusal`, extracted from `assertStartedWorkReviewed`, which now throws its result; read without the job row locks) are marked "Blocks approval" when they fail, with the exact refusal text. Three advice checks are marked "Check" and do not block approval, because approval does not enforce them: every job line still resolves a client rate (`computeClientCharge`), no job is on another non-void invoice of the client (`billedJobLinesWhere`, extracted from the generator) or twice on this one, and the stored total matches the lines (`invoiceTotalsMismatch`, extracted from `getClientInvoice`). A check that cannot be read is "Unknown", never OK. Past approval the card shows the advice checks only.

**Send, Xero, History.** Send: a "To" field (blank = the client's invoice delivery contacts, listed) passed to the existing send dialog; the PDF is always attached; sending does not push to Xero. Xero: contact linked or not (`Client.xeroContactId`, set by the first push), the configured sales account code (and the disbursement account when shopping disbursements are on the invoice), and the state; since step (d) the state is the hourly Xero check and the card has "Refresh from Xero" (see "Xero status and payment pull"). History: this invoice's AuditLog rows (payments, reversals, reconciled voids, started-work reviews, single-invoice bulk actions) plus the dates the invoice stores (created, last sent, pushed to Xero, fully paid), in the organisation timezone. Line edits, approval, sending and Xero pushes write no audit row, and the card says so.

**Not offered (no supporting field or service):** editing Bill to, Period or Due; Client reference / PO; Add finished jobs to an existing draft; Add discount (lines cannot be negative); Add note to client (`metadata.notes` is not printed on the PDF); payment link and "push to Xero when sent" send options.

### Credit notes, blank invoices, discounts and server-side invoice locks (2026-11)

Money sub-phase (c) of `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`, built with the owner's "Additions approved for Money step (c)".

**Schema** (migration `20261111180000_client_invoice_credit_notes`, additive): `ClientInvoice.kind` (`ClientInvoiceKind` INVOICE | CREDIT_NOTE, default INVOICE), `creditsInvoiceId` (self-relation, `NO ACTION` on delete so a client cascade still removes both), `clientReference` (varchar 100), `clientNote` (text); `ClientInvoiceLine.discountPercent` (float, null unless a discount was entered as a percentage). A database CHECK ties `kind = CREDIT_NOTE` to a non-null `creditsInvoiceId`. Existing rows became INVOICE with nulls; nothing else changed (checksums before/after are in the review record).

**Server edit lock.** `invoiceContentLock` (`lib/billing/client-invoice-guards.ts`) is the one rule for content (lines, discount, GST, client reference, note to client, order): editable only while DRAFT or APPROVED with no payment evidence, no Xero invoice, no pending export and a readable started-work snapshot. The PATCH refuses content edits with its reason (409) and the editor shows the same reason. Status moves and payments still follow the transition graph. The internal `metadata.notes` / `metadata.dueDate` PATCH fields are not printed anywhere and are not locked.

**Server PIN.** Removing a line (`PATCH { removeLineId, security }`) and deleting an invoice (`DELETE` body `{ security }`) verify the PIN or password on the server with `verifySensitiveAction` (bcrypt comparison; the credential is never logged or stored), before the row lock. Refusals use the existing shape `{ error: "INVALID_SECURITY_VERIFICATION" | "PIN_OR_PASSWORD_REQUIRED" }`, status 423; both are refused (403) while impersonating, as the reconcile route already did. Reconcile / void already verified the PIN on the server and is unchanged apart from refusing an invoice with live credit notes. The standard Void has no PIN prompt and needs none.

**Client reference / PO** and **note to client.** Editable while the content lock allows; reference drawn in the editor header (saved on leaving the field), note via "Add note to client" in the lines footer. Both print on the PDF ("Your reference: …", a "Note" box). The reference is searchable in the list and sent to Xero first in the reference: `PO · Service period yyyy-mm-dd – yyyy-mm-dd` (unchanged when there is no PO; a credit note sends `PO · Credit for INV-…`). The idempotency key stays `client-invoice-<id>`. The note is a new column, not `metadata.notes`, because that field was written by the PATCH for internal notes whose audience was never defined, and existing internal notes must not start printing to clients.

**Discount line.** `PATCH { addDiscount: { amount } | { percent }, description? }` adds a `DISCOUNT` line (quantity 1, negative price). A percentage is of the current subtotal of every non-discount line; the resulting amount is stored and `discountPercent` keeps the basis (it is not recalculated when other lines change). Totals come from `calculateShoppingAwareInvoiceTotals`, so GST is charged on the discounted taxable amount. A discount (or any later edit) that takes the taxable lines below zero is refused. Its amount cannot be edited (remove and re-add); it is locked like any line once the invoice is. Xero receives it as a negative line on the default sales account. Every line consumer was checked: PDF grouping (under "Other charges", subtracted in the section and the total), owing/receivables, stats, CSV export (negative amounts no longer formula-escaped), started-work review (no job), shopping locks (no shopping ids), double-billing (no job).

**Blank invoice.** `POST /api/admin/invoices { kind: "BLANK", clientId, periodStart, periodEnd }` creates a DRAFT with no lines, numbered from the CLIENT sequence, GST from settings, period read as Sydney calendar days; the "New invoice ▾ → Blank invoice" dialog then opens the editor. An invoice with no lines cannot be approved, sent, paid or pushed (`emptyInvoiceRefusal`, also listed in "Before you approve").

**Credit notes.** `POST /api/admin/invoices/[id]/credit-note` on a SENT, PART_PAID or PAID invoice creates a DRAFT credit note numbered on its own `CREDIT_NOTE` rail (`CN-0001`, settable in Settings → invoice numbering), with every original line copied and negated (description, quantity, category so GST and the Xero account follow, property grouping) and deliberately no job, shopping run or shopping charge. The office reduces or removes lines for a partial credit. Its magnitude may not exceed the original total less approved/sent credit notes already issued (an over-limit pre-filled draft may only shrink; approving or sending re-checks under the original's row lock). Credit notes are never paid; they go to Xero only once approved or sent, as a draft `ACCRECCREDIT` credit note with positive amounts (key `client-credit-note-<id>`, its id stored in `xeroInvoiceId`), not allocated to the invoice (Xero allocates only authorised credit notes). An invoice with live credit notes cannot be voided or reconcile-voided. "Reverse" is now only for APPROVED invoices; on SENT and later the API refuses it and the screen offers "Issue credit note".

**Owing.** `invoiceAmountOwing` (`lib/finance/receivables.ts`) subtracts approved and sent credit notes. A SENT/PART_PAID invoice whose credits cover its whole total with no payment shows "Fully credited" (a display state; the status is unchanged so the graph is untouched), owes nothing, is not overdue and is not counted in Outstanding. Recording a payment settles the invoice to PAID once payments plus credits reach the total. Credit notes themselves are never outstanding. Client account stats and the client payment routes use the same rule.

**Client portal.** Credit notes are shown deliberately in the client's invoice list ("Credit note for INV-…", negative total) and on the summary page and PDF, and are never payable: the pay and payment-link routes refuse them and charge what is owed after credits. Property-scoped portal views exclude them, as they already excluded manual invoices.

**PDF.** "CREDIT NOTE" title with "Credits invoice INV-…", "Total credit", no due date or bank details; negative amounts print as "-$12.50". The due day comes from `invoiceDueDay` (`lib/billing/invoice-due.ts`), the same function the list uses, in the organisation timezone.

**Emails.** The invoice email's button opens the client portal page `/v2/client/finance/invoices/[id]` (was the admin `/admin/invoices`); the document-delivery email did the same with the old `/client/finance`.

**History and fixes found in this step.** Single send and Xero push now write audit rows (`CLIENT_INVOICE_SENT`, `CLIENT_INVOICE_XERO_PUSH`: who, outcome, recipient count; never addresses, bodies or credentials). A bulk action keeps its one request row and now also writes a `CLIENT_INVOICE_BULK_ROW` per affected invoice so it appears in that invoice's History. GET/DELETE `/api/admin/invoices/[id]` and POST `…/send` answer 401/403 for the session guards (were 400). An unreadable started-work snapshot no longer breaks the GET route or the editor: the invoice opens read only with "The started-work review data … could not be read", and approval stays refused. The list gained the old screen's Refresh button.

### Xero status and payment pull; cleaner invoices on the shared design (2026-11)

Money sub-phases (d) and (e) of `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`. No schema change: the pull's state lives in `ClientInvoice.metadata.xeroSync`, Xero payments in the existing `metadata.payments[]` ledger, the last whole run in `AppSetting` `xeroInvoiceSync`, and `APPROVED` is a new value of the string `CleanerInvoiceSubmission.status`.

**Xero reads** (`lib/xero/client.ts`, parsing in `lib/xero/xero-documents.ts`). `readXeroInvoices(ids)` reads up to 50 ACCREC invoices per request (`Invoices?IDs=`), `readXeroCreditNote(id)` one ACCRECCREDIT credit note; both use the push's token handling and never throw: no connection, HTTP errors, network errors, a persistent 429 (retried once after `Retry-After` when that is at most a minute), unparseable JSON, a missing total/status/amount, and an id Xero did not return all come back as typed failures. `If-Modified-Since` is deliberately not used (an unchanged invoice would be indistinguishable from a missing one).

**The rules** (`lib/billing/client-invoice-xero-sync-rules.ts`, pure). Checked in order: Xero VOIDED/DELETED → needs review (never voided here); totals differ by more than half a cent → needs review (lines never edited); a credit note is never paid, only confirmed; overpayment/prepayment allocations, or more credited in Xero than here → needs review; a payment recorded from Xero that Xero no longer lists → needs review (never reversed); a payment of zero or less → needs review; new payments that with what is already paid and credited would exceed the total (e.g. the same money recorded by hand) → needs review, nothing recorded; otherwise the new payments are recorded oldest first; Xero PAID but not settled here → needs review.

**Recording** (`lib/billing/client-invoice-xero-sync.ts`). Each new Xero payment goes through `patchClientInvoice` — the function "Record payment" uses — with `recordPayment.method = "XERO"`, the Xero payment date, and an internal `xeroPayment { paymentId, trigger }`. Under the invoice row lock the payment path refuses method XERO from anyone but the sync, records a Xero payment id at most once (the id is kept on the ledger entry with `source: "XERO"`), never touches a PAID or VOID invoice, refuses a payment above what is still owed (`XERO_PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_OWING` → needs review), and settles to PAID by the existing owing rule (payments + approved/sent credits ≥ total). The client receipt email is NOT sent for a payment read from Xero (owner decision pending). The payment audit row carries `source`, `xeroPaymentId` and `trigger`; a separate `CLIENT_INVOICE_XERO_SYNC` row is written when the state changes or payments were recorded. Scheduled runs attribute audit rows to the earliest active administrator (an audit row needs a user) and History shows them as "Xero sync (scheduled)"; with no active administrator no payment is recorded and the state is unknown.

**State per invoice** (`metadata.xeroSync`): `IN_XERO`, `PAID_IN_XERO`, `NEEDS_REVIEW` (with reason), `UNKNOWN` (with reason, "Xero state unknown: …"; the last good figures are kept and labelled), plus Xero's status, check time and last successful read. A failed read changes nothing else on the invoice.

**Runs.** Worker job `xero-invoice-sync`, hourly at :20 Australia/Sydney (`workers/boss.ts`, honours `SNEEK_DISABLED_JOBS` / `SNEEK_ENABLED_JOBS`): a logged no-op when Xero is not connected; otherwise up to 200 invoices (in Xero, not PAID/VOID, least recently updated first) and 20 credit notes (approved/sent, in Xero, not yet fully allocated there), with a per-invoice outcome summary. `lastSuccessAt` advances only when every read in the run succeeded. Manual: `POST /api/admin/invoices/xero-sync { ids ≤ 50 }` (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER with the finance pack, as the push) answers per row; "Refresh from Xero" on the editor's Xero card, in the list header (this page's invoices that are in Xero) and in the bulk bar.

**Screens.** The list's Xero column and the editor's Xero card show Not in Xero / Push pending / In Xero (not checked yet, or last checked {time}) / Paid in Xero (recorded) / Needs review: {reason} / State unknown: {reason}; the Xero filter gained In Xero checked, Paid in Xero, Needs review and State unknown (JSON-path filters); the CSV has the check state and reason. The editor's stage bar keeps "In Xero" done only with a Xero id, now noting Needs review / State unknown / Paid in Xero; "Paid" stays the local status. The header badge shows "last checked …" only from the stored last successful run (it used `XeroConnection.lastSyncAt`, which is a token refresh time). "Paid this month … as recorded here" is unchanged: it is still true.

**Pre-existing fixes.** The Stripe webhook (`checkout.session.completed`, now `lib/finance/stripe-checkout-payment.ts`) recorded the invoice total as paid whatever was charged and never updated a PART_PAID invoice; it now records `amount_total`, settles to PAID only when payments + credits reach the total (else PART_PAID), is idempotent per checkout session (ledger entry), and records nothing when Stripe omits the amount or reports the payment unpaid (logged for review). `POST /api/client/invoices/[id]/pay` stored the invoice total on its `ClientPayment` attempt row; it now stores the amount charged (owing after credits). What clients are charged is unchanged.

**Cleaner invoices: transitions** (`lib/cleaner/invoice-transitions.ts`, the one table for routes, bulk and screen). SUBMITTED → APPROVED (ADMIN only, 403 otherwise); SUBMITTED/APPROVED → CHANGES_REQUESTED or VOID; APPROVED/XERO_EXPORTING → XERO_PUSHED (push); APPROVED/XERO_PUSHED/PAID_CLAIMED → PAID (record payment); PAID_CLAIMED → SUBMITTED, or XERO_PUSHED when a Xero bill exists (reject claim); delete from SUBMITTED, APPROVED, CHANGES_REQUESTED, PAID_CLAIMED or VOID without Xero bill or payment; SENDING → delivery review. Nothing changes with payment evidence or (except payment / claim rejection) once in Xero. Existing rows keep their status; a legacy SUBMITTED invoice is approved before push or payment; a legacy XERO_PUSHED one can be paid. Every transition writes `CLEANER_INVOICE_STATUS_UPDATE` with before/after and the action. The services live in `lib/cleaner/invoice-admin-actions.ts` (status, push, delete) and `invoice-admin-notify.ts` (payee emails, moved unchanged). A payee may claim payment on APPROVED too.

**Where APPROVED counts.** Needs action (chip, tab badge and the merged Finance badge: SENDING, SUBMITTED, APPROVED, XERO_EXPORTING, PAID_CLAIMED); a live claim on its work everywhere that excludes only VOID/CHANGES_REQUESTED (payroll engine, invoice claim, holiday rates, job delete guard, expected-invoice pairing); never paid (only PAID with its paid block is). Shopping settlement reads no submission status.

**Cleaner invoices: list.** `GET /api/admin/cleaner-invoices?paginated=1` (status chip, `q` number/name/email, `cleaner`, `from`/`to` Sydney days, sort number/period/submitted/jobs/hours/total/status, paging through `list-query`, grouped counts); `?view=stats` (awaiting action, value of SUBMITTED+APPROVED, in Xero, paid — stored totals); `?view=context` (payees); with no parameters the old bare array of the newest 200 is unchanged for the classic `/admin/cleaner-invoices` screen. `POST …/bulk { approve | xero-push, ids ≤ 50 }` with per-row outcomes through the single-row services (approve by an operations manager: 403). `GET …/export` CSV. Record payment has no bulk path (each needs its own method, amount and bank account).

**Review page** `/v2/admin/finance/cleaner-invoices/[id]` (payroll pack for operations managers, also mapped in `ops-catalog`): stage bar Submitted → Approved → In Xero → Paid (approval of a legacy row reads "passed"), lines from the snapshot with the stored total, change note, payment record, the payee's claim with proof thumbnails, the current submitted-vs-expected comparison for that payee and period (`getExpectedInvoicesForPeriod`, stated as today's forecast, not a snapshot), history from audit rows, and the actions this role may take. **Admin PDF** `GET /api/admin/cleaner-invoices/[id]/pdf` renders the office copy from the submission's own snapshot with the cleaner download's renderer (`renderCleanerInvoicePdf`); it does not re-run the live invoice calculation, which would print a different document.

**Components.** The 834-line workspace and 677-line forecast panel are split into `cleaner-invoice-{model,filters,columns,dialogs,bulk}`, `use-cleaner-invoice-{list,actions}`, `review/*`, `expected-invoice-{model,cards}` (each ≤ 315 lines). The Submitted / Predicted switch uses the shared filter chips; the Predicted view is unchanged.

### Forms library: one Forms page, draft versions and bulk edits (2026-11)

Forms step (library half) of `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`. The builder redesign (drag, multi-select inside one form, undo/redo, test as cleaner, version list UI) is the next step. Cleaner forms, submission validation and job-form resolution are unchanged.

**One page.** `/v2/admin/forms` is titled "Forms", with tabs Job forms (default), QA templates, Checklist blocks, Message templates, Results and stats. QA templates embeds `QaTemplatesManager`; Checklist blocks has two views, the checklist library (`view=library`, was `/v2/admin/checklists`) and the per-service checklists editor (`view=service`, was the forms page's `?tab=checklists`, which still opens it); Message templates has the notification and message template workspaces (`view=notifications|messages`, was `/v2/admin/templates?tab=`); Results and stats is the old statistics page in panel mode (same data and definitions, `lib/forms/form-stats-data.ts`; its filter form posts back to `?tab=stats`). `/v2/admin/qa-templates`, `/v2/admin/templates`, `/v2/admin/checklists` and `/v2/admin/forms/stats` redirect to their tabs with their other query parameters (`lib/admin/forms-redirects.ts`), so `?templateId=&weeks=` links to the stats page still open the same view. `/v2/admin/checklists/coverage`, `/v2/admin/forms/new` and `/v2/admin/forms/[id]/edit` are unchanged routes. The four sidebar entries (Checklists library, QA templates, Forms & stats, Templates) are one "Forms" entry. One h1; each tab has a screen-reader h2. "New form ▾" (admins) links to the existing job-form launcher, the QA templates tab and the message templates view.

**Operations managers.** On this page QA templates stays a `quality` feature and Message templates a `communications` feature, as their old pages and APIs are; every other tab is `forms` (`FORMS_TAB_FEATURES` in `lib/rbac/ops-catalog.ts`, read for the page only, never for an API path). The page shows only the tabs a manager's packs allow and the sidebar entry falls back to `?tab=qa` or `?tab=messages` for a manager without forms. Write controls follow the held roles the APIs accept: form-template and QA-template writes are ADMIN only, so an operations manager sees the library and QA templates read-only (no checkboxes, bulk bar, Edit, row menus, "New form", "+ New" group; QA "New template", Edit and Reset hidden and the Active switch locked). Before, the QA templates page offered those buttons to managers and the API answered 403.

**Job forms tab** (concept `FormsLibrary.dc.html`). Left: Groups (All forms, each group with its count, "+ New", rename and delete for admins), Tags (click to filter), Drafts, Archived; at phone width the panel stacks above the table. Right: search over form names, section titles and question labels (live and draft), Status (live, live + draft, draft) and Used by (default for its job type, property override, made for one job, not used) popovers, "Has problems N", the table, the bulk bar and paging. Columns: Form (name; version, role and job type), Status, Used by, Questions, Avg time, Completion, Checks, Changed (organisation timezone), Edit plus a row menu (duplicate as draft, publish / publish changes, archive or unarchive, results and stats, delete with PIN or password). Filter, sort and page live in the URL beside `tab`; reads are cancelled when superseded and rows are only shown for the query that produced them; selection resets when the filters change.

- **List route.** `GET /api/admin/form-templates/library` (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER) reads paging and sort through `parseListQuery` (sorts: name, status, usedBy, questions, checks, changed; unknown sort, filter value or page size → 400) and filters `scope` (active = not archived | drafts = draft or live with a draft | archived), `group` (id or `none`), `tag`, `status`, `usedBy`, `problems=1`, `q` (`lib/forms/library-list.ts`). The template set is read whole (one row per form) because search, Checks, Questions and Used by come from the schema JSON; paging is applied after the filters, so `total` and `counts` (active, drafts, archived, problems within the current filters, per group, tags) are real.
- **Used by** resolves with the runtime rules (`lib/forms/library-usage.ts` over `resolve-job-template.ts`): job-scoped → "1 job"; registered as a property override → active properties whose override for its job type points at it; the newest active global template of its job type → active properties without a usable override for that job type; any other live form → "Not used"; drafts and archived → "–".
- **Questions** counts fields and sub-fields of the live schema except read-only blocks (`countQuestions`).
- **Avg time and Completion** use the stats tab's definitions (`lib/forms/submission-stats.ts`, which the stats tab now imports): submissions to this template in the stats tab's default range (8 weeks from the start of the week); Avg time = mean of submit time minus the recorded `__startedAt`/`startedAt`; Completion = mean share of required, always-shown questions answered (no condition on the question or its section, no rotation, no device-status exception, no balcony applicability; uploads count from `uploads[id]`). Fewer than 5 timed or parsed submissions, or no such questions, shows "–", never 0% or 100%. Neither column is sortable (they are computed for the page's rows only).
- **Checks** (`lib/forms/template-checks.ts`) run the live schema — and the draft, prefixed "Draft:" — through `formTemplateSchemaZ`, `lintTemplateSchema` and the field-type registry (unknown question type = problem; choice question without options and min above max = warning). Status: Passes, N warnings, N problems, with the messages in the hint. "Has problems" counts validator results only.
- **Usage check.** Under the checks, "Never answered: …" lists required, always-shown questions (as above) with no answer in any submission of the last 90 days that arrived after the current live version was recorded, and only when there are at least 20 such submissions. It is a warning for review, not a validator result.

**Draft and live versions.** New table `FormTemplateVersion` (template, kind PUBLISHED | DRAFT, version, schema, name, service type, note, restored-from, author id, timestamps; unique on template + kind + version). Before this there was no version table: `lib/phase4/template-versions.ts` (API only, no screen) treats sibling FormTemplate rows named "… vN" as versions and switches `isActive` between them, which changes what jobs resolve to, so it could not hold drafts; it is left unchanged and unused by the library. A template has at most one DRAFT row, its working copy (`lib/forms/template-drafts.ts`). Saving writes only the draft — `FormTemplate.schema`, `version`, `name` and `serviceType` are untouched, so jobs and cleaners keep the published form. Publishing (`lib/forms/template-publish.ts`, used by `POST …/[id]/publish` and bulk publish) copies the draft to the live row, raises `version` to one above the highest recorded, writes a PUBLISHED row, removes the draft, then applies the existing rules (active, published now, unarchived; the previous active global of the job type is archived; property-scoped and job-scoped rows are skipped on both sides). Publishing a template without a draft behaves as before and records a baseline PUBLISHED row if its current version has none. A draft whose structure fails `formTemplateSchemaZ` cannot be saved or published. Restore (`POST …/[id]/versions/[versionId]/restore`) makes an earlier PUBLISHED version the draft (note "Restored from vN"); it refuses with 409 when a draft exists unless `replaceDraft: true`; no version row is ever rewritten. `GET …/[id]/versions` lists history; `GET|PUT|DELETE …/[id]/draft` reads, saves or discards the draft. Concurrency: every save may send the edit token the editor loaded (the draft's `updatedAt`, else the template's); a stale token is refused with 409 `STALE_TEMPLATE`, the draft update is conditional on the row read, and two first saves racing are separated by the unique key. Publishing accepts the same token.

**Builder.** `/v2/admin/forms/[id]/edit` opens the draft when there is one (schema, name, service type and its token). "Save draft" now saves the draft (`PUT …/draft`) with the existing 409 stale-write guard; before, it overwrote the live schema. A live form with a saved draft shows "Unpublished changes" and a "Publish changes" button (disabled while edits are unsaved); "Publish (make live)" and "Archive" are as before. The legacy `PATCH /api/admin/form-templates/[id]` (classic `/admin/forms` and `components/forms`) still writes the live row directly and records no version.

**Bulk.** `POST /api/admin/form-templates/bulk` (ADMIN) takes `{ templateIds (1–50), operation, dryRun?, note? }` (`lib/forms/template-bulk.ts`, `template-bulk-lifecycle.ts`, contract in `template-bulk-types.ts`) and answers one outcome per form: changed / would-change (dry run) / unchanged / refused, with a reason and before/after for each affected field. `dryRun: true` changes nothing. Forms run one after another and a failure on one is reported for that form. One `BULK_FORM_TEMPLATES` audit row records an applied request.

- *edit-question*, *add-section*, *find-replace* apply to each form's working copy (draft, else live) and are SAVED AS DRAFTS (`lib/forms/template-bulk-edits.ts`). Archived forms are refused. An edit that would add a validation error the form did not have is refused for that form.
- *edit-question* matching rule (`lib/forms/template-questions.ts`): by field id, or by section title + question label + question type, comparing title and label trimmed, with whitespace collapsed and case ignored, and the type exactly. Exactly one match is edited; no match, or more than one in a form, leaves that form unchanged with the reason. Properties: label, help text, required, minimum photos and maximum files (photo/video/file only), priority, details when No (yes/no only). The picker (`GET /api/admin/form-templates/questions?ids=`) offers only questions in the selected forms, with "in N of M".
- *add-section* inserts a titled section (optionally tick-box questions, one per line, required) at the start (after arrival evidence) or end (before trailing standard sections), with fresh ids; a form that already has a section of that title is unchanged.
- *find-replace* changes section titles and descriptions, question labels, help text, instructions, placeholders and options — never ids or answer keys. Renaming an option that a condition compares against is refused for that form.
- *move-to-group*, *set-tags* change library organisation only.
- *publish*, *duplicate*, *archive* call the single-form services. Bulk publish also refuses a form whose schema to publish has a validation error, and refuses every selected form when two selected global forms share a job type (publishing one would archive the other); the preview names the live forms that publishing would archive. Duplicates are drafts copied from the live row (not a pending draft) and keep group and tags.

The screen requires a preview of the exact inputs and selection before "Save as drafts" / the action is enabled; inputs are frozen while a request runs and kept after a failure, and after a failed apply the next step is a fresh preview. Results stay on screen with the selection (refused and unchanged forms included).

**Groups and tags.** New table `FormTemplateGroup` (name, sortOrder) and `FormTemplate.groupId` (nullable, ON DELETE SET NULL) and `FormTemplate.tags` (text array, default empty). `GET|POST /api/admin/form-templates/groups`, `PATCH|DELETE …/groups/[groupId]` (read ADMIN and OPS_MANAGER, write ADMIN; names unique ignoring case; delete ungroups its forms). Resolution never reads groups or tags.

**Migration.** `20261111150000_form_template_library` creates the enum, both tables, the two columns and their indexes and keys, and inserts one PUBLISHED baseline row per existing template copied verbatim from its schema, name, service type and version, dated at the template's `updatedAt`, with a note saying whether it was live, a draft or archived. No existing row is changed. Run `npx prisma migrate deploy` before starting this build.

**Not offered yet.** Compare view; Import; a version history / restore screen (the API exists; the builder step adds it); usage-based checks beyond "never answered"; sorting by Avg time or Completion; an export bulk action.

### Form builder: outline, questions, shared settings, versions and test as cleaner (2026-11)

Forms step (builder half) of `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`, built to the "Template builder" design. What a job resolves to is unchanged except where a form uses the new per-room repeat (none did before).

**Page.** `/v2/admin/forms/[id]/edit` (ADMIN). Header: "← Forms", the form name as the page's one h1 with its state pill ("Draft of vN+1" / "Live vN" / "Not live" / "Archived"), a line with where the live version is used (the library's usage rule, `lib/forms/library-usage.ts`; "could not be read" when unavailable, never zero) and the draft's save state, then Undo, Redo, Versions, Test as cleaner, More (form settings — name, service type, theme, stock selection —, checks, duplicate, archive/unarchive, discard draft) and "Publish vN". Three panes: Outline (sortable sections with question counts and per-room / "if property has" badges, "+ Add", "Add a question" with every registered type, Heading/Divider, "Insert saved block…"); Questions of the selected section (sortable rows with type, wording and badges, Select all, Expand/Collapse all, a "Section settings" disclosure, bulk bar: Move to section, Copy to other forms, Duplicate, Save as block, Delete); right: the properties panel for one question, or "Editing N questions together" for several, and "Cleaner sees". At phone width the panes are steps (Outline · Questions · Settings / Preview). Files: `components/v2/admin/forms/builder/*` (shell `form-builder.tsx`; state hooks `use-builder-document`, `use-draft-autosave`, `use-builder-editing`, `use-builder-lifecycle`).

**Badges are real behaviours only** (`lib/forms/question-badges.ts`): Required, Min/Max files, "Every Nth clean", "Shown if …", "If property …", "'Yes' shows N" (rules that watch the question), "'No' asks for details", Device status, High priority, Scored, and "Never answered in 90 days" only from the library's usage check (`lib/forms/submission-stats.ts`). The design's "Raises urgent stock below 3" and "'Yes' opens a damage report" are not drawn: no form schema drives those today.

**Editing.** Pure schema operations in `lib/forms/builder-ops.ts`, `builder-dnd.ts` and `multi-edit.ts`; drag and drop with dnd-kit (pointer, touch, keyboard sensors) to reorder sections, reorder questions, and move questions (the whole selection when a selected row is dragged) by dropping on an outline section; the row menu offers Move up / down / to section. Multi-edit offers only settings valid for every selected type, shows "Mixed", and writes only what changed; Reset restores the selection's values from when it was made. Undo/redo (`lib/forms/builder-history.ts`): bounded (100), typing in one input coalesces into one step, Ctrl/Cmd+Z and Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+Z / Y handled on the builder's root element and ignored inside text inputs; history resets on publish and reload.

**New settings in the panel.** "How often" (every clean, or every 2nd–12th clean: `frequency: "ROTATIONAL"` + `rotationEveryNCleans`, the keys the property checklist profile writes and `lib/accountability/rotation-schema.ts` filters per property; a generated "CONDITIONAL" question keeps its value). Show/hide rules can watch another question or a property fact (`{ propertyField, value }`, equality only, keys limited to `lib/forms/property-conditions.ts`, all in `jobFormPropertySelect`). Evidence category and device status are now editable too. Per-room repeat is a SECTION setting (`repeatBy: "bedrooms" | "bathrooms"`), expanded at read time by `lib/forms/repeat-sections.ts` with the compose id scheme (`${id}-${n}`, `__bedN` / `__bathN`) inside `resolveEffectiveJobForm`; only forms that use it read the room counts (from the job's property, else the database), and a missing count fails closed. The property checklist profile is unchanged and writes the same keys.

**Saving.** Autosave to the DRAFT (`PUT …/draft`, 1.5 s debounce, one save at a time, edit token on every save). A 409 stops autosave and offers "Reload theirs" or "Keep mine as a copy" (a new form with these edits as its draft); any other failure keeps the edits with Retry; leaving with unsaved edits warns. Fields are not frozen during an autosave: the payload is a snapshot and later edits go in the next save.

**Versions** drawer: draft and published versions with author, time (organisation timezone) and note; View shows a version read-only in the cleaner renderer with a diff against live (`lib/forms/version-diff.ts`; `GET …/versions/[versionId]`); "Restore as draft" uses the existing restore endpoint and asks before sending `replaceDraft: true` on 409.

**Publish** goes through `assertPublishable` in `lib/forms/template-publish.ts` — the single publish path used by the builder, the publish route and bulk publish: the strict save check plus no check ERROR (`CHECKS_FAILED`, 422). The dialog shows the impact panel, an optional version note (saved on the draft first) and the real in-flight rule: jobs not yet submitted get the new version the next time the form loads; a cleaner with the form open is asked to reload before submitting (the revision check keeps their answers and photos); submitted jobs keep the schema they were filled in on.

**Test as cleaner** renders the whole form in the real renderer in `mode="sandbox"` (media are pretend counts — nothing uploaded — and the job extras are hidden), with a sample property (rooms, balcony, stock, laundry), and "Check answers" runs the phone's validation and the server's (`POST …/test-run`, `lib/forms/test-run.ts`), which writes nothing.

**Copy to other forms / saved blocks.** Copy runs the bulk service with the new operation `insert-questions` (`lib/forms/template-bulk-insert.ts`): preview, then drafts; fresh collision-free ids per form; a rule watching a question outside the copied set is dropped and reported. Saved blocks live in one AppSetting row `formBuilder.savedBlocks` (`lib/forms/saved-blocks.ts`, `GET/POST /api/admin/form-templates/blocks`, `DELETE …/blocks/[blockId]`), because the "Checklist blocks" library stores checklist modules with only a few item properties.

**Duplicate from the builder** (`POST …/duplicate` with `{ from: "draft" }`) copies the draft when one exists — what the admin sees — otherwise the live form; the library's duplicate still copies the live form.

**One typed schema.** `lib/forms/types.ts` types `frequency`, `rotationEveryNCleans`, `FormSection.repeatBy` and property rules (`FormCondition`). `formTemplateSchemaZ` stays the tolerant reader; `validateFormSchemaForSave` refuses unknown question types (legacy `upload`, `textarea`, `inventory`, `laundry_confirm` are accepted and flagged by the checks), malformed rules, property keys cleaners do not receive, and invalid cadence or room types — on draft save, on create, and on publish. Restoring an old version skips it (history is copied as published; publish then enforces it). `lib/forms/editable-schema.ts` reads any stored schema into the builder shape.

**Legacy PATCH** `PATCH /api/admin/form-templates/[id]`: `schema` and `serviceType` now save the DRAFT through the same service (strict, 409 on a stale token); `name` and `isActive` stay direct metadata edits, audited as `FORM_TEMPLATE_METADATA`. Its callers were the retired classic builder and the classic forms page's builder tab, both removed.

**Server validation on submit.** The cleaner submit route runs `collectFormErrors(..., { extended: true })` on the same effective schema, property projection and laundry state it already validates against (`lib/forms/submission-warnings.ts`). Extended rules — number ranges and format, email, phone, date/time format, option membership (an "Other" answer is valid only when allowed), details-when-no, rating/slider/scale ranges, maximum files — are stored as `data.__validationWarnings` (server-computed; anything the client sent under that key is overwritten) and shown on the job's submission review as "Answer checks to review"; the submission is accepted. Rules the route already enforced reject exactly as before. `ENFORCE_EXTENDED_FORM_VALIDATION = false` in `lib/forms/validate-submission.ts` switches both the server rejection and the phone's inline display on in a later release.

**Field types.** Every registered type has an audit case (`tests/components/form-field-type-audit.test.tsx`). Fixed: phone, date, time, datetime, option membership, rating/slider/scale ranges and maximum files are now validated (warnings); "Searchable" on a dropdown is implemented (filter box, the chosen answer always stays listed); barcode is a real scanner (`components/v2/cleaner/barcode-field-input.tsx`: BarcodeDetector where available, else the ZXing decoder already in the app; no camera, refused permission or a failed decoder fall back to the text box with a message; the method is stored under `__barcodeCapture`).

**Renderer modes.** `FormRenderer` takes `mode: "live" | "preview" | "sandbox"` (default live, unchanged). Preview/sandbox replace media capture with `components/v2/cleaner/sandbox-media-input.tsx` and hide the job extras. The builder's "Cleaner sees" phone uses preview; the old look-alike `form-preview.tsx` was removed.

**QA templates.** Every content save (name or schema) records a revision in the audit log (`QA_TEMPLATE_REVISION`, with the previous content; count shown as "Revisions"). The `version` column is deliberately not bumped: it decides which active template of a scope inspections resolve to. Overlap warning when two ACTIVE templates share job type and scope, naming the one that resolves (highest version; a tie is reported as either) — `lib/qa/template-conflicts.ts`. Duplicate (inactive copy, same scope), Delete (deactivated instead when any inspection used it), filter by job type and scope, sort, Preview. Edit, Duplicate, Reset and Delete sit in the row menu; ADMIN only.

**Classic builder retired.** `components/forms/{form-builder,builder-canvas,field-palette,properties-panel,condition-editor,reference-media-editor,theme-editor,field-icon,form-preview}` deleted; `device-status-input` moved to `components/shared/`. `/admin/forms/[id]/edit` and `/admin/forms/new` redirect to the v2 builder (id and query kept; same ADMIN admission), `/admin/forms/stats` to the stats tab. `/admin/forms` itself stays (its Submissions tab — submission edit and media add/remove — and Checklists tab have no v2 equivalent yet); its builder tab, which wrote live forms, now only links to the v2 builder. The classic cleaner renderer pieces (`field-renderer`, `field-input`, `guided-capture`, …) stay: the classic cleaner and QA pages use them.

### Quality hub: one page for review, issues, cleaners, inspectors, categories and coaching (2026-11)

Quality step (12, 13) of `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`. QA scoring, deductions, bonuses, the false-confirmation penalty and coaching rules are unchanged; proposals stay proposals that need their existing approval. No schema change.

**One page.** `/v2/admin/quality` (h1 "Quality", one main landmark) has one period control (7 days / 30 days / 90 days / Custom), Export, five stat cards, the tabs Review queue N, Issues N, Cleaners (default), Inspectors, Categories, Coaching N, and one shared filter row (search, Property, Category, "Has open fixes", plus removable Cleaner / Raised-by chips from drill-downs). Period, filters, tab, sort and page live in the URL (`components/v2/admin/quality/hub/use-hub-state.ts`). `/v2/admin/quality/issues`, `/quality/accountability` and `/quality/qa-performance` redirect to the Issues, Cleaners and Inspectors tabs keeping every parameter; the QA issue register's `from`/`to` become `period=custom` over the same days (`lib/quality/redirects.ts`). `/v2/admin/quality/[assignmentId]` is unchanged. The sidebar has one "Quality" entry (Reports kept) badged with `qualityHub` = unclaimed inspections + issues awaiting rectification (both existing counts).

**Access.** The page admits ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER and QA_INSPECTOR (live `requireRole`). A QA inspector sees only the Issues tab, read-only (no selection, bulk bar or detail actions), as the old register allowed; the stat, cleaner, inspector, category and queue routes stay ADMIN / OPS_MANAGER. An operations manager whose quality pack is read-only sees no actions.

**One period helper** (`lib/quality/period.ts`) replaces the routes' `periodToDays` copies. Periods are Sydney calendar days, today included ("7 days" = today and the six days before); the previous period is the same number of days immediately before and exists only when the period has a start. Custom ranges are inclusive; unknown keys, impossible days and reversed ranges are refused (400) by the API and fall back to 30 days in the browser. The classic `/api/admin/accountability/overview` and `/qa-performance` routes keep their "anything else reads as 30d" leniency (`lib/quality/legacy.ts`) but now use these Sydney-day boundaries instead of a rolling `now − N×24h` window.

**One filtered query** (`lib/quality/where.ts`): every grouping reads `QaIssue` through `issueWhere` (created in the period; property, category, cleaner, raised-by, open fixes, Issues-tab status filters, and search over description, category key and label, cleaner, property and job number). Groupings (`lib/quality/groupings.ts`) by cleaner, inspector and category and the stat totals are computed from that one set, so their totals agree (tested). Cleans use `cleanWhere`: jobs scheduled in the period (Sydney day) that reached submission (Submitted, QA review, Completed, Invoiced), narrowed by property, cleaner and search only.

**Figures and their basis** (each card's title states it):
- Cleans reviewed = submitted cleans in the period with at least one QA review, "of N cleans".
- Average score = mean authoritative review score (QA > ADMIN > AUTO, then latest; `lib/quality/authority.ts`) of those cleans; "+N on last period" only when the previous period has reviewed cleans, else "No earlier data to compare"; no reviewed cleans → "–" and "Not enough data".
- Issues raised (with critical count), Fixes still open (pending, returned to cleaner, not fixed, escalated; with the count raised more than 48 h ago), Fix cost (sum of recorded `rectificationCost`, with recorded rework hours; "–" / "No fix cost recorded" when none is recorded).
- Review queue count = open inspections (OPEN, ASSIGNED, IN_PROGRESS) + quick-score candidates for the property/search filters; Issues = the filtered total; Coaching = open coaching records (OPEN, ACKNOWLEDGED, ESCALATED).

**Cleaners tab** (`lib/quality/cleaners.ts`). Rows: active cleaners plus anyone with an issue in the filtered set (marked "Not an active cleaner"), hidden when an issue-level filter leaves them no issues. Columns: Cleans, Score, Trend (8 Sydney weeks to the end of the period; a week without a review is a gap, never 0), Issues by severity, Top category, Open fixes, False confirmations, Streak (current streak, last-5 in the hint; "Reset" at 0, "No reviewed cleans" without data, "–" when unreadable), one action. Sorted on the server against an allow-list (nulls last). Streak and last-5/last-10 use `summariseAccountabilityStreak`, now exported from `lib/workforce/performance.ts` and used by `getPerformanceMetrics` itself; the hub reads them with one query per cleaner (`lib/quality/streaks.ts`) instead of the overview's full `getPerformanceMetrics` per cleaner (about a dozen queries each). Row action (`lib/quality/cleaner-signals.ts`): "Start coaching" when the existing recurring-issue pattern rule (`detectPatterns`, settings count and window) flags a category and the cleaner has no open coaching record — it posts to `POST /api/admin/accountability/coaching` with the pattern key and issue ids; otherwise "Review bonus" when the nightly job has a PENDING STREAK_5 / STREAK_10 / MONTHLY_RANK_1/2 proposal — it offers the Approvals centre's approve / decline through `PATCH /api/admin/pay-adjustments/[id]`. The streak cell reads "N clean · bonus due" while a proposal waits. The expanded row lists that cleaner's open issues (hub filters, `openFixes=1`, 5 shown with the real total) with Mark fixed and Propose deduction (open the issue detail on that form) or Reject / Confirm for a suspected false confirmation (existing PATCH), a by-category breakdown and "Open all N in Issues" (Issues tab filtered to the cleaner). Superseded in part by Wave C (2026-11-11): the row also offers "Propose bonus", actions beyond the first sit in a row menu, and severities read Low / Medium / High; see "Quality design completion: manual bonus proposal, both row actions, Low / Medium / High (Wave C, 2026-11-11)".

**Issues tab** (`lib/quality/issue-list.ts`, `GET /api/admin/quality/issues`): `parseListQuery` paging and sort (created, severity, category, rectification, cleaner, property; anything else 400), real total, filter options from the database (`GET /api/admin/quality/options`: cleaners, properties and raisers that occur on issues, configured plus used categories). The row's Issue button opens the detail (moved to `components/v2/admin/quality/issues/`): facts, description, QA and before/after photos, job link and every existing action — mark rectification with minutes and photos, propose deduction (a PENDING `CleanerPayAdjustment`), escalate, confirm / reject a suspected false confirmation; the Confirm label shows `falseConfirmationExtraDeduction` from settings (the old drawer hard-coded −10). Bulk "Mark fixed" (`POST /api/admin/quality/issues/bulk`, ADMIN / OPS_MANAGER, 1–100 ids, status fixed by manager / QA / another cleaner) runs each issue through `rectifyQaIssue` (`lib/quality/rectify.ts`, the transaction moved unchanged out of `PATCH /api/admin/qa/issues/[id]`, which now calls it) and answers done / unchanged / refused per row; issues already fixed another way are refused, not relabelled, because who fixed it decides who is paid. CSV export (`GET /api/admin/quality/issues/export`, same filters; refuses above 5,000 rows rather than truncating). Header Export downloads the same CSV for the current period and filters.

**Inspectors tab** (`lib/quality/inspectors.ts`): the QA performance definitions unchanged (roster = active QA inspectors + raisers in the set; inspections completed in the period with average on-site minutes; issues found by severity; fixed by QA credited to the rectifier with minutes and cost; false-confirmation flags; complaint-rate caution with "Not enough data" without QA-passed jobs), server-sorted, "View issues" drills to the Issues tab by raiser. `/api/admin/accountability/qa-performance` is now a thin caller with its old shape and order.

**Categories tab** (`lib/quality/categories.ts`): issues by category with severity split, open fixes and the cleaners behind it (three named, "+N more"), drilling to the Issues tab. Replaces the category × cleaner matrix.

**Review queue tab**: the quick-score list (whole list for the property/search filters, up to the API's 200 oldest with "Showing the N oldest of M" when capped; Select all, Clear and a sort that re-orders that complete set) and the open inspection queue paged with its real total (`GET /api/admin/quality/inspections`, sorts due / queued / property) with the existing Inspect links and the QA templates link. The period does not apply to live queues. `/api/admin/qa/awaiting` accepts optional `propertyId` and `q` (narrowing only; `quickScoreCandidateWhere` in `lib/qa/auto-score.ts`).

**Coaching tab**: the existing `CoachingWorkspace` embedded as a panel (it has no h1), with its own filters, plus a link to `/v2/admin/workforce/coaching`.

**Laundry team metrics** moved to `/v2/admin/laundry/stats` (section "Laundry team and suppliers", own 7/30/90-day choice) through `GET /api/admin/laundry/team-metrics` and `lib/laundry/team-metrics.ts` (same arithmetic, `lib/accountability/laundry-stats.ts`). The overview route reads the same helper; a failed laundry read omits the block instead of reporting zeros. The hub links to it.

**Kept for other callers.** `GET /api/admin/qa/issues` (classic workspace; same shape, rows now built by `lib/quality/issue-rows.ts`), `/api/admin/accountability/overview` (classic dashboard; shape unchanged), `/api/admin/accountability/qa-performance`, `PATCH /api/admin/qa/issues/[id]` (Approvals centre, classic pages). The v2 accountability, QA performance and QA issues components were removed; the classic `components/admin/*` versions are untouched.

**Deliberately changed.** Period boundaries are Sydney calendar days (was rolling 24-hour windows). "Cleans" is now the submitted-clean basis above (the old overview counted reviews created in the window). The Quality badge adds pending rectifications. Quick score no longer calls `router.refresh()` after approving; the hub re-reads its figures.

### Jobs list: view tabs, visible filters, peek panel and server board (2026-11)

Jobs step (2) of `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`. Job status transitions, assignment rules, pay and charge arithmetic are unchanged. No schema change.

**Page.** `/v2/admin/jobs` (h1 "Jobs") shows "N jobs match · updated …" from the last successful read, then Export, "Import or bulk create" and "New job" (both create actions open the existing `/v2/admin/jobs/new`, which holds single and bulk create). Below: view tabs, the filter chips, the List | Board switch and "Columns", then the table or board, paging, the bulk bar and the peek panel. `components/v2/admin/jobs/jobs-workspace.tsx` only wires the pieces; list data (`use-jobs-list.ts`), filters (`jobs-filter-bar.tsx`, `jobs-status-filter.tsx`), tabs (`jobs-view-tabs.tsx`), view options (`jobs-view-options.tsx`), table (`jobs-table.tsx`), board (`jobs-board.tsx`, `use-jobs-board.ts`), bulk (`jobs-bulk.tsx`, `bulk-remind-dialog.tsx`, `job-status-dialog.tsx`), quick assign (`quick-assign-dialog.tsx`) and peek (`job-peek-panel.tsx`, `peek-sections.tsx`, `use-job-peek.ts`, `use-peek-param.ts`) each have their own file. The old list-row component (`EJobRow`) and the three-dot "Jobs options" menu are gone; `job-row.tsx` keeps the shared helpers and the board card.

**View tabs.** Built-in views are defined once in `lib/jobs/view-presets.ts` as filter presets over the ordinary filter fields: Today, Needs a cleaner (Unassigned or Awaiting confirmation), This week (today and the next six Sydney days), Awaiting QA (Submitted or QA review), Not invoiced (Completed and the Invoice "no" rule: not INVOICED and no line on a non-VOID invoice; since 2026-11-11 a line on a voided invoice no longer counts, see "Existing problems fixed (2026-11-11)"), All. A tab applies exactly its filters and clears the others; sort, density, columns and layout stay. A tab is current only while the filters equal its preset. Counts are real totals from ONE grouped query (`lib/jobs/view-counts.ts`, `COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE …)` per view, generated from the same declarations) returned as `counts` when the list asks with `counts=1`; "All" shows no number, as drawn. Personal saved views (`/api/me/jobs-views`) appear as tabs after the built-ins (no count: they can hold any filter, including search, and are not counted by the grouped query), then "+ Save view", which opens the existing "Save view as" dialog. The saved-views list is loaded once (`SavedViewsPanel` takes the list and selected view from the page; `SavedViewsControls` keeps its self-loading form). Personal and team defaults load as before.

**Filters.** Search, Date (shortcuts Any date, Today, Tomorrow, Next 7 days, From tomorrow, Before today, or a From/To range), Status (Any, Active, or any set of the eleven statuses; stored as `statusChip=UNASSIGNED,OFFERED` in workflow order, old single values and `active` still read), Client (with Property inside; an active property filter also shows as a removable chip), Cleaner (incl. Nobody assigned), Job type and Invoice are always visible chips that name their value; "Clear filters" resets filters and keeps display preferences. "Columns" opens View options (density, the Client / Cleaner / When toggles, Reset appearance, saved views and team default); the section stays mounted while closed.

**Table.** `EDataTable` (gained optional group rows, row click, an active row and cell padding) with headers When, Property, Client, Type, Cleaner, Status, Guest in, Charge. When, Property, Client, Type and Status sort on the server; Cleaner, Guest in and Charge do not (several cleaners per job; the arriving guest is inside the notes JSON; the charge is computed from rate tables), so they are not offered as sortable. Rows are grouped by Sydney day. One visible action: "Assign" (the existing quick assign) when the job is Unassigned and not skipped; everything else is in the row menu: Preview, Open full job, Manage schedule (`?tab=schedule`, the old "Manage"), People and assignment, Client messages, Change status… (the reviewed bulk-status flow for one job; disabled for invoiced jobs and read-only access), Send reminder… (only Paused, In progress, Submitted). Clicking a row or its property name opens the peek panel. Guest in shows the arriving guest's check-in time (Sydney) from `jobMeta.reservationContext` with a "Same day" flag from `Job.sameDayCheckin`, "None" when there is no arriving guest. Charge is `computeClientCharge` (fixed price, property rate, price book) computed on the server; no rate shows "No rate", never $0. Both columns appear only when the server sent them.

**Peek panel.** `ESidePanel` (`components/v2/ui/side-panel.tsx`, reusable): beside the list from `lg`, a full-height modal sheet below it (Tab stays inside); focus moves to its title on open and returns to the opener on Escape or close; labelled "Job preview". The open job is `?open=<jobId>` (survives reload and Back; other parameters untouched). Summary comes from `GET /api/admin/jobs/[id]/peek` (`lib/jobs/peek-summary.ts`): client, departing guest from `Job.reservation` (name, phone as a tel link), arriving guest, laundry, charge, people and QA. People reuses that read; Money (`/pay-summary`) and Activity (`/timeline`) load only when their tab is first chosen. Changing the job aborts every read for the previous one and responses are checked against the current job after parsing. Suggested cleaners (`GET /api/admin/jobs/[id]/cleaner-suggestions`, only while nobody is on the job) rank with `lib/jobs/cleaner-suggestions.ts` (pure, tested) from existing data only: saved availability (`lib/accounts/availability.ts`; a day outside a saved roster or a job outside the saved hours is "Unavailable" with the reason), clashes with the cleaner's other active jobs that day with known times ("Clash", with the property and end time), and completed or invoiced cleans at this property. Unknown times are stated, never invented. Travel distance and QA scores are not used (no distance service; no batch QA-score function). Assign uses the job's existing assign route. Reschedule and Message client open the job's Schedule and Messages tabs; Cancel job opens the Schedule tab's "Skip this clean" (the app has no cancelled status), with its own confirmation.

**Bulk bar.** Assign cleaner (existing bulk-assign), Change status (existing reviewed preview, all or nothing), Send reminder, Assign QA (existing `/api/admin/qa/assignments`), Export selected, Clear. Reschedule is not offered: there is no bulk-safe reschedule service. Reminders go through ONE request, `POST /api/admin/jobs/bulk-remind` (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER; 1–200 jobs), which runs each job through `lib/jobs/job-reminder.ts` — the single remind route's logic moved unchanged (same remindable states, transports and `JOB_REMINDER_SENT` audit; the single route keeps its 404 / 409 `notRemindable` / 400 answers) — and answers requested / partly sent / not sent / skipped (with the reason) / not found per job. A push is reported as "requested" and an email as "accepted by the provider", each with "delivery is not confirmed". Jobs not reminded stay selected. Export selected uses the reviewed CSV export (5,000 cap, preview) limited to the selection through `ids=`; the header Export still exports the filters.

**Board.** `GET /api/admin/jobs/board` (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER) reads the same filters and sort as the list: one `groupBy` gives real lane totals over every matching job; each non-empty lane carries its first 25 rows ("Showing 25 of N" when more). Lanes (`lib/jobs/board-lanes.ts`): Unassigned, Scheduled, In progress, Done. Dragging a card to another lane opens the reviewed status change for that job limited to the lane's statuses; nothing changes until the review is applied. Invoiced cards cannot be dragged; keyboards use the card menu's Change status….

**API.** The paginated `GET /api/jobs` reads sort through `parseListQuery`: `sort` ∈ when, property, client, type, status, created (plus the legacy soonest/latest), `dir` asc/desc; anything else is refused with 400. `status` is a comma list of real statuses (unknown → 400); `ids` limits to selected jobs (≤ 500); `counts=1` adds the view totals; `extras=1` adds `guestIn` and `charge` per row. The where builder, include and ordering moved to `lib/jobs/list-filters.ts`, `list-include.ts` and `list-sort.ts` unchanged in meaning (column orders end on `id` for stable paging). The non-paginated array response, its lenient sort and raw single status, the cleaner scope and `redactJobForCleaner` are unchanged; a cleaner never receives counts, extras or the paginated shape.

**Job detail.** The Quality tab's two blocks are merged into one (QA inspection, photo review, QA score and quick review, in that order). Nothing else on the detail page changed.

**Deliberately changed.** Row "Manage" and "Open" buttons removed (menu items instead; row click previews). Sorting moved from the "Sort jobs" select to the headers (the URL keeps `sort`, with `property_desc` etc. for descending). Filters are visible chips, not a collapsible "Filters" disclosure (the stored disclosure preference is no longer read). The board reads its own server lanes instead of grouping the list's current 50 rows. Bulk reminders are one server request instead of a browser loop. A selection now survives refreshes while its rows stay visible and is cleared when the filters change.

### Calendar: resource week, visible range only, checked moves (2026-11)

Calendar step (3) of `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`. Scheduling, assignment, status-transition and pay rules are unchanged; no schema change. The page keeps its roles (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER, through `requireJobsViewsContext`) and the Calendar operations permission (`/api/admin/calendar*` map to `calendar`); a read-only impersonation sees the calendar but cannot move or assign.

**Page.** `/v2/admin/calendar` (h1 "Calendar") with "Week of 9 November · N jobs · N without a cleaner" (counts of the loaded, filtered range; "without a cleaner" = not skipped, not started or finished, nobody assigned), Previous / Today / Next, Day | Week | Month | Agenda and a quiet Refresh. Chip row: Rows: cleaners | Rows: properties, Client, Status (groups Offered / Planned / On site / Done, or one of the eleven statuses, with counts, plus "Only jobs without a cleaner"), Show laundry runs, Show guest arrivals, and the drag hint. State is in the URL (`view`, `date`, `rows`, `clientId`, `status`, `unassigned`, `laundry`, `arrivals`, `open`); rows and the three toggles are also saved per view scope in local storage, because a full refresh resets view parameters app-wide (view memory). Week is the default at ≥1024px, Day below. `calendar-workspace.tsx` wires the parts: `use-calendar-state`, `use-calendar-data`, `use-calendar-move`, `use-chip-drag`, `calendar-toolbar`, `calendar-week-grid`, `calendar-day-view`, `calendar-day-list` (phones), `calendar-month-grid`, `calendar-agenda`, `calendar-job-chip`, `calendar-move-dialog`, `calendar-move-confirm`, `calendar-assign-dialogs`, `calendar-states`.

**Views.** Week (wide): resource grid, first column the row, seven days, today highlighted; cleaners mode starts with "Unassigned (N jobs)", then every active cleaner plus any cleaner (active or not) with work in range, each with "N h this week"; properties mode lists properties with a job in the filtered range. Hours (`lib/calendar/presentation.ts`): each job's estimated hours, else its start-to-finish (end or due) window; skipped cleans not counted; a job counts in full for each cleaner on it; jobs with neither basis are shown as "N without hours"; hours cover the whole workload in range, not only the filtered jobs.
