Days with no hours in a cleaner's saved availability show "Not rostered" (`dayAvailabilityBlocker`, the suggestions' wording); there is no leave record, so "Day off"/"Leave" are not shown. "Laundry runs" row: pickups (excluding no-pickup-required and skipped pickups) and drops per day by stored calendar date, linking to `/v2/admin/laundry?date=` (the Laundry page now opens Active runs for that one day). Chips: time, property, "Guest in HH:mm" when shown and a guest checks in that day (arriving stay from `jobMeta.reservationContext`; the stored same-day flag only when there is no context and the job was not moved by hand), and status in words, icon and border as well as colour (done ✓, on site, late, offered, planned, no cleaner dashed, skipped). Late = someone assigned, not started, and the Sydney start time passed (or the day is past). Phones: Week and Day are a day-by-day list grouped by row. Day (wide): the same rows across hours; jobs without a start time in a "No time" column; a job with a start but no end is drawn one hour wide and says so. Month: the previous grid, "+N more" opens that day in Day view, laundry entries kept; phones get the agenda list. Agenda: a fortnight from the cursor day, stated in the header; rows keep Assign/Reassign and QA.

**Data.** `GET /api/admin/calendar?start&end&rows&status&clientId&unassigned&laundry` (`lib/calendar/load.ts`): only the asked days, at most 45 (400 beyond, reversed, malformed, unknown rows or status); `scheduledDate` read as UTC midnight of the calendar day, never shifted; drafts excluded; a slim job shape (no notes); rows with hours and roster gaps; laundry counts and items when asked; summary with status counts; `truncated` above 3,000 jobs. The page no longer calls `/api/jobs` (unchanged). Reads abort when the range or filters change, clear the old data, refresh every 60 s while visible and on focus/visibility, never overlap, check the query after parsing, show an error (not an empty calendar) on a failed first read and a stale notice on a failed refresh.

**Moves.** Drag (mouse or pen) to another day and/or cleaner row, or from Unassigned onto a cleaner; "Move or reassign…" in every chip menu does the same from the keyboard or touch. Every move: `POST /api/admin/calendar/check-move` (read-only, `lib/calendar/check-move.ts`) → confirmation ("Move Bondi Studio to Fri 13 Nov, Aiko T.?" with clashes, roster gaps, booking and notice lines) → the server's plan: `POST /api/admin/phase4/reschedule/:id/apply` (the Schedule tab's endpoint, with `expectedUpdatedAt` and reason "Moved on the calendar") and/or `POST /api/admin/jobs/:id/assign` (the dragged-from cleaner replaced; primary moves with them or to the new cleaner on an empty job). Refusals: the calendar move rule (`lib/calendar/move-rules.ts`: on the way, started, submitted, QA review, completed, invoiced, skipped, draft — the services themselves do not refuse by status and are unchanged; the job's Schedule tab remains for corrections), the reschedule service's start/finish check (`rescheduleWindowError`, now exported from `lib/phase4/analytics.ts`), and the assign route's refusals (inactive or non-cleaner account, draft, skipped). No optimistic success: the chip stays, frozen and marked "Move pending…", until the services answer; a background refresh never redraws it (`lib/calendar/merge.ts`); refusals and failures stay on screen; a date change that succeeded before a failed cleaner change is reported as such. A primary-cleaner replacement offers the existing unsent client note (`reassign-note-dialog.tsx`, now shared with `AssignCleanersModal`). Reservation-driven turnovers: the reschedule sets `manuallyRescheduledAt`, so the iCal sync keeps the moved date, does not move the job back and does not create a second job (jobs are matched by reservation); it stops updating that job's date and times, which the confirmation says.

**Deliberately changed.** Title "Schedule" → "Calendar"; chips open the peek panel instead of the job page ("Open full job" is in the menu); "+N more" opens the day; the agenda is a fortnight from the cursor day (was the cursor month from today, under a "fortnight ahead" description); drafts are no longer shown; the old "Laundry overlay" is the laundry-runs row; the per-status chip row became the Status filter.

### Jobs and Calendar: design completion, Wave A (2026-11)

Wave A of the owner's sixth decision set (`docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`, "close every difference from the designs"). No schema change; no scheduling, assignment, status or charge rule changed; everything writes through the existing services. Not yet checked in a browser or a production build.

**Sortable Cleaner, Guest in and Charge** (`lib/jobs/list-computed-sort.ts`, `list-computed-order.ts`; `readJobsListOrder` in `list-sort.ts`). The paginated list and the board accept `sort=cleaner|guestIn|charge` (URL values `cleaner`, `cleaner_desc`, … in `JOBS_SORT_VALUES`); the legacy array contract still refuses them. Each orders the WHOLE filtered set before the page is cut. Cleaner: one SQL query over the filtered ids, by the primary (else earliest-assigned) active cleaner's name (name, else email, lower-cased); unassigned jobs first in both directions. Guest in: the arriving check-in from `jobMeta.reservationContext.checkinAtLocal` (ISO string inside the TEXT notes column, so it is parsed with the column's own `guestInForJob` rather than a SQL JSON cast that one malformed row would fail), else the arriving stay's Sydney day; jobs with neither last. Charge: `computeClientCharge` over the stored rates (`loadChargeInputs` / `chargeForJob`, now shared with the column); jobs with no rate last and still show "No rate". Guest in and Charge read the filtered set in one bounded pass and refuse more than 5,000 jobs with 400 `SORT_SET_TOO_LARGE` and a sentence asking to narrow the filters (shown in place of the list); they never sort only the current page. Ties: scheduled day, then id.

**Saved-view tab counts.** `POST /api/admin/jobs/view-counts` (`lib/jobs/view-saved-counts.ts`): up to 20 views, each sent as the exact list query its tab applies (`buildJobsQuery`), counted with the list's own `readJobsFilterParams` → `buildJobsWhere` (search semantics included). A stored filter that no longer parses returns `count: null` with the reason (screen-reader text), never 0. Re-read whenever the list reloads; a failed read shows no numbers rather than stale ones. POST because the body carries the queries, so an operations manager with Jobs "view" gets no saved-view numbers (fails closed).

**Suggested cleaners: travel and QA** (`cleaner-suggestions-signals.ts`, `cleaner-suggestions-qa.ts`). Travel is a straight-line (haversine) distance to the job's property, only when both ends are stored: from the cleaner's previous job that day (latest other job starting before this one, with property coordinates), else their saved home coordinates (`User.latitude/longitude`). The reason names the basis: "10.6 km straight-line from Manly Loft (previous job)" / "… from home". No drive time is shown (the Maps ETA provider is not called per candidate). Live pings are not used (a future job does not start from where the phone is now). QA is the Quality hub's own Cleaners figure: average authoritative score over its default 30-day Sydney period from one `loadCleanFacts` read for every candidate; "QA –" below 3 reviewed cleans, with the count. Ranking (pure, tested): assignable first; cleans here; fewer jobs that day; shorter known distance; higher shown QA; name. The calendar's move check calls the ranking without travel/QA and is unchanged.

**Bulk Reschedule.** Bulk bar → dialog (new date, optional shift: 15 min to 2 h earlier/later) → `POST /api/admin/jobs/bulk-reschedule` `mode: "preview"`: each job through `checkCalendarMove` (the Calendar's check) → per-row "Can move" / "Not movable" with blockers, clashes, roster and booking lines → confirm → `mode: "apply"`: each movable row re-checked, then `applyReschedule` with the preview's `updatedAt` as the stale-write guard and reason "Rescheduled in bulk from Jobs", one at a time, a result per row (moved / not moved / failed). Unmoved jobs stay selected. At most 100 jobs. Operations managers also need Calendar "manage" (single reschedule and the move check are Calendar routes), so access is not widened.

**Calendar move check with a time** (`lib/calendar/time-drop.ts`). `check-move` accepts `toStartTime` (Day view) or `shiftMinutes` (bulk). `plannedTimeChange` keeps the job's length (due time moves by the same amount) and refuses: a job with a separate `endTime` (the reschedule service writes only `startTime`/`dueTime`, so the end would be left behind — change it on the Schedule tab), no start time to shift, a start that leaves the day, a finish after midnight. Clashes and roster are checked against the new window; the plan passes `startTime`/`dueTime` to the same reschedule endpoint only when the time changes. The response carries `time` (before → after) for the confirmation.

**Import or bulk create.** The Jobs header button opens its own dialog: "Open bulk create" deep-links to the existing New job page section (`/v2/admin/jobs/new#bulk-create`), and a CSV import (`lib/jobs/csv-import.ts`, `csv-import-server.ts`, `POST /api/admin/jobs/import`): template download; columns property (exact name or id), date, start, due, job_type, cleaner (exact email or name), notes; at most 200 rows / 200 KB; preview validates every row server-side (unknown, ambiguous or inactive property, bad date or time, due before start, unknown job type, unknown cleaner — each refused with the reason) and creates nothing. Apply sends the file again with its SHA-256 key; each valid row is created by the job-create service (`lib/jobs/admin-create.ts`, extracted unchanged from `POST /api/admin/jobs`, which now calls it) through `createAdminJobOnce`: job + `AuditLog` receipt (`JOB_CSV_IMPORT_ROW`, entity `JobImport`, id `<fileKey>:<row>`) in one transaction under an advisory lock, so a retry or a re-upload of the same file reports "Already created" instead of creating twice. Follow-ups are the create route's own (carry-forward tasks, preferred cleaner, the client's booking confirmation when switched on). A named cleaner is then assigned through the existing assign route for newly created rows only, with the outcome per row. Same roles as creating a job.

**Smaller design items.** Day group rows read "Thursday 12 November" (`lib/jobs/list-day-label.ts`, Sydney); today reads "… · today" in the primary colour like the Calendar's day header (no Today/Tomorrow prefix). The Jobs status pill draws Unassigned in the danger tone (`JOBS_PILL_TONE_OVERRIDES` in `job-row.tsx`; list, board and peek only — the shared `JOB_STATUS_TONES` used by other portals is unchanged); the written label and the status icon stay, so it is not colour-only.

**Calendar touch drag and time drop.** `lib/calendar/touch-drag.ts`: mouse/pen drag after 6 px as before; touch only after press-and-hold `TOUCH_HOLD_MS` (450 ms) within `TOUCH_SLOP_PX` (10 px) — moving earlier abandons the gesture so the page scrolls; a live touch drag suppresses page scrolling (non-passive `touchmove`) and the long-press menu. Draggable chips show a grip; the floating label sits above the finger. Same check → confirm → save path; "Move or reassign…" stays for keyboard and menu users. Day view: the hour track carries its axis (`data-drop-axis-start`, `data-drop-px-per-hour`); a drop proposes the start under the pointer less where the chip was grabbed, snapped to `TIME_DROP_STEP_MINUTES` (15), shown while dragging ("Moving Bondi Studio to 10:45"), then the server check and confirmation ("Move Bondi Studio to Wed 11 Nov at 10:45?").

**Calendar view and date survive a refresh.** Documented exception to the view-memory reset (`docs/style/operational-ui.md`, "Returning to a workspace"): the view is saved per view scope in local storage (`sneek:calendar-view:v1:`) and restored always; the date is saved in session storage (`sneek:calendar-date:v1:`) with the Sydney day it was saved on and restored only in the same browser session on that day. A restored view/date is written back to the URL. "Today" clears the saved date; an explicit URL always wins.

### Admin job page: "Job full view" redesign (2026-11-12)

Owner-approved boards "Job full view" and "Job page in four states". The eleven-tab page at `/v2/admin/jobs/[id]` is now one page: header, eight-step stage bar, key facts, a lead block for the status, blockers, the job as a timeline with five tabs for heavy content, and a right rail. No schema change, no new route, no new permission: the page keeps `requireRole([ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER])`, and an operations manager's packs (read through `getOpsAccess`, a corrupt policy failing closed) decide view-only and which linked workspaces appear.

**Files.** Page `app/v2/admin/jobs/[id]/page.tsx` (composes only). Pure, tested rules in `lib/jobs/job-page/`: `primary-action.ts` (status → primary action table, secondary buttons, reminder eligibility, title badge), `more-menu.ts` (menu entries, groups and disabled reasons; cancel reasons mirror `lib/jobs/cancel-rules.ts`), `deep-link.ts` (`?view=` and old `?tab=` → view, anchor, what opens), `stage-bar.ts`, `timeline.ts`, `money.ts`, `facts.ts`, `blockers.ts`, `briefing.ts` (start-briefing proof, moved). Server reads `load.ts` (the old select plus cancel record, invoice id, property radius and access text, quote, lost items, urgent needs, latest client message), `rows.ts` (payloads for the existing components, unchanged), `view-model.ts`. Components in `components/v2/admin/jobs/detail/`.

**Header.** Breadcrumb, one h1 with the status badge (board words and tones), sub line, actions. Primary action by status: Unassigned/Offered → Assign cleaner (to Dispatch, assign panel open, ranked suggestions in the lead); Assigned/En route/In progress/Paused → Message team (office compose; the job chat is client↔office only by policy); Waiting approval → Review continuation request; Submitted → Review and approve; QA review → Open quality check; Completed/Invoiced → Open invoice (disabled "Not invoiced yet" with no live line); Cancelled → Restore job (disabled with `restoreRefusal` when it started before cancelling). Secondary buttons per state (Message, Send back for rework, Send reminder disabled with its reason unless paused or running 24 h+, Share report, Message client). "More" menu (`ERowMenu`, now with group headings, badges, a footnote, an outline trigger and an open signal): Job — Edit job details, Reschedule or change times, Change status… (status, hours and per-cleaner pay), Skip / Review skip request / Restore skipped clean, Send reminder to cleaners ("Paused or 24 h+"), Save job as template, Download report, Open in Live ops, Edit clock and GPS (when clocked); Danger zone — Reset job…, Cancel job… (disabled "Invoiced jobs cannot be cancelled", or "Only an administrator can cancel a job that has started"), Delete job…. Cancelled jobs show only Download report and Delete. A view-only viewer sees every change disabled with the reason. Each item opens the existing editor or dialog (`JobManagePanel` sections in a modal, `JobReminderButton` now controllable, `JobResetDialog`, `CancelJobDialog`, delete with PIN/password).

**Timeline** (`job-timeline.tsx`, stage per file): 1 Booking (arriving then departing guest, departing booking details, client notes, schedule, skip decision), 2 Scope (extras, admin tasks, notices, tags, internal note; Add extra, Edit scope and notes), 3 Dispatch (cleaners and responses, Change cleaners = assign panel with SMS/email resend, briefing read with Show proof), 4 On site (clock records, clock-in distance against the property radius, continuation requests, time over allocation), 5 Submitted (Approve and complete / Send back for rework = the quick QA score review, checklist count and rework flags, inventory used, laundry outcome with Correct, photo strip, AI photo review, task requests, draft evidence review, task evidence with "Task photos in report"), 6 Quality check (QA result, inspector assignment, quick QA review), 7 Report (report actions, forms and QA centre with damage reports), 8 Laundry (planned against actual, weight/cost, evidence), 9 Invoice and pay (lines with status and paid date, billing and pay ledger, holiday-rate review for administrators). States: now (gold, from status), done (only with a record), todo (dashed). Finished jobs collapse done stages behind "Show details". Secondary tools are lazy disclosures (`job-disclosure.tsx`: mount on first open, keep drafts when collapsed, open from the URL hash). Tabs: Form answers, Photos (submission, task evidence, laundry), Clock and GPS (editors and map), Messages (`#chat`), History (latest 50 audit rows of the real total).

**Rail.** People (cleaners with phone and response, client contact, QA inspector), Property access (address, lockbox and parking text, key pickup, NFC tag; codes are not shown here — encrypted, kept on the property's access guide), Money, Linked (quote, cases, lost items by `LostFoundItem.jobId`, urgent needs by `UrgentStockNeed.sourceJobId`, invoice; Open case and Log lost item open the existing create dialogs), latest client message.

**Money is honest** (`money.ts`): charge from the canonical `computeClientCharge` (fixed price, property rate, price book) or "No rate"; on an issued invoice the charge is the invoice line total, marked Locked; pay "Not enough data" when nobody is assigned or any cleaner lacks a rate or hours, else an Estimate; margin "Not enough data" unless both are known.

**Deep links.** `?view=timeline|answers|photos|clock|messages|history`. Old `?tab=`: overview → top; schedule → Booking and opens Reschedule; people → Dispatch; quality → Quality check; laundry → Laundry; money → opens Billing and pay ledger; forms → Submitted (and `#draft-evidence-review` still lands); scope → Scope; messages → Messages; activity → History; danger → opens the More menu; `#chat` → Messages tab (client-side).

**Fixed on the way.** Pay and margin counted a cleaner with no rate or hours as $0; Delete refreshed the deleted job's page (404) instead of returning to the board; the full property record including encrypted access and alarm codes was serialised to the browser for form conditions (secret columns now stripped); history and briefing times used a hard-coded timezone (now the organisation's); guest check-in times were formatted in the server's timezone; an empty "Send back for rework" score could be read as 0 (now refused).

### Insights: business, property, client, cleaner and time figures; client report PDFs (2026-11)

Insights step (14) of `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`. Read-only analytics: no business rule, money rule or schema changed. The only writes are the PDFs tab's existing report actions and their new bulk endpoint.

**Page.** `/v2/admin/reports` (route unchanged; sidebar label "Reports" → "Insights"), h1 "Insights", subtitle "… October 2026 compared with September 2026". Header: period chips (This month, Last month, Quarter, Custom), a "Client: all" filter and Export (CSV of the current tab). Tabs: Business (default), Properties, Clients, Cleaners, Time and cost, Client report PDFs. Everything is in the URL (`tab`, `period`/`from`/`to`, `clientId`, `sort`, `dir`, `page`); each tab loads its own data with a cancellable read, so one failed tab never blanks the page. Components: `components/v2/admin/insights/*`; services: `lib/insights/*`; API: `GET /api/admin/insights/{business,properties,clients,cleaners,time,export,options}`.

**Period** (`lib/insights/period.ts`, built on `lib/quality/period.ts`'s Sydney-day ranges): This month = 1st → today, compared with the same days of last month (clamped to its end). Last month (default) = the whole previous calendar month, compared with the month before. Quarter = quarter to date, compared with the same number of days from the start of the previous quarter. Custom = inclusive days, at most 366, compared with the equal-length period immediately before (Quality's `previousRange`). Malformed or reversed days and unknown keys are refused (400), never replaced.

**Clean set.** The Quality hub's (`cleanWhere`): jobs scheduled in the period (Sydney day) in Submitted, QA review, Completed or Invoiced; the client filter narrows to that client's properties. One bounded read per period (refused above 20,000 cleans rather than half-counted); issue counts are `groupBy` in the database.

**Definitions** (also stated on screen under each figure/table):
- *Revenue* — the SCHEDULED CHARGE per clean from `computeClientCharge` (fixed job price → property rate → price book), the rule invoice generation uses. Not invoiced or received money; used consistently on every tab. Excluded: no client rate; skipped cleans (not billed).
- *Cleaner cost* — recorded pay per clean from `computeJobPaySummary` (assignment rate snapshot × allocated hours split across cleaners, or clocked hours when none are allocated, or the custom / rework payout; plus transport and APPROVED adjustments; pending ignored), summed over every payee — the figure the cleaner invoice and payroll produce. Excluded: no cleaner assigned; any assigned payee without a pay rate (the pay function's default-rate fallback is NOT counted as known); no allocated hours and no clocked log.
- *Gross margin* — (revenue − cleaner cost) ÷ revenue, only over cleans where both are known; none when that revenue is not positive.
- *Cleans done* — count of the clean set (a true count; zero is zero).
- *On time* — first clock-in no later than 15 minutes after the scheduled start (Sydney day at `startTime`, Sydney time; the workforce reliability rule's 15 minutes). Excluded: no start time; no time log.
- *Planned / actual hours* — `estimatedHours` against finished time logs, over cleans with both; ratio = actual ÷ planned. Excluded: no allocated time; no log; a log still running; an assigned cleaner without a log. *Overrun* = more than 5 minutes over. Per cleaner: their share of allocated time (split as pay splits it) against their own logs.
- *QA score* — the Quality hub's clean totals (authoritative review QA > ADMIN > AUTO, then latest), so both pages agree.
- *Issues* — QaIssue rows raised in the period (Quality's issue period rule); per cleaner equals the Quality hub Cleaners tab for the same period when no client filter is set.
- *Rework rate* — rework jobs ÷ cleans; for a cleaner, rework jobs redoing one of their cleans ÷ their cleans.
- *Days to pay* — Sydney days from `sentAt` to the payment (`paidDate`, else `paidAt`) for INVOICE rows marked PAID in the period. Excluded: no sent date.
- *Overdue now* — owing (`invoiceAmountOwing`, credit notes applied, fully credited not counted) on invoices the client-invoice list calls overdue (`overdueWhere`, default payment terms). A current figure, not a period one. There are no per-client terms.

**Missing data is never zero.** Every figure is `{ value, basisCount, excludedCount, excluded, enough, reason }` (`lib/insights/measure.ts`); the screen shows e.g. "5 of 7 cleans; 2 excluded: no time log (2)". A page figure needs at least 5 supporting records, a row figure 3 (days to pay per client: 1 paid invoice, basis shown); below that the value is withheld ("Not enough data") and no change is shown. A change appears only when both periods have enough data (percent for revenue, points for rates, count for cleans).

**Worth knowing** (`lib/insights/findings.ts`, rule-based, named thresholds): property ≥ 20 minutes over allocated time on average over ≥ 3 cleans; an issue category at least doubled with ≥ 5 issues (needs a previous period); a client paying ≥ 7 days beyond terms on ≥ 2 paid invoices with an overdue balance; a property margin ≤ 15% over ≥ 3 cleans. Each fires at its threshold, carries a templated sentence with real numbers, its basis and a link (jobs list `propertyId`/`dateFrom`/`dateTo`; Quality `tab=issues&category&period=custom`; invoices `tab=invoices&client&status=overdue`). The strongest per detector, then the top three; none → "Nothing notable this period".

**Business tab.** Findings strip; six cards (Revenue, Gross margin, Cleans done, On time, Actual vs planned hours, Rework rate) with change and basis; "Revenue and cleaner cost by week" (Monday Sydney weeks, only cleans with both sides, two series differing by fill and pattern, text legend, per-bar values for screen readers, "Show as a table"); "Properties, least profitable first" (five rows; sorting opens the Properties tab in that order; "All N properties").

**Properties / Clients / Cleaners.** Server-sorted, server-paged tables (`parseListQuery`; unknown sorts 400). Rows link to the jobs list, the Quality hub and client invoices with the parameters those pages read. Clients adds cleaner cost, average days to pay and overdue now. **Time and cost**: by job type and by property (most over planned first, 25 rows) with overrun counts; cleaner cost by week.

**Access** (`lib/insights/access.ts`). ADMIN: everything. OPS_MANAGER: the page and `/api/admin/insights/*` follow the `reports` feature (`insights` added to the ops catalog's admin sections); revenue, cost, margin, days to pay and overdue need `finance` at read or above and are left out of the API response (and CSV) otherwise — the sorts on them are refused. Other roles: refused (the page's guard is unchanged: ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER).

**Client report PDFs tab.** The existing manager (`components/v2/admin/reports/reports-manager.tsx`, split into row, theme picker, bulk and filter-hook files) unchanged in function, plus: the laundry visibility toggle (the API already accepted `laundryVisible`); row selection with bulk "show/hide to client, cleaner or laundry", "Regenerate" (current theme, ≤ 20) and "Delete" (shared `EConfirmModal`) through `POST /api/admin/reports/bulk` with one outcome per row (failed rows stay selected); the single delete confirmation now uses `EConfirmModal`; write controls disabled for a read-only reports pack. Single and bulk actions share `lib/reports/admin-report-actions.ts`. Its filters (`q`, `propertyId`, `visibility`, `sort`, `page`) now live in the URL instead of session storage. Old links: without `tab`, a link with `q`, `propertyId`, `visibility`, `reportId` or a manager sort (newest/service-date/oldest) opens this tab with its filters. `/v2/admin/reports/themes` is unchanged; its back link opens this tab.

**Not built.** "Email monthly": a recurring email needs a schedule, a stored recipient and a settings destination that do not exist; nothing was added and nothing is sent.

### Accounts: one table for every login, person panel edited in place, editable pay rate (2026-11)

> Superseded in layout by "People: clients and cleaners in one directory" (2026-11-12): the Accounts route is now the People directory; the login list, person panel and their rules below are unchanged and reused there. The Clients tab shows Outstanding receivables again, as drawn on the approved People board.

Accounts step (11) of `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`. No schema change. No change to role/permission, impersonation, 2FA/PIN rules or pay arithmetic.

**Page.** `/v2/admin/accounts` (`components/v2/admin/accounts/hub/`): h1 "Accounts", summary line from the list API ("N people · N invites not accepted · N documents expiring", plus "N expired" when any), Export (CSV of the current tab, filters and sort, every page) and "Invite person" (ADMIN; the old create dialog unchanged: invitation or set password, role, linked client, ABN for client/laundry, bank details for cleaner/laundry). Role tabs with server counts: Everyone, Cleaners, Office (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER, QA_INSPECTOR), Client logins, Assistants (VA), Partners (LAUNDRY, MAINTENANCE), Needs attention (`lib/accounts/list/tabs.ts`). Old `?tab=team|staff|cleaners|clients|assistants|vas|partners` map onto the new tabs. Filters: search (name, email, phone digits), Active / Invited / Disabled, Documents (missing, expired or expiring), Role (on tabs that span roles), Columns (saved per browser). The receivables KPI left this page (it lives in Finance); staff/cleaner/client counts are the tab counts.

**List.** `GET /api/admin/users?paginated=1` (`lib/accounts/list/*`) answers `ListResponse` plus `counts`, `summary` and `columns`; every other caller of `GET /api/admin/users` keeps the plain array, unchanged. Sort allow-list: name, role, status, rate, jobs30, qa30, documents, lastSeen; an unknown sort, or a sort on a column the viewer cannot see, is 400. Derived columns are sorted over the whole filtered set on the server; missing values sort last. Columns: Name (initials, email), Role (+ extra roles), Status (Active, "Invited N d ago", Disabled), Rate, Jobs 30 d, QA 30 d, Documents, Last seen. Jobs and QA use the Quality hub's clean basis (`loadCleanFacts` + `cleanTotalsByCleaner`, 30 Sydney days), so they equal the hub's Cleaners tab; a cleaner with no clean is 0, anyone else "–"; no review is "–". Last seen is `User.lastSeenAt`, which only the field apps' location pings write; office logins show "–". Status: Disabled = not active; Invited = active with an unaccepted `UserInvitation`.

**Needs attention** (`lib/accounts/list/attention.ts`, named constants): invite not accepted more than `INVITE_PENDING_ATTENTION_DAYS` (7) days after it was first sent; documents missing (a `StaffDocumentRequest` still REQUESTED), expired, or expiring (a staff document within `DOCUMENT_EXPIRY_WARNING_DAYS` = 14, the workforce sweep's window; visa 60 / licence 30 / rego 30 from `credential-expiry`); disabled but still assigned to an open job scheduled today or later; active with an unverified email.

**Who sees what** (`lib/accounts/viewer.ts`). Page and list: ADMIN and OPS_MANAGER, as before. Rate column: both (unchanged). Jobs/QA columns: ADMIN, or an OPS_MANAGER whose quality pack allows reading. Writes: ADMIN for everything except the office note and resend invite, which OPS_MANAGER keeps (subject to the accounts pack). Controls a viewer cannot use are not shown. An impersonated session carries the target's roles.

**Person panel** (`?open=<userId>`, `components/v2/admin/accounts/person/`), a column beside the table from lg and a sheet below it; `GET /api/admin/users/[id]` (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER; never selects password hash, 2FA secret, invitation token or admin PIN). Header: initials, name, "Role · since Mon YYYY", Test as (ADMIN; opens `/v2/admin/system/test-as?user=<id>`, which shows that person under the console's existing real-session, ADMIN and unlock guards) and a more menu: Open full page, Resend invite, Reset password and Reset 2FA (PIN or password, verified by the routes), Disable / Activate, Delete (type DELETE plus PIN or password). Tabs Profile | Work | Pay | Documents | Activity; each section edits in place (Edit → Save/Cancel, fields frozen while saving, a failed save keeps the draft and shows the server's message, a save that finishes after switching person is ignored). Sections: Contact (name, email with verified state, phone; suburb read-only), Access (role, linked client, sign-in state incl. 2FA, status, profile-edit override switches; extra roles through the existing roles panel), Pay, Office notes (private `adminNotes`; the person's own note read-only), Work (KPIs, recent jobs, Full performance link), Pay & time and pay adjustments, Payroll & identity (extended profile fields; the payroll bank line from the User row and the extended-profile bank line are shown separately), Documents & compliance (credentials, files, requested-not-uploaded, upload through the workforce document flow for viewers that endpoint accepts), Recognition, VA delegated access (read-only), Activity (organisation timezone). Client logins now have a panel and a page.

**Full page** `/v2/admin/accounts/users/[id]` renders the same sections in two columns (kept, not redirected, so nothing is lost); CLIENT users no longer 404.

**Pay rate** `PATCH /api/admin/users/[id]/pay` `{ hourlyRate }` (`lib/accounts/pay-rate.ts`): ADMIN only (OPS_MANAGER 403), $1–$500, two decimals, client-side accounts refused, audited `UPDATE_USER_PAY_RATE` with old and new value in the same transaction; writes only `User.hourlyRate`. What reads it: `computeCleanerPay` uses assignment `payRate` snapshot → per-cleaner job-type rate → `User.hourlyRate`; QA pay uses the inspection's rate → `User.hourlyRate` → default. Frozen and untouched: payroll payouts and run totals, submitted cleaner invoices (`totalAmount`, `lineData`), settled QA and shopping pay. Job pay is not stored per job, so screens that recompute a past job without a snapshot (job pay summary, finance and insights estimates) show the new rate; the paid amount does not change. No "Applies from" date: there is no effective-date table; the new rate applies from saving.

**Bulk** `POST /api/admin/users/bulk` `{ action, ids ≤ 100, hourlyRate? }` (`lib/accounts/bulk.ts`): resend-invite (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER), disable and change-rate (ADMIN), each person through the single-person service (`resend-invite.ts`, `account-status.ts`, `pay-rate.ts`) with a per-person outcome (done / refused / no change, with reason). Message is not offered: no staff messaging path takes a list of accounts through the notification intent pipeline.

**Last active admin** (`lib/accounts/account-status.ts`). Disabling or demoting the last active ADMIN is refused, in the write's transaction under one advisory lock, for the account PATCH and bulk disable (two admins disabling each other at once could otherwise leave none). Existing guards kept: not yourself, sessions dropped on disable.

**Assistants tab** keeps the VA directory and the client-scoped VA team manager as they were. The shared account menu used there (`account-actions-menu.tsx`) now sends the PIN or password with Reset password, Reset 2FA and Delete; before, those calls omitted it and the routes always refused them (423), and "Reset password" asked for a new password the route ignores (it issues a temporary one).

**Removed files** (functions moved, nothing lost): `staff-manager.tsx`, `accounts-tab-nav.tsx` (v2), `account-notes.tsx`, `account-activity.tsx`, `extended-profile-editor.tsx`. Birthdays stay on Everyone and Office in a disclosure under the table.

### Command and Live ops: work queue, filtered counters, one live feed (2026-11)

**Command (`/v2/admin`).** h1 is today's date in the organisation timezone; the summary line counts today's published jobs, cleaners on shift (Live ops' definition) and same-day guest arrivals before 15:00 (`Job.sameDayCheckinTime`). The header search uses the existing `GET /api/portal/search`; "New job" opens `/v2/admin/jobs/new`. Four counters link to their page already filtered: Unassigned next 48 h (`/v2/admin/jobs?statusChip=UNASSIGNED&dateFrom&dateTo`), Running late (`/v2/admin/ops?view=exceptions`), Waiting for QA (open or assigned inspections, `?tab=review`), Invoices to send (the Invoices list's own Needs action filter, finance only). Each tile is omitted for a viewer who cannot open its destination; an unreadable figure shows "—" and Unavailable, never 0.

**Work queue** (`lib/dashboard/work-queue*.ts`) replaces the old "Needs attention" list, the shared immediate-attention panel on Live ops and the Live ops planning lists. It is built from the existing sources (`getAdminAttentionSummary`, receivables, per-record loaders) and keeps every kind they produced: unassigned (next 48 h per job, the rest as a count), overdue / open / high-priority / new / stale cases, continuation, timing, skip, client task, client approval, clock adjustment, QA rework, QA outcome, pay request, flagged laundry, laundry reschedule draft, laundry exceptions (next 2 days), QA pending, outstanding and draft invoices, low stock, new leads (24 h, linked to the lead's quote), expiring documents (14 days, linked to the account), plus shopping reimbursements awaiting approval and lost items reported. Urgency (now / today / this week) and categories are named constants (`work-queue-rules.ts`). Inline actions call existing endpoints unchanged: continuation approve/decline (`PATCH /api/admin/job-continuations/[id]`), reimbursement approve (`PATCH /api/admin/inventory/shopping-runs/[id]` `{cleanerReimbursementStatus:"READY"}`), Assign / Suggest cleaner (the Jobs peek panel), Contact guest (mailto/tel from the recorded contact). The row shows pending, then the server's answer; only a confirmed success removes it; a refusal keeps the row with the server's reason; a network failure says the outcome is unknown. Rows are omitted server-side when the viewer cannot open their destination, money rows need `finance` (pay requests also `payroll`, reimbursements also `inventory`), actions need the endpoint's method level, and a read-only impersonation gets no actions. Chip counts are records, not rows; overlapping views (high, new, stale cases) are not added twice. A failing source is named ("may be incomplete") instead of reading as all clear. Expiry reminders are link-only (no inline send; see the plan's owner items).

**Today by cleaner** draws today's jobs per cleaner on an hour axis from the Live ops board (done, on site, travelling, late, off site, not started, planned, unassigned), each state with its own shape and a written label, with a table view. A job with no recorded length is drawn at 60 min and marked. **Money this month** (finance or payroll only, omitted server-side otherwise): Paid this month and Outstanding with overdue count (`getClientInvoiceStats`), Revenue / Known charges today with the missing-rate note (`getDashboardMetrics`), Cleaner pay due (month-to-date gross from `getPayrollSummary` in the organisation timezone, excluded missing-rate jobs counted). **Below the fold:** today by status (each a link to Jobs for that status today), on the way / on site / clocked in / completed, Today's dispatch → the Jobs Today view, laundry due today, upcoming six days, cleaners tomorrow, recent feedback.

Each section is its own server component in its own Suspense boundary with a first read, then refreshes alone from `GET /api/admin/command/[section]` (`tiles`, `queue`, `cleaners`, `money`, `glance`; dashboard pack; live `requireRole` on every call) every 30 s while visible and on focus (money every 5 min). Obsolete reads are aborted and late answers dropped; a failed refresh keeps the last figures marked stale with Retry; a failed first read shows that section's error only.

**Live ops (`/v2/admin/ops`).** h1 "Live ops"; summary of cleaners and laundry drivers on shift and the age of the freshest GPS position; a stream indicator from the real EventSource state (Live, Reconnecting · last update, Stale since … after 2 min without a message or heartbeat, or "Refreshing every 15 s" when the stream is off); layer chips (Cleaners, Laundry drivers, Properties); Plan routes (route map for today). One provider (`live-feed-provider.tsx`) owns the position snapshot (15 s), the stream and the board (`GET /api/admin/ops/live-board`, 30 s and on focus); the list and the map read it, so the feed is no longer fetched twice. The board (`lib/ops/live-board.ts`, rules in `live-board-rules.ts`) classifies today's published jobs: Late = on the way, not arrived, projected arrival (recorded ETA, never before now) more than 10 min after the start time; Off site with the clock running = open time log and live status off site / left site (minutes since the recorded departure, distance at the last ping); Not started = assigned or unassigned, not on the way or clocked in, more than 15 min after start. Chips (Exceptions, On site, Travelling, Not started) are real counts of the whole day. Exceptions carry Call (tel), Message and Tell client (both open the job's Messages tab; no delay notice is sent from here), Reassign (Jobs peek panel), Approve / Decline (same endpoint and body as before) and Review clock (the job's Activity tab, where clock records are edited; there is no admin pause endpoint). On-site rows show elapsed of allocated time and checklist progress from the cleaner's live draft (`computeChecklistProgressPercent`), or "of allocated time" when there is no checklist data; laundry drivers show their active route's stops. The map (presentational `estate-ops-map.tsx`) shows the selected person's card (ETA, next job, phone). A Field status disclosure keeps the old live map's safety check-in and GPS check-in list with Maps links. "Also needs attention" links to Command's queue with counts. `/v2/admin/ops/map` redirects to `/v2/admin/ops` keeping its parameters, or to the route map for another `?date=`; `/v2/admin/jobs/route-map` is unchanged apart from its Live ops link.

**Fixed here:** Live ops built "today" from the server's local clock; its counters were the length of lists capped at 8; two components polled the same location feed; stale-case rows linked to a `?jobId=` filter the Cases page ignored (now `/v2/admin/cases?open=<caseId>`, the selection parameter of the Cases inbox being built in parallel, plus a job link; case counts link to `?queue=open`); leads all linked to the pipeline (now the lead's quote when one exists); documents linked to the accounts list (now the person). `getAdminAttentionSummary` "new cases since yesterday" and the cleaner/client attention date cut-offs used server-local time (now Sydney keys). The old Command page failed whole when any of seven queries failed.

### Cases inbox and Laundry pipeline (2026-11)

**Cases (`/v2/admin/cases`).** Four columns at desktop as designed: Queues, Case list, Conversation, Details. Queues are server filters with real counts (`lib/cases/queues.ts`): *Needs my reply* (open, owned by the viewer, and the latest non-internal message was written by a CLIENT or VA account; internal and status notes never count), *Unowned*, *Waiting on client* (status WAITING_CLIENT), *SLA at risk* (open, deadline under 4 h away or passed, the old rule), *All open*, *Resolved* (RESOLVED or CLOSED), plus the old *Awaiting me* and *All*. Type filters use the system's case types with counts. Old view ids (`awaitingClient`…) still resolve.

The list reads `GET /api/admin/cases?shape=summary&queue&q&caseType&status&assigneeUserId&jobId&reportId&page&pageSize&sort&dir` (`lib/cases/summary-request.ts`, `lib/cases/summaries.ts`): one page of summaries (no comment or attachment bodies; comment/attachment counts, the latest non-internal message excerpt, "client replied", invoice reference from legacy dispute metadata), the real total, queue counts and type counts. Sorts (allow-listed through `parseListQuery`): SLA soonest, newest, oldest, priority, recently updated, type (damage first); ties fall back to the existing damage-first working order. Unknown queue, sort, direction, status or a malformed id answers 400. Without `shape=summary` the route returns the old `{ items, assignees, viewer }` shape unchanged (classic workspace, `phase4/disputes` alias, `admin/issues`). `countsOnly=1` returns only the counts, used after a save so the list is not reloaded.

Opening a case (`?open=<caseId>`) loads the full case and `GET /api/admin/cases/[id]/linked` (`lib/cases/linked-context.ts`, read-only): job, property, client, cleaners on the job, the departing guest from the job's reservation (name, phone, check-out, as job detail shows), the service report (case report or the job's), job photo count and client/office attachment counts; with no job linked it says so rather than showing zeros. Obsolete reads are aborted and checked after parsing, so a late answer never fills another case. `?jobId=` (Live ops links) and `?reportId=` open all of that job's or report's cases; `?q=` and `?queue=` are honoured.

Conversation: state actions *Waiting on client* and *Resolve*; the "more" menu holds Triage, Investigate, Waiting on team, Close, Reopen (shown by status as before), Assign to me, Edit details, Open job, Open report, Delete case (typed DELETE plus PIN or password, unchanged). Every status change goes through the existing required note. Thread interleaves messages, gold internal notes labelled "Internal note" and attachments, times in Sydney. Composer: Reply to client | Internal note (`isInternal`), Insert template (the existing per-type resolution templates), Attach (existing direct upload and attach call). Reply to client is the default only when the case is client-visible; a reply on a hidden case says it stays on the office timeline (as before). Unsent text is kept per case while the page is open. Details: owner (and Assign to me), priority, type, status, client can see / can reply, title, description, resolution note with Use template — saved as a draft of changed fields only. Next steps link only to existing places: Create repair quote (`/v2/admin/quotes/new`, which takes no prefill), Add charge to invoice (the client's draft invoices, or New invoice); nothing is charged from Cases. "Raise QA issue for cleaner" is not offered: QA issues are only created by a QA review of the job.

New case: the old fields plus optional job (search on `/api/jobs`), property and client (`/api/admin/properties`, `/api/admin/clients`); picking a job fills its property and client visibly. `POST /api/admin/cases` validates links (`lib/cases/link-validation.ts`): each must exist, a job implies its property, a property must belong to the chosen client; the client is never derived silently. Bulk (`POST /api/admin/cases/bulk`, `lib/cases/bulk.ts`): assign owner (must be an active office user), change status or close (CLOSED), each with the required note, one outcome per case through `applyCaseUpdate` (`lib/cases/apply-update.ts`, now also used by `PATCH /api/admin/cases/[id]`). The list refreshes on focus and every 30 s without blanking rows. Phone: queue and type selectors, list then case, details in a sheet.

**Laundry (`/v2/admin/laundry`).** Header: "Laundry", the day's summary (`EEE d MMM · N pickups and M drops today · K exceptions`, from the whole day before filters), Previous / Today / Next day, Generate week (the existing generate-week flow, relabelled from "New run") and Add run (a dialog over the existing `POST /api/admin/laundry`, the classic "Create laundry task"). Three tiles with their scope stated: Runs today (pickup or drop-off dated today, Sydney), In transit (picked up, not delivered, any date), Delivered today. A line links to Approvals with the count of FLAGGED runs (the Approvals laundry queue). Tabs: Pipeline, By property, Drivers live, Costs and invoices, Suppliers, Statistics. `?tab=` accepts new and old ids (today/active → Pipeline, completed → Pipeline's Completed history, investigate → By property, live, reports → Costs and invoices, suppliers, stats); `?date=` opens the Pipeline on that day; `?open=<taskId>` opens the run panel (read through the new `GET /api/admin/laundry/[taskId]` when the run is not on screen). `/v2/admin/laundry/stats` redirects to the Statistics tab keeping its other parameters; its figures come from `GET /api/admin/laundry/stats` (`lib/laundry/stats-load.ts`, same `ops-stats` rules) with the team metrics section.

Pipeline (`GET /api/admin/laundry/pipeline?date&q&supplierId&driverId`, `lib/laundry/pipeline.ts` rules, `lib/laundry/pipeline-server.ts`): lanes for the Sydney day — To pick up (PENDING/CONFIRMED/FLAGGED with pickup that day; on today's view also earlier pickups not done), At the laundry (PICKED_UP, picked up by that day, drop-off later), To drop (PICKED_UP with drop-off that day; on today's view also earlier drop-offs not done), Delivered (DROPPED that day). SKIPPED_PICKUP is in no lane. Dates compare as Sydney days, which reads both stored conventions alike across daylight saving. Exceptions: flagged, failed pickup awaiting a decision, not ready (latest cleaner handoff), pickup overdue, drop overdue, over the supplier's average turnaround (hours) or, with no supplier figure, over the Laundry operations max outdoor days, and no drop photo when the portal setting requires one. Lane counts are after filters. Driver = the day's laundry route that holds the stop (stop number shown); "No route yet" filters the rest. Cards carry one action: Move to tomorrow / next day. Multi-select moves several. Moves go through `POST /api/admin/laundry/reschedule` (`lib/laundry/reschedule.ts`): pickup moves and the gap to drop-off is kept; for a picked-up run only the drop-off moves (never before pickup); delivered or skipped runs are refused; each row carries the `updatedAt` the screen saw and a changed run is refused, never overwritten; an audit row `LAUNDRY_RESCHEDULE` per move. The admin edit `PATCH /api/admin/laundry/[taskId]` accepts the same optional `expectedUpdatedAt` and the edit dialog now sends it. "Ask cleaner", "Chase supplier" and "Request photo" are not offered: no existing reminder path does that on demand. Completed history (inside Pipeline) is the old Active runs / Completed date-range list with search, status and Completed / Active / All. Polling (30 s and focus) runs only on the Pipeline lanes and Drivers live, pauses while a move, delete or edit is in progress; the shared week feed for history and By property loads once per range, only when one of them is open. The run panel holds status, linked clean and cleaners, dates, next guest (job's reservation context), supplier, driver, bags/weight/cost when recorded, evidence (existing media gallery), history from confirmations and every old card action: Edit, Reschedule, Replace evidence, Skip or delete (existing soft/permanent dialog), Task report / PDF, Open linked job. By property is the old Investigate tab restyled with Estate fields.

**Fixed here:** `GET /api/admin/laundry/live` bounded "today" by the server's local midnight (on a UTC host a morning stop showed under the wrong day); it now uses the Sydney day. The admin laundry edit could overwrite a run changed after the dialog opened (now refused with 409 when the version is sent). Stat captions said "in selected date range" for figures that were not.

## Existing problems fixed (2026-11-11)

Cleared under the owner's standing rule (fix existing problems by default, with a test). No schema change, no change to who can see or do what, no money rule changed.

- **Voided invoice no longer counts as invoiced (Jobs).** The Jobs Invoice filter, the "Not invoiced" view tab and its count treated any `ClientInvoiceLine` as invoiced, so a job whose only line sat on a VOID invoice never showed as unbilled. `lib/jobs/invoiced-rule.ts` now holds the Jobs rule: invoiced = status INVOICED, or a line on a non-VOID invoice. It mirrors `billedJobLinesWhere` in `lib/billing/client-invoices.ts` (the generator's "not on another non-void invoice" rule, which is client-scoped and server-only, so it is mirrored, not imported); `tests/lib/jobs-invoiced-rule.test.ts` pins both to the same invoice statuses. Used by `buildJobsWhere` (list, count, export, board) and the grouped view counts (SQL joins `ClientInvoice` and ignores VOID). The job detail's invoice-line list says "Not on a live invoice" when every line is on a voided invoice. The INVOICED job status still counts as invoiced (an explicit marker; the generator does not set it). Deletion / reset guards (`lib/jobs/delete-financial-guard.ts`, QA reset, job reset) still treat any invoice line, VOID included, as a financial record and were not changed.
- **Job reminder records its real state.** `lib/jobs/job-reminder.ts` (single and bulk routes): the audit row (`JOB_REMINDER_SENT`, name kept for history) now stores `requested`, `failed`, `deliveryState` (`QUEUED` for push, `PROVIDER_ACCEPTED` for email) and `deliveryConfirmed: false` instead of `sent`. The single route keeps its codes (404 / 409 `notRemindable` / 400) and its `sent` / `failed` keys, and adds `deliveryState` and `message` ("Push requested for …; delivery is not confirmed."). The button's toast says "Reminder queued" / "Reminder accepted for email", never "sent" or "delivered". A browser-push error after the inbox notice was recorded no longer reports the cleaner as not reminded (a retry would duplicate the notice). The admin note and names are HTML-escaped in the email. The PUSH inbox row keeps the existing convention (PUSH + SENT = available in the inbox, phone push pending in the mobile-outbox marker), which the mobile outbox requires. **Not moved onto the notification-intent pipeline:** `lib/notifications/intent-store.ts` / `queue-delivery.ts` / `intent-provider.ts` only authorize `ADMIN_OPERATIONS` recipients (ADMIN / OPS_MANAGER) and say not to generalize that to assignments, so cleaners need a new job-assignment authorization adapter (an access decision); the pipeline has no phone-app transport (INBOX intents are deliberately excluded from the mobile outbox); and EMAIL / WEB_PUSH intents are only dispatched when `SNEEK_NOTIFICATION_OUTBOX_TRANSPORTS` opts in, so moving the email reminder would stop it in any deployment without that setting. Owner decision needed.
- **Field labels tied to their controls.** `EField` in `components/v2/admin/estate-kit.tsx`, the settings `EField` (`components/v2/admin/settings/estate-form.tsx`) and the worker `EField` (`components/v2/cleaner/fields.tsx`) use `useAssociatedField` (`components/v2/admin/field-association.tsx`): when the only child is a native `input` / `select` / `textarea` or a kit control marked with `markFieldControl` (each kit's `EInput`, `ETextarea`, `ESelect` / `ESelectNative`), it gets a generated id (an existing id or `htmlFor` wins) and the hint id in `aria-describedby`. Any other child (layout `div`, composite widget, several children, hidden input) renders as before. The Quality screens use `EField` again instead of the `LabeledField` workaround; `labeled-field.tsx` stays for its one remaining importer in `components/v2/admin/insights/`.
- **QA issue register dates are Sydney days.** `GET /api/admin/qa/issues` read `from` as UTC midnight and `to` with server-local `setHours(23,59,59)`. It now uses `sydneyCreatedAtFilter` (`lib/quality/period.ts`): `from` = Sydney midnight, `to` = the whole Sydney day (exclusive next Sydney midnight), the same days as a custom Quality hub period, across daylight-saving changes; a full timestamp is read as its Sydney day; unreadable values are ignored as before. Internal 500 errors are no longer echoed.
- **Quality hub tools follow the viewer's packs.** `lib/quality/hub-access.ts` decides, with the middleware's `canUseOpsPath`, what the page passes to the hub: the Coaching tab is hidden when the coaching records (`quality`) or cleaner list (`/api/admin/users`, `accounts`) would refuse an operations manager; create / update controls only with quality manage; the "Open the full coaching page" link only with `workforce`; "Review bonus" only with `payroll` manage. ADMIN keeps every tool. `CoachingWorkspace` now shows "You do not have access to coaching records." or "Coaching records could not be loaded." with Try again instead of an empty list, warns when the cleaner list fails (New record disabled), and cancels an older filter read. Issue evidence photos the access rail refuses show "Photo unavailable" instead of a loading tile forever. No access was widened.
- **Old template-versions writes refused.** `POST /api/admin/phase4/template-versions/[templateId]` (create a sibling "… vN" and deactivate the others) and `…/rollback` (switch `isActive`) had no caller in `app/`, `components/`, `lib/`, `mobile-app/` or tests and changed which form jobs resolve to. Both now answer 410 with a message pointing to the Forms library's `GET /api/admin/form-templates/{id}/versions` and restore-as-draft; the GET read route is kept for anything external; their write functions were removed from `lib/phase4/template-versions.ts`. Auth answers stay 401 / 403.
- **Sweep.** Removed the leftover "Estate preview · live data…" / "Estate workspace…" footers (Command, Activity, Clients list and detail, Laundry). Removed `components/v2/admin/forms/builder/new-template.tsx`, superseded by `NewFormLauncher` and imported nowhere. `components/v2/admin/jobs/reclean-review-panel.tsx` is also imported nowhere but is an unwired feature, not a replaced one, so it was left.

Tests: `tests/lib/jobs-invoiced-rule.test.ts`, `tests/lib/jobs-view-presets.test.ts` (updated: it encoded the void rule), `tests/api/jobs-bulk-remind.test.ts` (extended), `tests/api/audit-v03-notification-producers.test.ts` (response shape updated), `tests/components/estate-field-label.test.tsx`, `tests/api/qa-issues-sydney-range.test.ts`, `tests/components/quality-hub-coaching-access.test.tsx`, `tests/api/legacy-template-versions-gone.test.ts`.

## Notifications in one place (2026-11-11)

Owner decision (second set, item 1): move job reminders onto the notification pipeline, keep every notification that works today going out, and control all notifications from one area. No schema change; nothing that was on was switched off and nothing that was off was switched on.

**Catalog.** `lib/notifications/catalog.ts` (data in `catalog-jobs.ts`, `catalog-office.ts`, `catalog-people.ts`, `catalog-actions.ts`; types in `catalog-types.ts`) lists every notification type: stable key, trigger, audience, transports it can use and uses by default, path (pipeline / pipeline with direct fallback / direct / not sent today), pipeline event keys, the existing switches that govern it, its template and whether personal preferences apply. It was built by reading every producer (each file that calls a low-level send helper). `tests/lib/notification-catalog.test.ts` scans `app/`, `lib/` and `workers/` for those helper calls and fails when a file sends something no entry lists, or a pipeline event key has no entry.

**Pipeline extension (smallest safe one).**
- Recipient scopes (`lib/notifications/recipient-scopes.ts`): `JOB_ASSIGNED_CLEANER` (assignment on the job not removed, account active), `LAUNDRY_TASK_CLEANER`, `STAFF_MEMBER` (active staff account addressed by id; never clients or VAs) and `JOB_CLIENT_CONTACT` (active CLIENT login of the job's client). Each is an explicit ENTITY scope checked when the intent is queued and again at dispatch (`deliverInboxIntent`, `deliverProviderIntent`); the recorded role must still match. Unknown entity scopes stay refused; `ADMIN_OPERATIONS` keeps its original rule.
- Envelope (`intent-contract.ts`), two optional fields, absent on every existing envelope (hashes unchanged): `mobilePushCategory` (INBOX only: the delivered inbox row carries `mobile-outbox:pending:<category>`, the explicit declaration the marker comment asks for, so the phone app keeps its push) and `recipientPreferences: "IGNORE"` (office-directed types that do not respect personal preferences).
- `lib/notifications/office-sends.ts`: office-initiated sends. "Push" = pipeline INBOX intent delivered at once (`inline-dispatch.ts`, the worker's own claim/deliver/reconcile steps; left for the five-minute worker if that fails) + phone push via the mobile outbox + browser push. Browser push and email go through the pipeline only when their transport is listed in `SNEEK_NOTIFICATION_OUTBOX_TRANSPORTS`; otherwise the direct send is kept (`transport-paths.ts`), claimed once per event and recipient (`dispatch-once.ts`) and the direct email is recorded as a `Notification` row. Every outcome is truthful: `queued`, `inbox_available`, `provider_accepted`, `requested` (browser push gives no receipt), `already_requested`, `uncertain`, `skipped`, `failed`, `switched_off`; never "sent" or "delivered" on request.
- Manual job reminder (`lib/jobs/job-reminder.ts`, single and bulk routes): now on the pipeline with `JOB_ASSIGNED_CLEANER`, per-cleaner outcomes and the path in the audit row (`JOB_REMINDER_SENT`), optional `idempotencyKey` (bulk derives one per job). If the pipeline enqueue fails, every cleaner is reported "Not sent" with `error: "pipeline_unavailable"` (nothing was queued). Bulk wording: "Push requested", "Email queued" (pipeline) or "Email accepted by the provider" (direct); delivery never confirmed.
- Composer sends (`/api/admin/messages/send`) are now recorded in the delivery log with the provider result (no account attached; still no audit row).

**Per-type controls** (`lib/notifications/type-controls.ts`, AppSetting `notificationTypeControls`, audited `NOTIFICATION_TYPE_CONTROLS_UPDATED`, stale-write refused): `respectsPreferences` for ADMIN_SWITCH types (default off = previous behaviour: manual reminders ignore personal category preferences; the email chokepoint's own gates still apply) and `disabledTransports` for types with a type switch (default none). Existing switches are changed in place through the notifications area (`catalog-switches.ts`): the automatic email kind switches and the scheduled-notification switches, audited as `SETTINGS_UPDATE`.

**The notifications area** (`/v2/admin/notifications`, the former Comms center, extended; nav entry "Notifications" in Configuration; settings catalog destination "All notifications" first in the Notifications group; old settings tabs keep working with a pointer). One h1, tabs via `?tab=` (no tab = Types):
- Types: the catalog table (group chips, transports with on/off state, path, template link to the Forms page's Message templates tab, preferences); side panel with trigger, recipient rule, transports and their path, switches (administrators edit; operations managers read), the preferences switch, "Send test to me" (signed-in user only, STAFF_MEMBER scope, `[Test]`, refused while impersonating) and the last 20 deliveries.
- Delivery log: `GET /api/admin/notifications/deliveries` merges pipeline intents, `Notification` rows and `NotificationLog` rows (template engine, email provider webhook) with real totals, time sort, filters (type, state, transport, recipient, dates, source), drill-in metadata (`?detail=`; no bodies, HTML, addresses or provider references; codes, PINs and link tokens masked by `redactSensitiveText`), retry only through the existing review endpoint (known rejection / inbox reconcile), the existing "Clear log" with its confirmation, and lazy disclosures for the review queue and the classic log.
- Health: `GET /api/admin/notifications/health`: per transport configured or not (presence only), pipeline switch, queue depth, oldest waiting, failures in 24 h; worker heartbeat (phone dispatcher, pipeline dispatcher where reported); master switches; non-secret env flags; direct-path banners; what cannot be known. Run-now dispatch, resend, test push and provider tests moved here as disclosures.
- Preferences: the control center and profile overrides, and for administrators the scheduled notifications, message channels and finance templates (same components and endpoints as the settings tabs); personal preferences stay on Accounts.

Access mirrors the existing APIs: reads ADMIN and OPS_MANAGER (ops pack "communications" via the middleware), switch changes ADMIN only and never while impersonating.

**Office actions for later buttons** (`lib/notifications/office-actions.ts`, `guest-message.ts`; routes under `/api/admin/notifications/actions/`): staff message (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER), laundry cleaner nudge (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER), client delay notice (ADMIN only, honours the client's "notifications off"), guest message for a lost and found item (ADMIN only; email/SMS chokepoints; per-sender and per-item limits counted from the audit log; masked address in the audit). All refuse impersonation, take explicit transports and return truthful outcomes. No buttons were built.

**Env switches and their effect.**

| Variable | Effect |
| --- | --- |
| `SNEEK_NOTIFICATION_OUTBOX_TRANSPORTS` | Comma list of `EMAIL`, `SMS`, `WEB_PUSH` the pipeline hands to providers. Empty (default): pipeline sends inbox rows only; fallback types send browser push and email directly (banner in Health). Adding `EMAIL` also lets the existing pipeline producers' email intents (low stock from submissions) go out. |
| `SNEEK_WORKERS_ENABLED`, `SNEEK_WORKERS_DISABLED`, `SNEEK_EMBEDDED_WORKER`, `SNEEK_WEB_SCHEDULER_ENABLED` | Whether the worker runs (scheduled notifications, mobile outbox every minute, pipeline dispatcher every five minutes). |
| `SNEEK_DISABLED_JOBS`, `SNEEK_ENABLED_JOBS` | Per-job switches; `mobile-notification-dispatch` and `notification-intent-dispatch` must not be disabled. |
| `RESEND_API_KEY`, `EMAIL_FROM` | Email provider (presence only shown). |
| `TWILIO_*` / `CELLCAST_*` with the SMS provider setting | SMS provider. |
| `VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY`, `VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY`, `VAPID_SUBJECT` (or Integrations credentials) | Browser push. |
| `EXPO_PUSH_API_URL`, `APP_URL` | Phone push endpoint and link base. |

**Operator note (production check so everything that sent before still sends).** Nothing must be changed for current behaviour: with `SNEEK_NOTIFICATION_OUTBOX_TRANSPORTS` unset, reminder browser push and email keep the direct path. Check: (1) the worker is running (Health shows ACTIVE) and `SNEEK_DISABLED_JOBS` does not contain `mobile-notification-dispatch` (phone pushes) or `notification-intent-dispatch` (pipeline inbox rows that could not be delivered at once, and the existing low stock / urgent stock / failed pickup inbox notices); (2) `RESEND_API_KEY` present; (3) VAPID keys present if browser push was working; (4) the SMS provider set and configured if SMS was used. To move reminder email and browser push onto the pipeline, set `SNEEK_NOTIFICATION_OUTBOX_TRANSPORTS=EMAIL,WEB_PUSH` on the worker and web containers; this also starts sending the email part of "Low stock from a cleaner submission", including any EMAIL intents already sitting QUEUED from before (a backlog of old alerts; check Health's EMAIL queue count first and review them in the Delivery log), which is the owner's call.

## Notification types and wording (2026-11-12)

Owner request: "Fix the notifications page and allow to change/edit/regenerate any notification templates". Approved board **Notifications: types and wording** (`NotificationTemplates.dc.html`). No schema change.

**Screen.** `/v2/admin/notifications` (tab "Types and wording N", old links and `?tab=` kept; `?type=<key>` opens a type). Left: every catalog type grouped by audience (Cleaners, Clients, Guests, Laundry, Office, Accounts; `lib/notifications/wording/audience-groups.ts`) with search, chips All / Edited / Off / Missing a variable, transport tags (In, Push, Email, SMS; filled = used by default and not switched off) and the row control from the EXISTING type controls (`type-row-switch.ts`: the per-type transport control, or the one existing switch; several switches show "On / Partly on / Off", none shows "Office sends" or "Always on"). Middle: details card (when, who, the "Respect each person's own notification choices" switch; "⋯" opens transports and switches, the last 20 deliveries and source files), the wording editor per transport (Inbox, Push, Email, SMS; audience/part chips; variable chips inserted at the caret; required-variable check; SMS characters and segments; Regenerate wording; Reset to default; Cancel; Save as vN) and the version history (compare, restore). Right: live preview with invented sample data (SMS on a phone, the email body in a sandboxed frame, the inbox row) and Send test to me. Header: transport health badges (from Health) and "Send test to me" (moves focus to the test). Switches stay ADMIN only and read-only while impersonating.

**Wording layer** (`lib/notifications/wording/`).
- Registry (`registry.ts`, `definitions/*.ts`): one definition per catalog type (a test fails for a type without one) with a status: Editable, Editable parts, Office sends (the sender writes it; message templates stay in Forms), Edited elsewhere (lifecycle emails in Forms, finance templates in Preferences) or In code (with the exact reason). Each part has default wording per transport, its variables and required variables. Fixed sentences used only under a condition (an admin note, timing notes, a new estimate) are separate "line" parts so the logic stays in code and the sentence is editable.
- Email-template parts are the existing shared templates (`settings.emailTemplates`, also listed in Forms): saving writes there (the `{name}` syntax converted from the editor's `{{name}}`), so `renderEmailTemplate` and Forms see the same text; this layer keeps their version history and flags an edit made in Forms ("changed in Forms").
- Store (`store.ts`): AppSetting row `notificationWording:<typeKey>` with every part's versions (v1 = the default, never stored; each save, restore or reset is a new version; last 60 kept). Advisory lock per row, stale-write guard on the live wording's hash (409), validation (unknown or missing required variable, broken placeholder, script in email: 400), audit `NOTIFICATION_WORDING_UPDATED` with before, after, actor and role.
- ONE render function (`render.ts`: `renderNotificationWording`, `loadNotificationWording` for a batch, `defaultNotificationWording` for previews): plain `{{variable}}` substitution only (own values only, no expressions), values HTML-escaped in email bodies (built fragments excepted), an invalid stored override falls back to the default and is logged, a store read failure sends the default wording. With no override the output is byte-identical to the code it replaced (`tests/lib/notification-wording-parity.test.ts`, the old compositions copied verbatim). Wired senders: job cancelled / restored, manual and daily unfinished reminders, 2-hour reminder SMS, cleaner day reminder (inbox, email, SMS), failed laundry pickup, laundry nudge and team request, client delay notice (and its confirm preview), clock paused by the office, urgent-stock purchase ask, document request (inbox, email, SMS). Email-template parts render through the existing `renderEmailTemplate`.
- Regenerate (`regenerate.ts`, `ai-provider.ts`): the app's configured AI text provider (Settings, AI; same selection as the post composer). The prompt holds only the current template text, variable names and labels and tone guidance. The answer is a PROPOSAL shown beside the current wording; a proposal that drops a required variable or adds an unknown one is rejected with the reason; "Use this wording" only fills the editor. Never saved or sent by the route; each request is audited (`NOTIFICATION_WORDING_PROPOSAL_REQUESTED`). No provider: the button is disabled with the reason.
- Access (`permission.ts`, owner decision 2026-11-12): editing wording, restoring, regenerating and Send test to me are allowed for ADMIN and for an operations manager whose notifications (communications) pack is at Manage; never while impersonating. Reading is ADMIN and OPS_MANAGER as before.

**Routes.** `GET /api/admin/notifications/wording` (summaries and whether you can edit), `GET|PUT /api/admin/notifications/wording/[typeKey]` (view; save EDIT / RESTORE / RESET with `expectedHash`), `POST /api/admin/notifications/wording/regenerate` (422 rejected proposal, 409 no provider), `POST /api/admin/notifications/catalog/test` (now any type with wording or a catalog test; transports INBOX, PUSH, EMAIL, SMS chosen explicitly; SMS to your own phone, reported as provider accepted / skipped / failed; the live wording with sample data, marked [Test]).

**Fixes to the existing page.** Reads no longer show the previous type's or filter's data under a new request while loading (`use-notification-read.ts`); every time on the delivery log, delivery detail, recent deliveries and Health is shown in the organisation timezone (was the viewer's browser); the delivery log date filter uses organisation days.

## Lost and found and urgent stock (admin redesign, 2026-11-11)

**Lost and found** (`/v2/admin/lost-found`, ADMIN and OPS_MANAGER as before; `components/v2/admin/lost-found/*`, `lib/lost-found/*`).
- Page as drawn: header summary ("N open items · N waiting more than 14 days", real counts), Export (CSV of the filtered list, `GET /api/admin/lost-found/export`), Log an item; stage tabs New / In storage / Guest contacted / Return arranged / Closed / All mapped onto the unchanged statuses (Closed = RETURNED, DISPOSED, DONATED, UNCLAIMED, ARCHIVED; a "Closed as" filter picks one); search (item, guest, guest email, property, found location, description; case-insensitive), Property, Found date (organisation calendar days), Value, "Held over N days"; sort control; one table with server paging and sorting (`GET /api/admin/lost-found?paginated=1`, `lib/admin/list-query`; without `paginated=1` the old `{ items, openCount, properties }` answer is unchanged); bulk Move to storage / Mark unclaimed / Donate or dispose (`POST /api/admin/lost-found/bulk`, one answer per item through the single-item change); item panel on `?open=`.
- **Guest from the job's booking.** `Job.reservation` is the departing guest. When an item is created with a job (cleaner or office, `lib/lost-found/create.ts`), the booking's guest name, phone (into `guestContact`) and email are copied, `reservationId` is linked and `guestSource` is BOOKING; a system timeline entry "Guest details added from booking …" is written (not the reporter's, so the cleaner never sees it). Stay dates, party, booking code and source are read live from the linked booking. A job without a booking copies nothing ("No booking on job"). An office edit sets MANUAL; nothing automatic writes guest fields after creation. "Pick another booking at this property" lists bookings that ended 21 days before to 3 days after the item was found; choosing one copies that guest (confirmation first if the details were MANUAL). The panel also shows the client and uses the property address in the client message.
- **Office can log an item** (`POST /api/admin/lost-found`): property required, job optional (must be at that property; picker of the property's jobs from the last 45 days and next 2, `GET /api/admin/lost-found/options`), photos through the existing direct upload with the upload-ownership check; links derived from keys.
- **Transition table** (`lib/lost-found/transitions.ts`): from an open status every move the old board allowed (any open status, any decision, Archived); Unclaimed → Returned / Donated / Disposed; any decision → Archived (the decision stays on the item); a closed item goes back to an open status only through Reopen with a reason, recorded as REOPENED with the previous decision kept in the entry's `previousResolution`; a different decision needs a reopen first. Offer return and Guest contacted go through the same table.
- **Holding period**: 14 days by default (`lost_found_v1:settings` AppSetting; Settings → Operations → Lost and found, ADMIN, audited). Calendar days in the organisation zone from the found day; only In storage, Guest contacted and Return arranged count; flagged in the table and panel, counted in the header, filterable. Nothing is disposed automatically.
- **Message guest / Tell client** (`lib/lost-found/message.ts`): an editable draft (item, found date and place, return options, office phone). Guest, ADMIN: Send by email / text through the notification system's guest-message action (`lib/notifications/guest-message.ts`; explicit transport, rate limits, audit, idempotency key kept until success) via `POST /api/admin/lost-found/[id]/send-guest-message`; the item moves to Guest contacted only when the provider accepted it, and the timeline says "accepted; delivery is not confirmed"; a skipped or failed send is recorded as "Message not sent". The intent pipeline itself has no guest recipient. Otherwise (operations managers, every client message, or by choice): Copy, mailto:, sms:, tel:, then "Mark guest contacted" / "Record client told", which records the channel the office says it used (`recordedByOffice`, `sentByApp: false`). No lost-and-found client recipient exists on the pipeline.
- The reporting cleaner's responses stay redacted: guest name, contact, email, source, booking, estimated value, storage, client and address are removed, and office entries lose their note and metadata.
- Nav badge unchanged: REPORTED items.

**Urgent stock** (first tab of Stock, `/v2/admin/inventory?tab=urgent-stock`, ADMIN only as before; `components/v2/admin/inventory/urgent/*`).
- Header "Stock" with a real summary (open needs across properties; not yet delivered for a clean within 48 hours), Reminder settings (the existing administrator settings) and Report a need (the existing report form). Tabs: Urgent needs N first, then every existing tab with its real name and `?tab=` id; a bare `/v2/admin/inventory` opens Urgent needs for an administrator and Items for an operations manager. The KPI strip, Shopping list and Shelf labels stay on the other tabs.
- Stage chips Open / Reported / Acknowledged / Ordered / Delivered / Closed (real counts), search, Property; one all-property table (`GET /api/inventory/urgent-stock?paginated=1`; the per-property answer is unchanged) sorted by Needed by: the property's next clean deadline from the same rule as reminders (`urgentStockCleanDeadline`), "Passed" when gone, "–" when no clean is scheduled or the need is closed. One next action per row (Acknowledge → Mark ordered → Mark delivered → "Waiting for cleaner to confirm"); every action asks for the reason the service requires (an editable suggestion). Bulk Acknowledge / Mark ordered / Close (`POST /api/inventory/urgent-stock/bulk`, each need through `actOnUrgentStock` and its policy, per-need answers, one receipt per need per bulk request). "Add to shopping run" and "Ask cleaner to buy" are not offered: no existing service adds a line to a shopping run or asks a cleaner to buy.
- Need panel: stages, counted / par level / suggested buy (par less a known count, else unknown) / requested / reported by / note, the existing stay-preparation panel on demand, Confirm at property (fresh count) and Resolve, history. Wording: "Marked ordered", "Marked delivered; awaiting confirmation at the property". With a Property filter, the property's stay-preparation panel (including the per-stay quantities editor) is available under the table.
- `/urgent-stock` redirects administrators to `/v2/admin/inventory?tab=urgent-stock&propertyId=…`; cleaners keep the existing workspace; other roles see the same message as before. The cleaner disclosure and supplies tab are unchanged.
- Inventory nav badge: needs awaiting acknowledgement (REPORTED), administrators only (`urgentStock`, null for others).

**Urgent stock storage.** Needs and history moved from `urgent_stock_v1:need:*` / `event:*` / `open:*` AppSetting JSON to `UrgentStockNeed` and `UrgentStockEvent` (migration `20261111200000_lost_found_guest_urgent_stock_tables`, ids preserved; the open pointer became `openPairKey`; reporter taken from the first REPORTED event; `observedAt` kept as the exact submitted text because the stock-ledger note quotes it). Reminder settings and request receipts stay in AppSetting. The old JSON rows are not deleted in this release and are no longer read. `lib/inventory/urgent-stock.ts` keeps every exported function and response shape. Rows the migration could not read stay in AppSetting; count them with:

```sql
SELECT
  (SELECT count(*) FROM "AppSetting" a WHERE starts_with(a.key, 'urgent_stock_v1:need:')
     AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM "UrgentStockNeed" n WHERE a.key = 'urgent_stock_v1:need:' || n.id)) AS needs_not_copied,
  (SELECT count(*) FROM "AppSetting" a WHERE starts_with(a.key, 'urgent_stock_v1:event:')
     AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM "UrgentStockEvent" e WHERE a.key = 'urgent_stock_v1:event:' || e."needId" || ':' || e.id)) AS events_not_copied;
```

**Lost and found migration** (same migration): `LostFoundItem.guestEmail`, `guestSource` (enum BOOKING | MANUAL, nullable), `reservationId` (FK to Reservation, SET NULL), `storageLocation`.
