Backfill only where guest name and contact are both empty and the job's booking has a name or phone (BOOKING); items with guest text get MANUAL and nothing else; all other rows unchanged.


**Update (Wave F, 2026-11-12).** Urgent stock is no longer administrator-only: operations managers use it by their inventory pack; "Add to shopping run", "Ask cleaner to buy" and the job on a need are built; a form's count reaches on-hand only on approval. See "Wave F: urgent stock shopping runs and buy asks …" below.

## Live ops follow-up: delay notice, clock pause, decline reason, QA issue from a case, laundry nudges, bulk message (2026-11-11)

Owner decisions: third set items 2, 3 and 5 and fourth set item 1 in `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`. No schema change. Shared pieces: `components/v2/ui/action-dialog.tsx` (labelled, focus-trapped confirm dialog through `EstatePortal`; Escape and scrim close except while a request runs; focus returns to the opener) and `components/v2/admin/office-actions/*` (request helper, truthful state wording, per-recipient result list, the shared decline-reason dialog). No result ever says "sent" or "delivered": the states are Queued, In their inbox, Accepted by the provider, Requested (no receipt), Already requested earlier, Outcome unknown, Skipped, Failed, Switched off in Notifications.

**Notify client of delay (Live ops, late exception).** ADMIN only (the client delay notice route); operations managers and impersonated sessions keep the "Tell client" link to the job's Messages tab. "Notify client" opens a confirm that reads `GET /api/admin/notifications/actions/client-delay?jobId&etaMinutes` (ADMIN, `lib/notifications/client-delay-preview.ts`): the client logins it would reach, the exact wording from the send's own `clientDelayBody` (with the cleaner's ETA in whole minutes when one is recorded; `LiveJobRow.etaAt`), transports switched off for the type, the send's refusal if it would refuse (client switched notifications off, no active login, no client — shown as "Not sent: …"), and today's earlier request. The send is the unchanged POST with explicit transports (inbox + phone/browser push and/or email) and an idempotency key: `delay:<jobId>:<organisation day>` for the first notice of the day, so a double click or a retry after "no answer" is one pipeline event; the audit row now stores the key. A notice already requested today shows on the exception row ("Client delay notice requested at HH:mm by …", from the latest `CLIENT_DELAY_NOTICE_SENT` audit row since the start of the Sydney day) and in the dialog; only ticking "Send another notice" enables "Send another", which uses a fresh key.

**Administrator clock pause (Live ops, off site with the clock running).** `POST /api/admin/jobs/[id]/pause-clock` (ADMIN only; refused while impersonating; body `{ cleanerId, reason (3–500), transports }`), service `lib/jobs/admin-clock-pause.ts`. It calls the same `clockOutCleaner` (`lib/jobs/clock.ts`) the cleaner's Stop/Pause route and the NFC tap use: the same `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` on the Job row (so a simultaneous cleaner stop serialises and cannot double-close), the same duration (`round((now − startedAt) / 60 000)`, never negative), the same guard (`updateMany` where `stoppedAt` is null; zero rows → nothing else happens), and the same status rule: IN_PROGRESS (not skipped) becomes PAUSED; any other status is left alone, also when another cleaner on the job still has a running clock (exactly as the cleaner's own pause). `clockOutCleaner` gained an optional `note`, appended to the log's notes ("Paused by the office: <reason>", the way automatic clock-outs mark a stop the cleaner did not make). Only that cleaner's open log for the job is closed; closed logs and recorded time are never changed or deleted. No open log → 409 "No running clock". An `ADMIN_CLOCK_PAUSE` audit row (actor, cleaner, reason, stoppedAt, minutes, status after) is written in the same transaction. After the commit the cleaner is notified through the pipeline (`sendClockPausedNotice`, catalog `jobs.clock_paused_by_office`, JOB_ASSIGNED_CLEANER scope, one event per closed log, transports chosen in the dialog); a notice that cannot be queued does not undo the pause and the dialog says so. The cleaner's job screen re-reads on focus and every 90 s (added for the geofence clock-out), shows the job as Paused and offers "Resume clock" (header control), which is the existing `POST /api/cleaner/jobs/[id]/start` and opens a new log. The reason reaches the cleaner in the notification; the cleaner screen itself does not show time-log notes. "Review clock" (the job's Activity tab) stays as the secondary link.

**Declining a continuation request needs a reason.** `PATCH /api/admin/job-continuations/[id]` answers 400 "A reason is required to decline. The cleaner sees it." for `REJECT` without a non-blank `decisionNote`; `decideContinuationRequest` refuses it too. Approve is unchanged. The cleaner sees the reason where they saw the decision before: the "Continuation request declined" notification body (unchanged code path; it used to fall back to "Admin declined this request."). Callers: Live ops and Command's work queue use one `DeclineReasonDialog`; the queue's decline action carries `reason: { field: "decisionNote", subject }` and the row opens the dialog; the v2 job page (`job-detail-reviews.tsx`) and the classic `/admin/jobs/[id]` dialog require the note. Approvals only links to these requests.

**Raise QA issue for cleaner (Cases → Next steps).** Shown when the case's linked job has an assigned cleaner (`CaseLinkedContext.cleaners` now carries ids). The dialog reads `GET /api/admin/quality/issues/case-options?caseId` (job cleaners, configured categories, MINOR/MAJOR/CRITICAL, image attachments of the case, the job's form photos, issues already raised from the case, and `canRaise`) and posts `POST /api/admin/quality/issues` (`lib/quality/raise-from-case.ts`): ADMIN, or OPS_MANAGER whose quality pack is at manage level (the pack rule of the issue PATCH and the hub's actions); QA inspectors are refused; refused while impersonating. It creates a normal `QaIssue` (job, property, cleaner, category, severity, description, evidence keys in `qaPhotoKeys`, chosen job photos also in `cleanerMediaIds`, `raisedById` = the actor) marked as raised from the case in the existing `itemKey` column as `case:<caseId>` (helpers in `lib/quality/case-origin.ts`; review-raised issues use it only for a form item key and nothing groups on it), an audit row `QA_ISSUE_RAISED_FROM_CASE` (`deductionCreated: false`) and an internal case note linking to `/v2/admin/quality?tab=issues&q=<issueId>` (the Issues search now also matches an exact issue id). No pay adjustment, review, score or auto-score is touched; any deduction stays the existing "Propose deduction" on the issue. Like every QA issue it counts in the cleaner's quality figures (issue counts, categories, patterns; a Critical issue breaks their streak) — the dialog says so, and that selected photos and the description are shown to the cleaner with the issue. Idempotent: a client key per draft replays the first answer; without one, the same case, cleaner and category within 2 minutes return the existing issue; both checks run under an advisory lock. The Quality hub's Issues table has a "Raised from" column ("Raised from case" links back to the case, otherwise "QA review") and the issue detail shows the same.

**Laundry nudges (run side panel).** "Ask cleaner" and "Request photo" from `lib/laundry/nudge-rules.ts`: Ask cleaner when a run not yet picked up carries NOT_READY or PICKUP_OVERDUE, or the cleaner has not confirmed bags on or after the pickup day with the clean no longer in progress; Request photo only for NO_DROP_PHOTO (there is no "missing pickup photo" exception). Both need a linked job. The confirm names the job's cleaners, the transports and the exact wording (shared with the server); the existing laundry nudge route (ADMIN and OPS_MANAGER) now refuses a second nudge of the same kind for the same run within 30 minutes (409 "Already asked at HH:mm. You can ask again after HH:mm.", organisation timezone) unless it is the same request (same key, fixed per run, kind and 30-minute window). "Chase supplier" is not offered: suppliers have no contact.

**Accounts bulk "Message".** In the bulk bar for ADMIN and for OPS_MANAGER whose communications pack allows POST (`AccountsViewer.canMessageStaff`). The dialog lists, before sending, who is skipped and why by the staff message route's rules (`lib/accounts/message-recipients.ts`: clients and other non-staff logins, disabled accounts; staff roles from `lib/notifications/staff-roles.ts`, shared with the recipient scope), takes subject, body and explicit transports, sends one request to the existing staff message route with one key per draft, and shows each recipient's states plus any server refusal. A failed request keeps the draft and the server's message.

**Cases manual Refresh (parity).** The list header has Refresh again: it re-reads the list and queue/type counts (aborting an older read; a late answer for an older query is ignored) and the open case and its linked facts in the background (`useCaseDetail().refresh`; the case stays on screen, a late answer for another case is ignored). The 30 s and focus refresh stay.

**Command.** The work queue has no late-job rows (late jobs are a Live ops exception), so only the continuation decline is shared there.

Owner questions: whether operations managers may pause a clock (route refuses them now) or send the client delay notice; whether a Request photo for a missing drop-off photo should go to the laundry driver rather than the job's cleaners (the route reaches only job cleaners); suppliers still have no contact for "Chase supplier".

## Quality design completion: manual bonus proposal, both row actions, Low / Medium / High (Wave C, 2026-11-11)

Quality half of Wave C in `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md` ("Owner decision, sixth set"). No schema change. Automatic bonus amounts and rules, deductions, scoring and coaching rules are unchanged.

**Propose bonus by hand.** `POST /api/admin/quality/bonus-proposals` (`lib/quality/manual-bonus.ts`, rules in `lib/quality/manual-bonus-rules.ts`) creates one PENDING `CleanerPayAdjustment` for a CLEANER. The schema has no bonus adjustment type (`PayAdjustmentType` is HOURLY | FIXED; bonus provenance lives in `source`), so the row is written exactly as `POST /api/admin/pay-adjustments` writes a pending manual payment (FIXED, STANDALONE, no job or property, SERVICE, taxable, `cleanerNote` "Added by admin.", not reviewed, audit action `PAY_ADJUSTMENT_ADMIN_CREATED`); a test pins the two rows together field for field. That route keeps its write inline, so the service mirrors it rather than calling it. Title "Quality bonus"; `source` stays null (a person proposed it: it lands in the Approvals "Pay requests" queue with other manual pay adjustments, and the payee is told the decision as for any person-raised adjustment); provenance is `sourceKey` `quality-bonus:<cleanerId>:<idempotency key>` and the audit's `origin: "manual quality bonus"`. It is paid only after the existing approval (`PATCH /api/admin/pay-adjustments/[id]`, Approvals or the row's "Review bonus"). Rules added: amount from $5 to $200 in whole cents (`MANUAL_BONUS_MIN_AMOUNT` / `MANUAL_BONUS_MAX_AMOUNT`; larger payments stay on Pay adjustments), reason required (3–1000 characters, stored in the private `adminNote` with the basis); the request cannot carry approval fields (strict schema). The period and figures it relates to are re-read on the server from the Cleaners-tab service for the hub period sent (`period`/`from`/`to`; unknown periods 400) and recorded in `adminNote` and the audit ("Last 30 days: 31 cleans, 28 reviewed, average score 94, current streak 12."). Idempotency: the client key per dialog is kept across a failed attempt; replay returns the first row (200, `duplicate: true`), under a per-cleaner advisory lock. A bonus already pending for the cleaner (automatic STREAK_5 / STREAK_10 / MONTHLY_RANK_1/2 or manual) is refused with 409 and the list unless `acknowledgePending: true`; the dialog shows the warning with "Review it" (the row's Review bonus dialog, or Approvals when the viewer cannot decide) and a "Propose another bonus anyway" tick. Access: ADMIN; OPS_MANAGER only with payroll at manage (who may create a pay adjustment today) and quality at manage (this path's pack); refused while impersonating; the hub hides the action where the server would refuse (`qualityHubAccess().canProposeBonus`).

**Pending bonuses on the row.** `lib/quality/cleaner-signals.ts` reads pending automatic and manual bonuses (`pendingBonusWhere`), each tagged `origin`. "Review bonus" covers both; the streak cell's "bonus due" is shown only for an automatic proposal (the real streak / ranking rule).

**Both row actions.** `components/v2/admin/quality/hub/cleaner-row-actions.ts`: one visible button per row, in the order Start coaching (filled, existing pattern rule), Review bonus, Propose bonus (outlined, active cleaners only); every other action that applies is in the row's "more" menu (`ERowMenu`). So a coaching row also reaches Review bonus / Propose bonus, and a row with a pending bonus still reaches Propose bonus (with the warning). With the propose permission every active row shows an action (the drawn sample leaves some rows blank).

**Severity Low / Medium / High.** `lib/quality/severity.ts` is the one mapping: MINOR → Low, MAJOR → Medium, CRITICAL → High (count pills "2 low", "1 med", "2 high"); the enum has exactly these three values and an unknown value reads as its own text. Used by the Cleaners, Inspectors and Categories pills, the Issues column and Severity filter (option values and `?severity=` stay MINOR / MAJOR / CRITICAL), the expanded row, the issue detail, the "Issues raised" card ("N high"), the Cases "Raise QA issue" dialog (option values unchanged) and its internal case note. The Quality CSV keeps "Severity" (stored value) and adds "Severity label". APIs, stored values, filters and the cleaner and QA inspector portals are unchanged.

**Design comparison (Quality.dc.html).** Matches: header, period chips and Export; the five stat cards' wording; tab order and labels; filter row (Property, Category, Has open fixes, "All tabs share the period and these filters."); Cleaners columns and order; severity pills; streak cells; expanded row (open issues with pill, meta, Fix pending N h, Mark fixed / Propose deduction or Reject / Confirm −N; By category bars; Open all N in Issues). The expanded row's date is now "6 Nov" (year added outside the current year). Deliberate differences: the search placeholder reads "Search cleaner, property or job" because the shared search matches all three; rows show "Propose bonus" wherever a proposal is allowed; the footnote stating the cleans and score basis and the Laundry statistics link are extra.

## Forms design completion: Compare, Import/Export, Table, Stock count, effects, Monthly (Wave B, 2026-11-12)

Wave B of the owner's sixth decision set. No database schema change: everything lives in the form schema JSON, the app settings JSON and existing tables.

**Library (`/v2/admin/forms`, Job forms tab).**
- *List | Compare* toggle (right of the filters, as drawn). Compare takes the 2–4 ticked forms and shows them side by side by section and question, computed on the server by `GET /api/admin/form-templates/compare` (`lib/forms/template-compare.ts`, pure; ADMIN and OPS_MANAGER read). Questions match by the library's existing rule (section title + label + type, whitespace and case ignored); a question asked twice in one form is shown as "Asked more than once" and not compared. Differences are relative to the first form and written out: Added, Missing, "Changed: Required, How often" with both values. "Only differences" filter. Each form is compared as it stands (its draft when it has one; the column says draft or live). From a difference: "Copy this question to N forms missing it" (bulk `insert-questions`) and "Copy “setting” from the first form" for the bulk-editable settings (required, help text, minimum photos, maximum files, priority, details when No; bulk `edit-question`). Both preview first and save drafts. Other settings (choices, rules, how often, tables, effects) are changed in the builder, as the screen says.
- *Export* in the bulk bar and the row menu: `GET /api/admin/form-templates/export?ids=` (ADMIN) downloads `{ format: "sneek.form-templates", formatVersion: 1, exportedAt, forms: [{ name, serviceType, tags, source, sourceVersion, schema }] }` — no template, group or user ids; presigned links (logo, reference media with a storage key) are removed, storage keys kept (`lib/forms/template-transfer.ts`).
- *Import* in the page header: `POST /api/admin/form-templates/import[?dryRun=1]` (ADMIN, body = the file, ≤ 1 MB, ≤ 20 forms, ≤ 500 questions per form). Format and version must match; name, service type and tags are checked; each schema runs the builder's strict save check, so an unknown question type is refused per form with the reason. Preview, then "Create N drafts": new rows only (kind CUSTOM, next version, `isActive: false`, de-duplicated name "(imported)", audit `FORM_TEMPLATE_IMPORTED`). Never overwrites, never publishes. Question and section ids are kept and renamed only when they collide inside one form; conditions keep pointing at the question that kept the id.
- *Avg time and Completion sort* over the whole filtered set: `lib/forms/library-list.ts` counts the figure submissions in the database first (`countFigureSubmissions`); up to `FIGURES_MAX_SUBMISSIONS` (20,000) the figures are read for every filtered form, so sorting is right across pages and "–" rows (too little data) sort last in both directions. Above the cap the two sorts are refused with a reason (400) and other sorts fall back to page-only figures (`checksBasis: "page"`).
- *Never answered inside Checks*: the usage check's questions (same thresholds and exclusions: 90 days, ≥ 20 submissions, always-shown required questions, since the live version) are folded into each row's Checks as warnings (rule `never-answered`, `withUsageWarnings`). The Checks cell shows errors and warnings as separate pills and the never-answered line marked as a warning. The chip reads "Has problems: errors or warnings N" (or "check errors or warnings" with a tooltip when the usage check could only cover the page).

**Builder.**
- *Table* (`table`): columns with label, cell type (text, number, yes/no, choice with choices), required per column; fixed rows (named) or "cleaner can add rows" with minimum/maximum (≤ 8 columns, ≤ 50 rows). Answer: array of row objects keyed by column id. Phone: one stacked card per row, every cell labelled "Row N: Column" (`components/v2/cleaner/structured-field-inputs.tsx`). Validation `lib/forms/structured-answers.ts` (types, unknown columns, row limits, required cells), enforced by the submit route from the start (rule `table`, not an extended warning — no draft could predate the type). Reports: the client report renders a real table (`ReportFieldVM.kind = "table"`, `lib/reports/estate-template.ts`); every other office view uses `formatFieldValue`, one line per row.
- *Stock count* (`stock_count`): the item is chosen from the stock catalogue in the properties panel (`stockItemId`, name and unit copied onto the question). Answer `{ itemId, count }`, a whole number 0–100,000 for the question's own item (rule `stock-count`, enforced). The count is stored on the submission and listed in `__formEffects.stockCounts`. It does NOT overwrite on-hand by itself: the submission's only stock write is the existing usage deduction (`deductJobStockOnce`), and turning a count into usage would invent a quantity; a count is applied to on-hand only through the urgent stock observation rule when it raises a need (below). Two questions on the same item are refused on save and shown as a check error; at runtime the first visible one counts.
- *Question effects* (`FieldEffect` in `lib/forms/types.ts`, strict-checked in `lib/forms/structured-schema-checks.ts`, badges and wording in `lib/forms/form-effects.ts`):
  - Stock count "Raises an urgent stock need below N": the submit route decides inside the submission transaction (`lib/forms/submission-effects.ts`) and puts the counts below N on the durable follow-up receipt (`lib/cleaner/submission-followups.ts`, stage `URGENT_STOCK`). After commit `lib/cleaner/submission-urgent-stock.ts` calls the existing urgent stock report through the additive `reportUrgentStockFromSubmission` (reporter = the cleaner, scoped by the submission because the job is SUBMITTED by then; request id derived from submission + item, so once per submission and item). The count goes as the observation, applied to on-hand only under the urgent-stock rule. A failure is kept on the receipt (`urgentStockErrors`), retried every 5 minutes and told to the office once by an inbox notice (`submission.urgent_stock_review`); it never blocks or undoes the submission. Items the property does not stock are recorded as skipped. Nothing is ordered. The need does not yet store the job (Wave F adds that column); the note names the submission.
  - Yes / No "'Yes' (or 'No') opens a damage report": on the phone the answer opens the job's existing damage report (`DamageReportForm`, new `prefill` prop) with the area (effect area → location tag → section) and category filled in. On submit, if the cleaner neither submitted a damage report for the job nor sent damage items with the form, the route adds one item to its existing damage loop — the same private DAMAGE case (`clientVisible: false`) it opens for reported damage. Outcome recorded in `__formEffects.damage`.
  - "Test as cleaner" lists "Would raise an urgent stock need …" / "Would open a damage report …" from `runFormTest().wouldHappen`; the sandbox opens, raises and writes nothing.
- *Monthly* under How often (`frequency: "MONTHLY"`): shown on the first counted clean of each calendar month at the property in the organisation timezone. Uses the existing `PropertyRotationState.lastCompletedAt` of the question's key as the last-shown date (no schema change): `filterRotationalSchema` hides it when that date is in the current month (`isMonthlyItemDue`, `formatInTimeZone`), and `applyJobRotationCompletion` stamps it (dated by the submission time, not the approval) via `recordMonthlyShown`, leaving `cleansSinceDone` alone. Every-Nth questions rotate exactly as before; the resolver adds `lastCompletedAt` to its read only for forms with a monthly question. Two cleans in one month before the first is approved can both show it.
- *Rename in place*: the h1 name has a "Rename form" button (or click the name); Enter or leaving the box saves, Escape cancels; still one h1. Saves through the existing metadata PATCH with `expectedUpdatedAt` = the template row's token (409 → nothing overwritten, typed name kept), then sets the draft name too (without creating a draft when there is none) (`use-inline-rename.ts`).

**Extended validation switch (owner decision, Wave B addition).** `ENFORCE_EXTENDED_FORM_VALIDATION` is now the default of `settings.formValidation = { enforceExtended, enforcedSince }` (app settings JSON; unreadable or "on without a date" reads as off). Results and stats tab: "Reject submissions that break these checks" with the checks listed and real counts (`GET /api/admin/form-templates/validation-setting`: submissions with warnings in the last 14 / 30 days, total and all submissions, by check, by form, the latest 20 linked to their jobs; "–" when unreadable). ADMIN switches it (`PUT`, confirm step, audit `FORM_VALIDATION_ENFORCEMENT`). When on, the submit route rejects and the phone shows the checks inline (`GET /api/cleaner/jobs/[id]/form-validation` gives the phone the same decision) — except for a job the cleaner first clocked in on before the switch-on moment (earliest `TimeLog.startedAt`, a server timestamp every submission has; the client's own start time is not trusted): accepted with warnings. No clock-in found → warnings only.

## Finance decisions: one invoiced rule, per-client terms, invoiced revenue, monthly email, Xero receipts and sign-in (2026-11-12)

Owner decisions of 2026-11-11 (second set 2–6; third set 4, 6, 8) plus two existing problems. Migration `20261112090000_finance_decisions`.

**One invoiced rule (`lib/invoicing/invoiced-job-rule.ts`).** A job is invoiced only while it has a line on a LIVE invoice: kind INVOICE, status not VOID, and not fully credited (`ClientInvoice.creditedInFullAt` null). Drafts count (billing in progress). The INVOICED job status on its own no longer counts anywhere. One module is used by the generator (`billedJobLinesWhere` and its in-transaction recheck), "Ready to bill", the Jobs Invoice filter and "Not invoiced" view count (`lib/jobs/invoiced-rule.ts` re-exports it) and the job detail Money tab; `tests/lib/jobs-invoiced-rule.test.ts` pins them together.
- *Fully credited*: approved/sent credit notes (`creditedAmountOf`) ≥ the invoice total (half-cent tolerance). Kept in `creditedInFullAt`, recomputed in the same transaction whenever a credit note's status changes (`refreshCreditCoverage`: PATCH, bulk, send, reconciled void). A partial credit leaves the job invoiced.
- *Returning to billable*: when an invoice is voided (standard or reconciled void), a draft is deleted, or it becomes fully credited, each job on it that is marked INVOICED and has no other live line goes back to the status before it was marked invoiced (latest audit row recording `before.status` → INVOICED), else COMPLETED, in the same transaction, with a `JOB_RETURNED_TO_BILLABLE` audit row ("Returned to billable: invoice INV-… voided"). Nothing sets INVOICED automatically today (only the manual status change), so this mostly affects manually marked jobs. Reissuing bills the job again through the generator. No invoice's lines, totals, number or payments are touched.
- *Voiding a credit note* that would make its invoice bill its jobs again is refused when one of those jobs has since been billed on another live invoice (it would bill the work twice).
- *Legacy repair*: the migration returns jobs marked INVOICED with no live line to COMPLETED and records the ids and count in AppSetting `migration_20261112_legacy_invoiced_repair` (no audit row: it is not a person's action).

**Delete and reset (owner: "it shouldn't").** Job delete (`lib/jobs/delete-financial-guard.ts`), QA reset and the option reset count only lines on non-void invoices (`FINANCIAL_RECORD_LINE_WHERE`); a fully credited but not void invoice still blocks (it is an issued record). `ClientInvoiceLine.jobId` is `ON DELETE SET NULL`, so deleting a job keeps a voided invoice's line with its stored description and amount (no FK migration needed). Fixed with it: the option reset only locked evidence on status INVOICED; it now also locks when the job has a line on a non-void invoice.

**Invoiced this month.** Totals (incl. GST, as issued) of INVOICE documents first sent to the client in the calendar month (organisation timezone, `sentAt`), less CREDIT_NOTE documents sent in the month; VOID excluded; approved-but-unsent is not invoiced (`lib/billing/client-invoice-stats.ts`). Shown on Command's Money this month beside Paid this month and Outstanding, and as a card in the client-invoice stats strip. `sentAt` is now the FIRST send: PATCH to SENT and a (re-)send never overwrite it; re-sends are recorded in `metadata.resentAt[]` and as their own History row ("Sent to the client again"). Past rows already restamped by a re-send cannot be repaired (the first time was not kept).

**Payment terms and due dates.** `Client.paymentTermsDays` (0–120, null = organisation default) on the Client 360 page ("Payment terms" card, `PATCH /api/admin/clients/[id]/payment-terms`, audited). Who: ADMIN; an OPS_MANAGER only with finance at "manage" (the right that changes invoices). The rule (`effectivePaymentTermsDays` in `lib/billing/invoice-due.ts`): an issued invoice uses `ClientInvoice.paymentTermsDays`, snapshotted when it first becomes SENT/PART_PAID/PAID/VOID; a draft or approved invoice uses the client's terms, else the default. The due day stays created + terms in the organisation timezone. Used by the PDF (and so the client portal), the list, the overdue filter/chip/stat (overdue ids resolved in SQL per invoice), the editor, "Send reminder", Insights days-to-pay context and the slow-payer finding (which compares with the terms the client's paid invoices carried). The migration backfilled every already-issued invoice with the organisation default of the time, so no issued due date moved.

**Insights revenue.** A clean's revenue is its invoiced amount when invoiced under the one rule: line totals EX GST, net of approved/sent credit notes spread in proportion over the invoice's job lines (`lib/insights/invoiced-revenue.ts`), attributed to the clean's date. A clean not invoiced uses the scheduled charge (`computeClientCharge`), shown separately as "Not yet invoiced (at current rates)". Cost carries no GST, so margin compares like with like. Cards show the split under Revenue; tables show "$… not yet invoiced" under the figure; the CSVs have Invoiced and Not yet invoiced columns/rows. An old period's invoiced revenue does not move when rates change. Invoice-level discount lines are not spread over jobs.

**Finance summary: no silent default rate.** `lib/finance/summary.ts` (the per-client profit card and `/api/admin/finance/summary`) leaves a clean out of cleaner cost when any payee has no known rate (`payRateUnknown`, the same test Insights uses), counts it (`uncosted.noPayRate`, per client `uncostedNoPayRate`) and the card says "N cleans not costed: no pay rate set" with a link to Accounts → Cleaners → "No pay rate" (`?rate=none`, filters on User.hourlyRate). The default rate still applies, unchanged, in `computeCleanerPay` and therefore in payroll runs, cleaner invoices, payouts, the payroll summary on Command and the job pay summary (they flag `rateMissing`).

**Monthly Insights email.** Settings → Notifications → "Monthly Insights email" (ADMIN): off by default; recipients picked from active administrators and operations managers with Insights access (re-checked at send time; no free-text addresses); day 1–28 and hour in the organisation timezone; sections Money / Work / Quality / Worth knowing. Stored in AppSetting `insightsMonthlyEmail`. Worker job `insights-monthly-email` (hourly at :35, Sydney) sends the previous month's Business summary built by `getBusinessInsights` per recipient's finance access (no money figures without it) through `sendOfficeNotification` with the EMAIL transport only and the STAFF_MEMBER scope; with `SNEEK_NOTIFICATION_OUTBOX_TRANSPORTS` lacking EMAIL it uses the documented direct fallback and records it in the delivery log; a transport switched off for the type is not sent. Once per month: AppSetting `insightsMonthlyEmailSent:YYYY-MM` is created before sending (a restart cannot send twice; a crash mid-send leaves it "sending", never resent automatically) and records each recipient's state. "Send me this month's so far" sends to the signed-in administrator only. Catalog entry `insights.monthly_email`, email kind `insights_monthly`. The Insights header shows "Email monthly: on/off", linking to the setting for administrators.

**Xero.** *Receipts*: a payment read from Xero sends the client the same receipt as "Record payment" (`lib/billing/client-payment-receipt.ts`), once per Xero payment id (only when newly recorded), never while the invoice is left as needs review (recorded as withheld), and a failed email never undoes the payment; each outcome is a History row. *System actor*: scheduled checks are still stored under the earliest active administrator (AuditLog needs a user) but carry `after.systemActor = "SYSTEM_XERO_SYNC"` and are shown as "System: Xero sync (scheduled)" in invoice History, both Activity pages (name, no email) and the payment ledger; a person's activity list omits them. *Sign-in*: the OAuth request now includes `offline_access`, so Xero issues a refresh token and the existing refresh code keeps the connection signed in; a refused refresh is recorded. Settings → Xero says "Reconnect Xero once to keep it signed in" (no refresh token) or "Xero sign-in lapsed — reconnect", with the button; the Finance header badge and Notifications → Health say the same. `XERO_REQUEST_OFFLINE_ACCESS=false` connects without it if a Xero app ever refuses the scope. **One-time owner action after deploy: Settings → Xero → Reconnect Xero (any admin, once).** Mocked only; not exercised against real Xero.

**Classic screens.** `/admin/cleaner-invoices` redirects to `/v2/admin/finance?tab=cleaner-invoices` (keeping its query; same roles and payroll pack), so Submitted invoices can be approved. Classic `/admin/finance?tab=invoices` redirects to the Invoices and pay client-invoice tab, whose actions follow the step (c) rules; the classic Overview and Payroll tabs stay.

## Insights design completion: money per clean, preview sorted in place, client chip, margin bands (Wave C, 2026-11-12)

Insights half of Wave C ("Owner decision, sixth set") in `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`. Read-only analytics; no schema, money rule or access rule changed. Supersedes, for these points, the "Insights" section above (its "Not built: Email monthly" note was already superseded by "Finance decisions").

**Overrun finding states the money effect per clean.** For a property flagged by the overrun detector, the detail adds the drawn sentence "At the current rate this property makes $N per clean." ("loses" when negative). The figure is `MoneyFigures.marginPerClean` (`lib/insights/aggregate.ts`): (revenue − cleaner cost) ÷ cleans over exactly the margin's basis (cleans where both the charge — invoiced, or the scheduled charge where not yet invoiced — and recorded pay are known), rounded to cents with `roundCents`; the only new arithmetic is that division. The finding's basis line carries the count ("over N cleans with both"). Below the row minimum (`MIN_BASIS_ROW`, 3) there is no number: "Money per clean: not enough data." with the count. Without finance access the measure is never computed and the sentence is absent from the API payload (and so from the monthly email built for that recipient), not hidden in the browser.

**Five-row preview sorted in place.** Header clicks on "Properties, least profitable first" put the order in the URL (`sort`/`dir` on the Business tab) and read `GET /api/admin/insights/properties?…&sort&dir&page=1&pageSize=5`, i.e. the first five of the whole property set in that order on the server; the Business figures are not reloaded. One ordering module (`lib/insights/property-order.ts`) serves the Properties tab, its CSV and the preview, so the preview's five are the full tab's first five. "All N properties" opens the Properties tab in the preview's current order. Defaults: margin lowest first with finance; without finance, actual ÷ planned highest first — a new non-financial sort key `ratio` with an "Actual vs planned" column on the Properties tab, so the link no longer drops the order (it previously opened name order). Time and cost's "All N properties" now also opens `ratio` highest first (same fix). While a new order loads the previous rows stay (busy); a failed read shows the table's error with retry; an order naming a column the viewer does not have is ignored.

**Client filter chip.** `components/v2/admin/insights/client-filter.tsx`: the shared `EFilterPopover` (new optional `emptyValue`, so the idle chip reads "Client: all" as drawn without looking active), with a search box and the database client list (`/api/admin/insights/options`); ArrowDown from the search enters the list, arrows / Home / End move, Enter or Space chooses, Escape closes and returns focus. Loading and failure states; on failure a chosen client can still be cleared. The native select is gone.

**Margin badge bands** (`lib/insights/margin-band.ts`): danger at or below `MARGIN_DANGER_MAX_PCT` (= the low-margin finding floor, 15), warning below `MARGIN_WARNING_BELOW_PCT` (30), neutral below `MARGIN_SUCCESS_FROM_PCT` (45), success at or above it. The percentage is always the badge text; the tooltip names the band and the basis.

**Element-by-element pass against `Insights.dc.html`.** Made to match: header order period chips → Client chip → Export → Email monthly; "Email monthly" is a header button with its real state ("On"/"Off") that opens Settings → Monthly Insights email for an administrator (owner-only setting), and for anyone else is an inert button with the state and "An administrator sets this up in Settings"; nothing when the state could not be read. Subtitle names a whole previous month in the same year without the year ("compared with September"). Findings strip: eyebrow size/tracking as drawn, three findings side by side from md, each with its inline link. Figure cards: the definition sentences moved out of the cards into one "How these figures are worked out" disclosure under the row; cards show eyebrow, serif figure (28px), change badge, and the basis count. Week chart: title and the two-badge legend (Revenue solid primary, Cleaner cost gold) on one header row; bars solid gold as drawn (the earlier hatch is removed; the series are told apart by fixed order, legend text, per-bar text and the table). Actual hours turn danger above 110% of planned (as drawn: 3.3 of 3.0 is not red, 3.6 is). Header controls stay hidden on the Client report PDFs tab: period, client and export do not apply to that manager, so showing them there would be untrue.

**Deliberate differences kept** (honesty or accessibility): each finding's basis line and each card's basis count ("212 of 240 cleans; 28 excluded: …"), the revenue card's invoiced / not-yet-invoiced split line, the chart's basis note and "Show as a table" disclosure, "Not enough data" / "No comparable earlier data" in place of numbers and badges, the non-finance variant of the preview ("most over planned time first", "Margin needs finance access") and the finance-access note at the foot of the page. The design's "Ideas" box is a designer note, not product.

**Tests.** `tests/lib/insights-design-completion.test.ts` (per-clean basis and exclusions, not-enough-data, loses/makes, omission without finance, margin bands, the true top five for every sort and direction, no-margin rows last, comparison label), `tests/api/insights-routes.test.ts` (money sentence for admin, absent for an operations manager without finance, preview top five through the properties route), `tests/components/insights-header-controls.test.tsx` (client chip search and keyboard, failure, Email monthly states, preview order guard).

## Money design completion: ready-to-bill dollars, batch drafts, Xero push failures, PDF archive, owing sort, draft fields, autosave, approval rules (Wave D, 2026-11-12)

Wave D ("Owner decision, sixth set") in `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`. **No schema change and no migration**: everything new is stored in existing columns, invoice metadata, AppSetting JSON or AuditLog rows. No issued invoice's lines, totals, number or payments are edited; no charge or pay changes; route role lists and the finance ops pack (prefix `/api/admin/invoices`) are unchanged. Supersedes, for these points, the "Invoices and pay", "Invoice editor page" and "Xero status and payment pull" sections above.

**List.**
- *Ready to bill* (`lib/billing/ready-to-bill.ts`): the finished jobs the card already counted (COMPLETED/INVOICED, not skipped, on no live invoice under the one invoiced rule) with the sum of `computeClientCharge` per client, using the generator's own rate tables (the client's active property rates, active price book), summed in whole cents. Jobs with no rate are excluded from the dollar figure and counted: "$3,140 · 22 finished jobs not on an invoice, 4 clients · 3 jobs have no rate (not in the total)". Shopping charges and client-paid repairs the generator also bills are not in this figure.
- *Create N drafts* (`POST /api/admin/invoices/generate-batch`, `lib/billing/client-invoice-batch-generate.ts`, dialog `batch-drafts-dialog.tsx`): runs the existing `generateClientInvoice` once per client of the card, sequentially, with the single generator's period / bill-by basis / GST and `completedOnly` (finished work only, as the card counts). Per-client outcome: created (INV- number, link), refused with the generator's own reason (missing rates, invoiced by another request), nothing billable in the period, or failed. Nothing is approved, sent or pushed. Idempotent per request key: a RUNNING then a FINISHED AuditLog row (`CLIENT_INVOICE_BATCH_DRAFTS`, entity id `batch:<key>`) under an advisory lock; the same key replays the stored outcomes, a key still running answers 409, a key reused with other options answers 409. The dialog makes one key per opening and keeps it (options frozen) for Retry after a lost answer. Capped at 200 clients per run. Each created draft gets a History row.
- *"N missing rates" on Drafts* (`lib/billing/missing-rates.ts`): the generator refuses a draft while any billable job has no rate, so a draft can only hold an unrated job when the rate (or fixed price) was removed after generation or the line came another way. The figure is therefore job lines whose job resolves no client rate NOW, by the same function and tables; the card shows the job count with "N drafts blocked by missing rates", and a draft row shows "N jobs without a rate" with "Fix and review" as its action. Approval refuses those drafts (below).
- *Xero column*: a failed push is recorded distinctly. After the reservation is written, any failure sets `metadata.xeroExportState = "FAILED"` with `metadata.xeroExportFailure = { reason, at }` (provider message sanitised: one line, bearer tokens and long secrets redacted, at most 300 characters; `lib/billing/client-invoice-xero-failure.ts`) under the row lock; the reservation keeps the row locked against edits (`isXeroExportPending` covers PENDING and FAILED, also in DELETE, reconcile and the started-work review route) and Retry Xero reuses the same provider idempotency key; a successful retry sets EXPORTED and clears the failure. States everywhere (`lib/billing/client-invoice-xero-display.ts`: list cell, filter, editor card, stage bar): Not in Xero ("Not sent" on an unsent draft/approved row), Push pending (in flight or cut off), Push failed (reason and time), In Xero · not yet checked, Synced (last successful read agreed), Synced · paid in Xero, Needs review, State unknown. Filter values `failed` and `unchecked` added; needs-action includes failed.
- *Due* for DRAFT/APPROVED rows reads "On send · N-day terms" (the provisional day in the tooltip): the terms are fixed at first send. The admin PDF preview (and the PDF archive) of an unsent invoice prints "Due (provisional): … · N-day terms, fixed when sent"; the emailed PDF, the client portal PDF and documents never do. The due day itself is unchanged: created + terms in the organisation timezone.
- *Download PDFs* (bulk; `POST /api/admin/invoices/pdf-archive`, `lib/billing/client-invoice-pdf-archive.ts`, `lib/billing/zip-store.ts`): one ZIP of the selected invoices' PDFs rendered with the existing renderer, files named by number (`INV-0421.pdf`, duplicates suffixed), at most 50 invoices and 60 MB of PDF data (later rows listed as not included, never dropped). A row that cannot be rendered is listed in `_errors.txt` inside the archive and in the `X-Archive-Summary` response header, which the bulk bar shows. No zip dependency existed, so a STORE-only writer (CRC-32, central directory, UTF-8 names, no ZIP64) was added; a test opens it with `unzip -t`. The body is streamed from the rendered chunks.
- *Owing sortable*: computed in SQL, not stored (`lib/billing/client-invoice-owing-sql.ts`): `max(0, total − paidAmount − round2(Σ|total| of APPROVED/SENT credit notes))`, 0 on a credit note, mirroring `invoiceAmountOwing`/`creditedAmountOf` exactly; `paidAmount` is the cumulative column every payment path keeps and credits are rows, so SQL is reliable. Sorting takes the ids of the whole filtered set (refused above 20,000 with a "narrow the filters" message), orders them in one SQL `ORDER BY owing, id`, then reads the page. A database test compares the SQL figure with the TypeScript function over 40 fixtures and checks the list order. The CSV export uses the same ordering. The CSV writer moved to `lib/billing/client-invoice-csv.ts` (re-exported); its Xero state column reads FAILED for a failed push.

**Editor.**
- *Bill to, Period, Due on a DRAFT* (PATCH fields `clientId`, `period: { start, end }`, `paymentTermsDays`; `lib/billing/client-invoice-draft-fields.ts`, `editor-draft-fields.tsx`). Read only once approved or later (refused on the server). Bill to: a draft with job lines moves only when every job belongs to a property of the new client; a manual line grouped under a property, a shopping allocation or a started-work snapshot ties the draft to its client; both clients' billing locks are taken and the jobs re-checked against live invoices; a credit note's Bill to never changes. Period: two calendar days stored as Sydney day boundaries like the generator; never adds or removes lines; the response warns when job lines are scheduled outside it. Due: "Client terms (N days)" or an invoice-specific override stored in `paymentTermsDays` (0–365), which `termsSnapshotFor` already never overwrites at first send; shown as provisional until sent. Each change is a content edit (content locks apply) with a `CLIENT_INVOICE_DRAFT_FIELDS` History row (before values kept).
- *Add finished jobs (N not billed)* (`GET/POST /api/admin/invoices/[id]/jobs`, `lib/billing/client-invoice-add-jobs.ts`, `add-jobs-dialog.tsx`): N is the real count of the client's finished jobs on no live invoice. The picker shows each job's generator charge (or "No rate: set one first", not selectable) and marks jobs outside the period. Adding runs under the invoice row lock and the client's billing lock, re-reads every job, refuses the whole request when a job is no longer finished/unbilled/the client's or has no rate, checks holiday-rate snapshots like the generator, builds lines with the generator's own builder (extracted to `lib/billing/invoice-job-lines.ts`, used by both) and recomputes totals with the shared totals function; drafts only; not on a credit note or a started-work draft; carries the stale-write guard; History row `CLIENT_INVOICE_JOBS_ADDED`.
- *Send options* (send route fields `includePaymentLink`, `pushToXero`; defaults are today's behaviour). Attach PDF stays always on. Include payment link: offered only when the existing client payment-link route could work (Stripe on in Integrations and a Stripe key on the server; never on a credit note; `lib/billing/invoice-payment-link.ts`), otherwise disabled with the reason; the email gets a link to the client portal's invoices page, whose existing Pay now opens a checkout for what is owed (a checkout session is not emailed: it expires and the amount can change). Requested but unavailable → 409 and nothing is sent. Push to Xero when sent: after a successful send only, the existing idempotent push runs; a push failure never fails or rolls back the send, is returned beside the send (toast) and recorded as a History row and as the FAILED state above.
- *Autosave* (`use-invoice-editor.ts`). The PATCH accepts `expectedUpdatedAt`; on mismatch it writes nothing and answers 409 `{ code: "STALE_INVOICE", currentUpdatedAt }` (`lib/billing/client-invoice-stale-guard.ts`); callers that send none keep working (bulk, Xero sync, other screens). The editor sends the token on every write, autosaves typed line edits 1.5 s after the last keystroke, one save at a time, never while a line is invalid, and does not repeat a failed autosave until the drafts change or Retry is pressed. States: Saving… / Saved N seconds ago / Unsaved changes / Not saved / Not saved: changed elsewhere, with a conflict banner "Reload theirs" / "Keep my edits and retry" (re-reads the version, then sends the same absolute values). Approve, send and the other stored-invoice actions stay disabled until saved; the leave-page warning is on only while something is unsaved or saving. Typed text stays usable during a background autosave (what is typed then stays a draft); every other control waits.
- *Add-line box* (`add-line-row.tsx`, `use-line-suggestions.ts`): typing offers the client's unbilled finished jobs (picked → added through Add finished jobs) and saved items (picked → fills description and rate). Keyboard combobox; the list sits in the flow so the scrolling table never clips it.
- *Saved invoice items*: no existing catalogue fits (the price book is per job type and size; property rates are per property and job type), so a small list (name, default rate, GST flag) is stored in AppSetting `invoice-saved-items`, managed under Billing schedule → "Saved invoice items" (lazy disclosure), `GET/PUT /api/admin/invoices/saved-items` (stale saves refused, audited `INVOICE_SAVED_ITEMS_UPDATED`). The GST flag changes no arithmetic: GST is per invoice, so the editor shows a note when the item's flag differs from the invoice's GST switch, and the switch decides.
- *Xero card*: the sales account's name is read from Xero's chart of accounts when connected (`readXeroAccountName` in `lib/xero/client.ts`, cached 15 minutes, failures 2 minutes; `lib/billing/xero-account-name.ts`); when it cannot be read the card shows the code, "name unavailable" and the reason, never a guess. This needs the `accounting.settings.read` scope, now requested (opt out with `XERO_REQUEST_SETTINGS_READ=false`); it takes effect after the one Xero reconnect already required. The card says payments recorded in Xero come back automatically (hourly) and shows the last check time and state.
- *"Before you approve" enforced on the server* (owner-approved rule change; `lib/billing/client-invoice-approval-rules.ts`). A DRAFT is not approved, sent directly or marked paid while (a) a job line's job resolves no client rate now or a line amount is not a real number, (b) a job on it is on another live invoice (any client) or twice on it, (c) its stored total does not match its lines, or any of these cannot be checked; (d) started-work review was already enforced. Checked on the lines as they are after the request's own edits, under the client's billing advisory lock (the generator's). The refusal text is built from the checklist items, so the editor ("OK" / "Blocks approval" with the server's words and a link to fix: client rates page, the other invoice, the started-work panel), the PATCH, the send route and bulk approve state the same reason. Invoices already APPROVED or later are not affected: there the same checks read "Check" (advice). `invoiceTotalsMismatch` moved to `lib/billing/invoice-totals-check.ts` (re-exported from `client-invoices.ts`).

**History rows added:** `CLIENT_INVOICE_BATCH_CREATED`, `CLIENT_INVOICE_JOBS_ADDED`, `CLIENT_INVOICE_DRAFT_FIELDS`, `CLIENT_INVOICE_SEND_OPTIONS` (payment link included; push after send outcome).

**Not done / limits.** Nothing was run against real Xero, Stripe or email (mocked). The payment-link availability does not check whether the client's portal has the finance module switched on (a client without it would be refused by the portal). The batch run happens inside one request (200 clients at most); a crash mid-run leaves its key "still running" (open the dialog again for a new key; drafts already created are kept and reported by the generator's "already on a non-void invoice" wording). Sending a draft checks the approval rules in their own transaction just before the email, not in the same transaction as the sent stamp.

**Tests.** `tests/db/money-wave-d.test.ts` (PostgreSQL: owing parity and order, add jobs, Bill to rules, approval refuses double billing, batch idempotency and outcomes, failed push recorded/listed/retried with the same key), `tests/lib/money-wave-d.test.ts`, `tests/lib/money-wave-d-send.test.ts`, `tests/components/money-wave-d.test.tsx`; existing tests updated where they encoded the old behaviour (listed in the review record).

## Wave E without a database change: repair quote from a case, Chase supplier, document reminder, Tell client, Forms submissions, monthly once (2026-11-12)

Wave E ("Owner decision, sixth set") items that need **no schema change and no migration** (`docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`). Everything new is stored in existing columns, QuoteEvent / LostFoundEvent / AuditLog rows or AppSetting receipts. Items that need a column (laundry pickup/drop clock times, charge from a case, cancel job, leave, effective-dated pay rates, required documents, "last seen", QA template snapshots, stock count to on-hand) stay for the schema step.

**Cases: Create repair quote, pre-filled (2026-11).** "Create repair quote" opens `/v2/admin/quotes/new?fromCase=<caseId>`. The page re-reads the case on the server (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER as the quotes pages) through `loadCaseQuotePrefill` (`lib/cases/repair-quote.ts`): client (case client, else the property's / job's property's client), service address and suburb from that property, quote heading "Repair: <case title>", the description (case title, job number, case description) as the notes, and the case's image attachments (http(s) URLs only, at most 12) as reference images. A missing case shows a notice and an empty builder. Nothing is sent by pre-filling; the quote is a normal draft. `POST /api/admin/quotes` takes `fromCaseId`, re-checks the case exists, and in the quote's own transaction writes a `FROM_CASE` QuoteEvent `{ caseId, caseTitle, actorId }` and an internal case note "Repair quote Q-XXXXXX created from this case as a draft". The case's Next steps list "Quote Q-… created <time>" with links (`quotes` in `/api/admin/cases/[id]/linked`); the quote page shows "From case <title>" and its timeline "Created from case …". Quote reference images (create and PATCH) now pass the upload-ownership rule: the linked case's own attachments (office-visible case evidence) are allowed, any other new key must be the caller's or an office account's upload (existing problem fixed: the quote routes accepted any key).

**Laundry: Chase supplier (2026-11).** Offered on a run the Pipeline already flags OVER_TURNAROUND (picked up and at the laundry beyond the supplier's `avgTurnaround`, or beyond the maximum days out when none is recorded) that has a supplier (`lib/laundry/supplier-chase-rules.ts`), on the lane card (as drawn) and in the run panel. The dialog loads `GET /api/admin/laundry/[taskId]/supplier-chase`: the drafted message (property, bags, days with the supplier, usual turnaround), the supplier's stored email / phone, per transport whether it can be used and why not (no contact, invalid contact, email key missing, SMS switched off / not configured), the cool-down, and whether this account may send. Copy message, `mailto:` and `tel:` for everyone who can open the run (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER); Send (email and/or SMS) for administrators only, never while impersonating, through `POST /api/admin/notifications/actions/supplier-chase` (`lib/laundry/supplier-chase.ts`). The send re-reads the run and re-applies the rule, sends only to the stored contact (the body cannot name an address; strict schema), one chase per run per 30 minutes (a retry of the same request passes; a failed send does not start the cool-down). Catalog `laundry.supplier_chase`.

**Shared external-recipient path (2026-11).** `lib/notifications/external-recipient.ts` now carries both messages to people without an account (`guest.message`, `laundry.supplier_chase`) with a per-kind policy: audit action and entity, per-record daily and per-sender hourly limits from the audit log plus the in-process limiter, one send per idempotency key (`dispatchNotificationOnce`), a delivery-log row with the provider's truthful state and an audit row with a masked address. `guest-message.ts` keeps its exports and behaviour on top of it.

**Command: Send reminder for an expiring document (2026-11).** The work-queue row offers "Send reminder" (primary) beside "Open account". The confirm (`document-reminder-dialog.tsx`) reads `GET /api/admin/workforce/document-reminder?documentId=` (person, which contacts exist, request title "Renewed <title>", wording, whether a reminder already went today) and sends with explicit transports (in-app notice with phone/browser push, email, SMS) through the workforce route's `REQUEST_DOCUMENT` with `documentId` (`lib/workforce/document-reminder.ts`): a renewal request due on the expiry date, one per document per organisation day (AppSetting receipt `documentReminder:<doc>:<day>`; a retry with the same key replays the recorded result, any other is refused with the first time), refused for an inactive account, a non-staff account, a document without expiry, or while impersonating; audited `STAFF_DOCUMENT_REMINDER_SENT`. Existing problem fixed in `REQUEST_DOCUMENT` itself: it wrote its notice as "SENT" and fired email and SMS without recording the providers' answers or escaping the title. It now delivers through `lib/workforce/document-request-send.ts`: notice and email on the office pipeline (STAFF_MEMBER scope, catalog `workforce.document_request`), SMS through the SMS chokepoint with its answer recorded (`workforce.document_request_sms`); the older Accounts "Request document" keeps its transports (notice, email when an address exists, SMS when a phone exists) and now returns real states. The row is shown only to viewers who may call the workforce route.

**Lost and found: Tell client sent from the app; Property sortable (2026-11).** New pipeline scope `LOST_ITEM_PROPERTY_CLIENT` (id = item; an active CLIENT login of the client that owns the item's property, re-checked when queued and at dispatch; fails closed when the reader cannot read items). `POST /api/admin/lost-found/[id]/send-client-notice` (ADMIN, not impersonating; `lib/lost-found/client-notice.ts`, catalog `lostfound.client_notice`): explicit transports, the client's "no notifications" switch refused, no active login refused, the timeline records "Client told (sent from the app)" with each transport's state, or "Message to the client not sent" with the reasons; a retry with the same key adds no second entry; audited. Operations managers keep copy-and-record (owner decision pending). Guest messages and the client notice keep Copy beside Send for every role. The app-sent guest message's timeline entry now says `sentByApp: true` (it said recorded by the office). The Property column and the sort menu sort by property name over the whole filtered set: the matching ids from the list's own where, then one raw query orders exactly those ids by `lower(name)` (no property last, then found date and id) with OFFSET/LIMIT; the CSV export uses the same order; refused above 20,000 matches. The Command work-queue lost-item row opens the item (`?stage=new&open=`).

**Forms: Submissions in Results and stats; classic page retired (2026-11).** Results and stats has two views (`view=` chips): Statistics and **Submissions** (the tab row stays as drawn). Submissions: `GET /api/admin/form-submissions?paginated=1` (`lib/forms/submission-list.ts`, `list-query`): sort by submitted, form, property, job, cleaner; filters search, form, submitted date (organisation days), with warnings; rows show form, property, job, cleaner, submitted, warning count, media count, laundry, and a PDF button; the old unpaged shape is kept. The submission view (side panel, `?submission=`; `GET /api/admin/form-submissions/[id]`, `lib/forms/submission-detail.ts`) shows the questions from the stored effective form (`__templateSchema`, else the live schema with a note), answers (Table as a real table, Stock count as "item · counted N unit"), warnings, laundry fields, media gallery, report preview (lazy) and edit history. Administrators edit answers and laundry fields and add / remove / restore media (`lib/forms/submission-edit.ts`). Existing problems fixed: edits overwrote answers with no history (now `FORM_SUBMISSION_EDITED` with before/after of each changed answer and laundry field); removing media deleted the stored file (now the link goes, the original stays, the full media row is a `FORM_SUBMISSION_MEDIA_REMOVED` tombstone and "Restore" puts it back, `FORM_SUBMISSION_MEDIA_RESTORED`); added media had no ownership check (now the caller's, a cleaner on the job's or an office account's upload only; a non-http URL is replaced by the key's public URL); internal `__` fields are refused; structured answers are read-only in the editor (the classic text box could turn them into "[object Object]"). The job pages keep using the same PATCH. `/admin/forms` redirects (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER) with its tabs mapped: builder → Job forms, checklists → Checklist blocks / per-service, submissions → Results and stats / Submissions; `components/forms/checklists-workspace.tsx` is deleted (the v2 per-service checklists already did everything it did, plus photos). The job page's submission review also shows Table and Stock count answers this way.

**Classic cleaner job page (2026-11).** Not redirected: the classic look stays fully routable by design (`lib/portal-version.ts`), notification links still point at `/cleaner/jobs/<id>`, and the 6,766-line page could not be shown to be covered by the v2 page. Instead the classic field input renders Table and Stock count with the same shared controls as the v2 page (`components/v2/cleaner/structured-field-inputs.tsx`, now styled through a kit: Estate tokens for v2, classic tokens for the classic page), answering the same shapes.

**Monthly question once per month (2026-11).** `resolveEffectiveJobForm` also treats a monthly question as shown when another clean at the property this organisation month is submitted but not yet approved (job SUBMITTED or QA_REVIEW, not rework, not the job itself) and its stored effective form contained the question (`lib/accountability/monthly-pending.ts`, at most 25 submissions read). When that clean is reset or rejected it leaves those statuses and the question is due again; when it is approved, the existing stamp takes over.

**Design differences closed here.** Laundry: the three stat tiles moved to the top of Statistics and the Approvals line under the Pipeline, so the tabs sit under the header as drawn; lane cards carry their exception action (Ask cleaner, Request photo, Chase supplier) beside Move to. Cases: "Client can" is one choice (Not see it / See it / See and reply) as drawn, over the same two fields. Live ops: the late row's button reads "Tell client" as drawn (the confirm still says Notify client). Lost and found: Property sortable, sort menu entry.

**Tests.** `tests/api/case-repair-quote.test.ts`, `tests/api/laundry-supplier-chase.test.ts`, `tests/api/document-reminder.test.ts`, `tests/api/lost-found-client-notice.test.ts`, `tests/db/lost-found-property-sort.test.ts` (PostgreSQL parity across pages, both directions, filtered, export), `tests/api/form-submissions-office.test.ts`, `tests/components/structured-answer-display.test.tsx`, `tests/lib/monthly-pending-clean.test.ts`; updated on purpose: `tests/components/forms-authority-surface.test.tsx` (classic page now redirects), `tests/api/lost-found-office.test.ts` and `tests/components/lost-found-format.test.ts` (a client message is now sendable by an administrator), `tests/components/live-ops-follow-up.test.tsx` (row label), `tests/lib/case-linked-context.test.ts` and `tests/components/cases-refresh-and-qa.test.tsx` (linked quotes), `tests/lib/work-queue.test.ts` (lost item opens the item; Send reminder). No real message was sent (providers mocked).

## Wave E with the database: Applies-from pay rates, required documents, last seen, leave, Cancel job, end-time drops, QA template copies (2026-11-12)

Wave E items that need the schema (`docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`, sixth set, and the eighth set's QA template copies). One forward migration, `20261112120000_wave_e_people_jobs`: enum value `JobStatus.CANCELLED`; `Job.cancelledAt / cancelledById / cancelReason / cancelledFromStatus`; `QaFormSubmission.templateSnapshot / templateSnapshotName / templateSnapshotSource / templateRevision`; tables `UserPayRate` and `StaffLeave`; CHECK constraints (rate 0–1000, leave end ≥ start, leave kind, a cancelled job has a reason). Backfills: one `UserPayRate` row per person with a rate (their current `hourlyRate`, effective 1970-01-01, source BACKFILL), so every past job resolves exactly as before; every existing inspection gets a template copy marked RECONSTRUCTED (the "before" schema of the first template revision recorded after it, else the template as it is now; revision = revisions recorded up to it); `User.lastSeenAt` raised to the latest recorded sign-in (passkey `WebAuthnCredential.lastUsedAt`, trusted-device creation) when later. Nothing else is written. Verified on a fresh database (twice, no pending changes, `migrate diff` empty), on the existing `sneek_ui` copy (row checksums of User, Job, QaFormSubmission, JobAssignment, ClientInvoice(+Line), Payout, PayrollRun, AuditLog unchanged apart from the new columns) and on a seeded database exercising each backfill.

**Effective-dated pay rate ("Applies from").** `UserPayRate` rows are never edited. THE resolver is `PayRateBook` (`lib/accounts/pay-rate-resolve.ts`): the rate on a job is the newest non-removed row with `effectiveFrom` ≤ the job's calendar day (`scheduledDate`, unshifted; a later save on the same day wins); someone with no rows falls back to `User.hourlyRate`; someone with rows but none in force that day has no default rate (null, never 0 — the calculators fall through to their next source). Every path that read `User.hourlyRate` for a job now reads the book: payroll (`lib/finance/payroll.ts`, jobs and QA inspections), cleaner invoices (`lib/cleaner/invoice.ts`, both job paths and QA), QA pay page (`lib/qa/pay.ts`), job pay summary, holiday rates, finance summary, Insights costs (`FactContext.rateBook`), branch scorecards (`lib/phase4/analytics.ts`), the cleaner briefing, the cleaner job form and home page, and the QA screens' rate previews. Order of precedence is unchanged (assignment snapshot → per-cleaner job-type rate → this default; QA: inspection rate → this default → settings). Snapshots and frozen amounts (assignment `payRate`, payouts, run totals, submitted cleaner invoices, settled QA/shopping pay) are never rewritten. `PATCH /api/admin/users/[id]/pay { hourlyRate, appliesFrom? }` (ADMIN only, as before) — Applies from defaults to today (Sydney) and is refused in the past or more than a year ahead; the first change for someone without rows writes the baseline row first; a future row on the same day is replaced (tombstoned); `User.hourlyRate` changes only for a rate from today. `GET` returns the history (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER); `DELETE ?entryId=` removes a row that has not started (tombstone, audited `REMOVE_USER_PAY_RATE`); rows in force are refused. Bulk "Change rate" takes the same Applies from (source BULK). Screens that show "the rate" (Accounts list, person panel, cleaners roster, classic account page) show the rate in force today from the book. Not converted (display only, documented): the workforce performance pages still print the stored rate; the person summary's "estimated earnings" multiplies all logged hours by today's rate, as it did with the single rate.

**One Pay editor.** The rate is edited in one place: the person panel's Pay section on Profile (as drawn: Hourly rate + Applies from, "Jobs already paid keep the rate they were paid at."), the same component on the full account page. The Pay tab now shows the **Rate history** (in force / scheduled / past / removed, who saved it, Remove for a scheduled row) instead of a second editor. The classic account page links "Change pay rate" to the account page; per-job-type rates stay in Settings → Rates (linked from the history).

**Required documents per role.** AppSetting `staff_required_documents_v1 { byRole: { ROLE: [category…] } }` (`lib/accounts/required-documents*.ts`), Settings → Access → **Required documents** (catalog key `required-documents`, ops-catalog tab → accounts; office can view, ADMIN saves, audited `REQUIRED_DOCUMENTS_UPDATED`). Empty by default: nothing is shown as missing until a requirement is ticked; an unreadable stored value is reported, not treated as "nothing required". A person's requirements are the union over every role they hold. Documents section: each required type as On file / N d left / Expired / Missing (rejected documents do not count; the accounts list's 14-day window). Accounts list: a required type with nothing on file counts in "N missing" and in the Documents filter and Needs attention; the header adds "N missing documents"; "Complete" replaces "Up to date" only when requirements are set and all are on file.

**Last seen for everyone.** `recordLastSeen` (`lib/auth/last-seen.ts`) is called by the live session guards (`requireSession`, `requireRealSession`) with the genuine signed-in user id, resolved BEFORE the "test as" swap, so an impersonated target is never marked as seen. At most one write per person per 5 minutes per process, and the UPDATE matches only when the stored value is null or older than 5 minutes (several instances still write about once per interval); a failed write never refuses the request. Field-app pings keep writing it as before. Shown in the list (sortable, as before) and the person panel; null reads "Never signed in" for an invite not accepted, else "Not recorded yet".

**Leave and days off.** `StaffLeave` (calendar dates, end inclusive, kind LEAVE / DAY_OFF, note, created by; removal is a tombstone). `/api/admin/users/[id]/leave` GET/POST/DELETE for ADMIN and OPS_MANAGER (accounts pack at manage, as office notes); overlapping records refused; ended leave is history and not removed. Person panel → Work: "Leave and days off" list with Add/Remove (draft kept on cancel). Treated as unavailable: the Calendar's unavailability lane ("Leave" / "Day off" with the full reason as title and for screen readers, before "Not rostered"), cleaner suggestions (blocker kind `leave`, reason "On leave 12–14 Nov", before clashes and roster; peek button "On leave"), the move check (warning kind `leave`, shown in the confirmation), restore of a cancelled job, and the assign routes: `POST /api/admin/jobs/[id]/assign` and `/bulk-assign` answer 409 `LEAVE_CONFLICT` with each reason for a newly added cleaner on leave unless `confirmLeaveConflict: true` (the same confirm-and-resend pattern as `confirmCompletedReset`). The assign modal, quick assign and the Jobs bulk assign show the reason with "Assign anyway"; the calendar sends the override only after its confirmation listed the leave.

**Cancel job.** Rules (`lib/jobs/cancel-rules.ts`, service `lib/jobs/cancel.ts`, one transaction with the job row locked): a reason is always required; unstarted (Unassigned, Awaiting confirmation, Assigned): ADMIN or OPS_MANAGER (Jobs pack at manage); started (on the way through Completed): ADMIN only; never Invoiced or already Cancelled. Refused, with the reason, while the job is on an invoice that is not void (draft included), in a payroll run, paid through or on a cleaner invoice (not void / changes requested), has pay requests pending or approved, has settled QA pay or an inspection in progress, or a cleaner's clock is running. A cancel sets CANCELLED with who/when/why and the status it came from, releases the active assignments (rows, snapshots and responses kept), cancels QA inspections not started, keeps time logs, photos, forms, tasks and the laundry task (reported back for review), writes `JOB_CANCELLED` (incl. the admin behind a "test as"), and queues an inbox notice to the released cleaners (catalog `job.cancelled`, PIPELINE, STAFF_MEMBER scope, INBOX only: the phone/browser pushes are switched off explicitly; no email/SMS; client not told automatically). Restore (`/api/admin/jobs/[id]/restore`, ADMIN and OPS_MANAGER): only to the unstarted status it was cancelled from (a job that had started stays cancelled), with the released cleaners who are still active cleaners; the slot check (leave, clash, outside roster, day passed) answers 409 `RESTORE_CONFLICT` unless `confirmConflicts`, or `skipUserIds` leaves those cleaners off; inbox notice `job.cancel_restored`; audited `JOB_CANCEL_RESTORED`. Plain status edits can neither set nor leave CANCELLED (job PATCH, bulk status, Jobs status dialog); assign, auto-dispatch and reschedule refuse a cancelled job; the iCal sync never updates or revives one. UI: "Cancel job…" in the row menu (Restore… for a cancelled row), the peek panel (as drawn) and the bulk bar (`/api/admin/jobs/bulk-cancel`, one outcome per job); the job page's Danger tab; a cancelled job shows "Cancelled. Reason: …" with Restore. The existing delete is unchanged. Exclusions: the invoice generator (`NOT: { status: CANCELLED }`, even with time logged), Ready to bill, payroll, cleaner invoices, Insights/Quality (status inclusion lists), Live ops board, daily briefing, cleaner lists and apps (released assignment, plus explicit status lists), calendar (lanes, hours and counts; shown only with the Status filter "Cancelled", count still offered), the Jobs list default and the All / Today / This week counts (shown when the Status filter includes Cancelled), reminders (status inclusion lists), rework de-duplication, task carry-forward, public availability and booking capacity. The full audit of status enumerations is in the review record.

**Calendar day-view drop for a job with a separate end time.** `plannedTimeChange` moves start, due and end together (refused if the end would pass midnight or the job has an end but no start); the move check's plan carries `endTime`; `applyReschedule` writes it (audited before/after) and validates start ≤ end; Jobs bulk Reschedule passes it too. The confirmation shows "10:00–13:00 → 11:00–14:00 (the job keeps its length; start and end move together)".

**QA inspections keep a copy of their template.** A new inspection stores the template exactly as scored (`exactTemplateCopy`, revision = the template's QA_TEMPLATE_REVISION count, source EXACT); an amendment re-scores against the amended inspection's own copy and keeps it. Reading a past inspection (`inspectionTemplateSchema`) uses the copy; the QA report says "Template copy reconstructed: the exact version used is not known" for backfilled rows; the QA job API returns the copy and its label while amending, shown in the amending notice.

**Rules added (for the final summary).** A pay rate can never be backdated; it applies from today or a scheduled day, and a scheduled rate can be removed only before it starts. A job's default rate is the one in force on the job's day. Cancelling a job needs a reason; a started job needs ADMIN; money attached (non-void invoice, pay run, cleaner invoice, pay requests, settled QA pay) or a running clock refuses it; a cancel releases the cleaners and restore returns only to an unstarted status. Assigning a cleaner on recorded leave needs an explicit override. Leave is recorded by ADMIN and OPS_MANAGER with the accounts pack at manage. Required documents are set by ADMIN only.

**Tests.** `tests/db/wave-e.test.ts` (PostgreSQL: rates by day incl. future, payroll rates, snapshot untouched, no backdating, remove only future; cancel rules, refusals, release and restore; billing, Jobs list and calendar exclusion; leave in suggestions and conflicts; inspection copy after a template edit), `tests/lib/wave-e-rules.test.ts`, `tests/lib/wave-e-session.test.ts` (actual actor only, throttle, failure tolerated), `tests/lib/wave-e-cancel-notice.test.ts`, `tests/api/job-assignment-leave.test.ts`, new end-time cases in `tests/lib/calendar-time-drop.test.ts`. Updated on purpose: `tests/api/admin-user-pay.test.ts`, `tests/api/admin-users-bulk.test.ts` (history rows), `tests/api/calendar-check-move.test.ts` and `tests/lib/calendar-time-drop.test.ts` (end-time drops now allowed), `tests/lib/jobs-route-filters.test.ts`, `tests/lib/jobs-list-route-v2.test.ts`, `tests/lib/jobs-saved-view-counts.test.ts`, `tests/lib/jobs-view-presets.test.ts`, `tests/components/jobs-workspace-url.test.tsx` (twelve statuses, cancelled left out by default), `tests/lib/cleaner-briefing-audience.test.ts` (CANCELLED in the cleaner day exclusion); db mocks gained `userPayRate` / `staffLeave` / audit `count` in the finance, invoice, payroll, calendar, QA and assignment tests.

## Office sends for operations managers and the document request transport choice (2026-11-12)

Owner decisions of 2026-11-12 (items A and B in `docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`, "Owner decisions, ninth set"). No schema change, no migration.

**A. Who may send the office messages that were administrator only.** One rule, `lib/notifications/office-send-rules.ts` (pure), checked on the server by `lib/notifications/office-send-permission.ts` on every send with the live session guard (`requireRole([ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER])`, which re-reads the account, its roles and the request path's pack) and the action's own pack read live with `getOpsAccess`:
- ADMIN: always (an administrator, or anyone holding ADMIN, never inherits a pack).
- OPS_MANAGER: only with the pack of the page the action lives on at "Manage available actions" (View only and Off are refused with 403 and the reason; another pack never stands in):

| Action | Pack | Send route (server check) | Where the button is |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Chase supplier | `laundry` | `POST /api/admin/notifications/actions/supplier-chase`; preview `GET /api/admin/laundry/[taskId]/supplier-chase` (`canSend`) | Laundry lane card and run panel |
| Message guest | `lost-found` | `POST /api/admin/lost-found/[id]/send-guest-message`, `POST /api/admin/notifications/actions/guest-message`; preview `GET /api/admin/lost-found/[id]/message` (`send.available`) | Lost and found item panel |
| Tell client (lost item) | `lost-found` | `POST /api/admin/lost-found/[id]/send-client-notice`; same preview | Lost and found item panel |
| Client delay notice | `jobs` | `POST` and preview `GET /api/admin/notifications/actions/client-delay` | Live ops late exception ("Tell client") |
| Clock pause | `jobs` | `POST /api/admin/jobs/[id]/pause-clock` | Live ops off-site exception |

- Nobody while impersonating (administrators included): the send is refused with 403 and the preview says so; the target's restrictions apply. A revoked or deactivated account is refused by the session guard (401); a role other than ADMIN / OPS_MANAGER gets 403.
- Route ownership: the three notifications-area send routes now belong to the page's pack in `opsRequestFeature` (`supplier-chase` → laundry, `guest-message` → lost-found, `client-delay` → jobs), so the shared middleware and the session guard apply the same pack instead of `communications`. Staff message, laundry nudge and the rest of the notifications area stay with `communications`.
- Unchanged: rate limits, cool-downs, idempotency keys, recipient scopes, explicit transports, truthful states. Audit rows now record the actor's role beside the actor (`actorRole`: `ADMIN` or `OPS_MANAGER`) in `CLIENT_DELAY_NOTICE_SENT`, `ADMIN_CLOCK_PAUSE`, `LOST_FOUND_CLIENT_NOTICE_SENT`, `LAUNDRY_SUPPLIER_CHASE_SENT` and the guest message audit (addresses stay masked). The per-sender limits count the manager's own sends.
