- UI: Send is shown where the server would allow it (Live ops gets `canNotifyClient` and `canPauseClock` from the same rule); Copy, mailto:, sms:, tel: and "record what you did" stay for everyone who can open the record. Live ops actions also still need the page not to be read-only (the Command viewer's existing `readOnly`).

**B. Request document asks which transport to use.** One choice component, `components/v2/admin/office-actions/transport-choice.tsx`, used by Accounts "Request document" (`components/v2/admin/accounts/document-request-dialog.tsx`, opened from the Compliance board and from a person's Documents section) and Command's "Send reminder" (`components/v2/admin/command/document-reminder-dialog.tsx`):
- Inbox (in-app notice with phone and browser push), Email and SMS are explicit, combinable choices; each is disabled with its reason (`lib/workforce/document-request-transports.ts`): account not active, no email address / phone number, switched off for `workforce.document_request` in Notifications, email key missing, SMS switched off or not configured. Options come from `GET /api/admin/workforce/document-request?userId=` (and the reminder preview, which now carries `options`).
- "Don't ask again" remembers the ticked transports for the signed-in office user only. Store: AppSetting `office_send_transports_v1:<userId>` (`{ version: 1, kinds: { document_request: { transports, savedAt } } }`, `lib/notifications/send-transport-preference.ts`, advisory-locked writes; the same pattern as the Jobs saved views). The next dialog uses it without asking but always shows "Sending by: Inbox, Email · Change · Ask me again", and names a saved transport this person cannot get with the reason. An unreadable row is reported and not used. Reset: "Ask me again" in the dialog, or Notifications → Preferences → "Your sending choices" (`GET` / `DELETE /api/me/send-transports`, the caller's own row only, not while impersonating).
- Server (`POST /api/admin/workforce` `REQUEST_DOCUMENT`, both paths): transports as named; when none are named, the sender's saved choice; otherwise **the inbox only**. Before this change a request that named none sent the inbox notice plus email when an address existed and SMS when a phone existed; automatic onboarding requests (workforce service) keep that old set because they do not come through the route. An explicit empty list, an unknown transport, or "remember" without transports is 400. Both paths are refused while impersonating (the plain request used to be allowed). `rememberTransports: true` saves the choice after the request succeeded. The answer adds `transportsUsed` and `transportSource` (`request` / `saved` / `default`). Recorded states as before (pipeline states for inbox and email, the SMS provider's own answer).

**Tests.** `tests/lib/office-send-rules.test.ts`, `tests/api/document-request-transports.test.ts`, `tests/components/document-request-dialog.test.tsx`; changed on purpose (ADMIN-only assertions replaced by pack rules, with OPS with pack → allowed, OPS without pack → 403, revoked / impersonated → restricted): `tests/api/admin-client-delay-notice.test.ts`, `tests/api/admin-clock-pause.test.ts`, `tests/api/laundry-supplier-chase.test.ts`, `tests/api/lost-found-client-notice.test.ts`, `tests/api/lost-found-office.test.ts`, `tests/api/notifications-area-routes.test.ts`, `tests/components/live-ops-follow-up.test.tsx`, `tests/components/lost-found-format.test.ts`; `tests/api/document-reminder.test.ts` (a request naming no transports is now inbox only). Providers mocked; no real message sent.

## Wave F: urgent stock shopping runs and buy asks, job on a need, operations managers, stock count observations, case charges, laundry clock times, New lost items (2026-11-12)

Wave F of the admin redesign (`docs/plans/admin-redesign-2026-11.md`, fifth / sixth / seventh sets and the database items carried from Wave B and Wave E). One forward migration, `20261112180000_wave_f_stock_cases_laundry`: `UrgentStockNeed.sourceJobId / sourceSubmissionId / shoppingRunId / shoppingRunAddedAt / shoppingRunAddedById` (FKs SetNull); new tables `UrgentStockPurchaseAsk` and `StockCountObservation`; `ClientInvoiceLine.caseId` (FK to `IssueTicket`, SetNull, indexed); `LaundryTask.pickupTime / dropoffTime` (`VARCHAR(5)`, `HH:mm` CHECK constraints); a backfill of the job on existing needs only where certain.

**Stock tabs as drawn.** Urgent needs, Levels by property, Shopping runs, Stock counts, Catalogue, then On-hand, Scan tasks and Suppliers (kept, the drawing does not show them). The `?tab=` ids are unchanged (`items`, `properties`, `on-hand`, `stock-counts`, `scan-tasks`, `shopping-runs`, `suppliers`, `urgent-stock`); the drawn names (`catalogue`, `levels`, `urgent`, …) map to them (`components/v2/admin/inventory/inventory-tabs.ts`). A bare URL opens Urgent needs for those who may see it, else Catalogue.

**Add to shopping run** (`lib/inventory/urgent-stock-shopping.ts`, `POST /api/inventory/urgent-stock/shopping-run`, GET lists open runs). Uses the existing shopping-run tables: the need's item and quantity become a PLANNED `ShoppingRunLine` on an open run (DRAFT or ACTIVE, not submitted, not settled) or on a new DRAFT run owned by the office user; an existing line for the same property and item is linked, not duplicated; the run's `updatedAt` is touched so an owner editing an older copy is refused by the run's stale-write guard. The need records `shoppingRunId` and an `ADDED_TO_SHOPPING_RUN` event; its stage does not change (adding is not ordering, buying or delivering). Quantity: the one entered, else the requested quantity, else par less the count; with none, the need is refused ("Enter how many to buy"). Idempotent per request id (receipt `urgent_stock_v1:run_receipt:*`) and per need ("Already on …"); a closed run is refused with the reason; a new run with nothing added is rolled back. Single (row "Next action" for an acknowledged need, as drawn; panel) and bulk ("Add to one shopping run"). The row and panel show the run and what the run says ("On run …", "Bought on run … not yet delivered" from the line's purchase, "line removed"); the need resolves only through its own stages and the property confirmation.

**Ask cleaner to buy** (`lib/inventory/urgent-stock-ask.ts`, `POST /api/inventory/urgent-stock/asks`, withdraw `POST /api/inventory/urgent-stock/asks/[id]`, cleaner `GET/POST /api/cleaner/urgent-stock/asks[/id]`). `UrgentStockPurchaseAsk` ASKED → ACCEPTED / DECLINED, or WITHDRAWN by the office; one live ask per need (`activeKey`). Default recipient: a cleaner on the next clean at the property (the same deadline rule as the list); any active cleaner may be chosen; a chosen job must be theirs at that property. Refused while shopping is turned off for cleaners, while the need is closed, already asked or on an open run. The notice is catalog type `urgent_stock.purchase_ask` (pipeline, scope `URGENT_STOCK_PURCHASE_ASK`: only the asked cleaner while the ask is ASKED, re-checked at dispatch), transports chosen by the office with the inbox alone as the default (`OfficeSendTransport` gained "INBOX": the inbox row without phone or browser push); the outcomes are stored on the ask. The cleaner answers on the v2 job screen (Clean stage, "Can you buy these?") and on Supplies → Shopping: Accept puts the need on their own open run (or a new DRAFT run they own) through the shared attach function; Decline takes an optional note. "Bought" is derived, never stored: run submitted, line purchased and `adminApprovedForCleanerReimbursement` (the existing office approval); submitted but unapproved reads "Bought · awaiting office approval".

**Job on a need.** A cleaner's report from the job screen sends `jobId` (checked: at the property and one of their active jobs; an office report may name a job at the property); a form submission's need stores the job and submission. Row: "Reported by Jane C. during job J-1038, today 11:05" with a link; panel: "Jane C. during J-1038". Backfill: the need's first REPORTED event note "… Submission <id>." where that submission exists, its job is at the need's property and its submitter is the need's reporter; every other need keeps null and reads "Reported by <name>".

**Operations managers** (`lib/inventory/urgent-stock-access.ts`). The urgent stock service, list, bulk, shopping-run and ask services accept OPS_MANAGER by the inventory feature of their pack: read to see (tab, nav badge, list, panel, open runs, candidates), manage for every write. ADMIN is unaffected. ~~Still ADMIN-only: Resolve (policy), bulk Close and the reminder settings.~~ Changed 2026-11-12 (owner decision): Resolve, bulk Close and the reminder settings need the pack at manage too; see "Owner decisions: urgent stock rights, need-opened notice, applied stock counts on reset". `/urgent-stock` redirects the office (with access) into Stock.

**Stock count observations** (`lib/inventory/stock-count-observations.ts`). At submission, inside the submit transaction, each Stock count answer becomes a `StockCountObservation` (unique per submission and item): PENDING, or NEEDS_REVIEW with the reason when the property does not stock the item or the item is inactive (the office gets an inbox notice, event `submission.stock_count_review`). Nothing changes while the clean waits for approval. `applyJobRotationCompletion` (called by every approval path) calls `applyApprovedStockCounts`: earlier submissions' pending counts become SUPERSEDED; each PENDING count of the latest submission is applied under the stock row lock (on-hand SET to the count, `ADJUSTED` ledger row linked to the submission, previous on-hand kept), unless a stock movement not written by that submission was recorded at or after the count (SKIPPED_NEWER, kept with the reason). Never applied twice. The urgent need raised from the same submission records the count as `PENDING_APPROVAL` instead of applying it at once (changed on purpose; the panel says "applied to stock when the clean is approved") and is settled to APPLY / STALE on approval. Changed 2026-11-12 (owner decision): an APPLIED count was left in place when the job was later reset; now every reset path asks the office to reverse or keep it (see "Owner decisions: urgent stock rights, need-opened notice, applied stock counts on reset"). Stock → Stock counts → "Counts from job forms" (lazy disclosure; `GET/POST /api/admin/inventory/stock-counts/form-observations`) lists them with state and reason; "Mark reviewed" (note required) closes a NEEDS_REVIEW count without touching on-hand.

**Cases: Add charge to invoice** (`lib/billing/client-invoice-case-charge.ts`, `GET/POST /api/admin/cases/[id]/invoice-charge`). Description, amount before GST (GST per the invoice's setting) and a DRAFT invoice of the case's client (the case's client, else its property's, else its job's) or a new blank draft (`createBlankInvoice`). The line is added by `patchClientInvoice` (row lock, content lock, stale guard, totals), whose `addLine` accepts `caseId` and refuses, under the lock, anything but a DRAFT invoice of the case's client (`client-invoice-case-charge-rules.ts`). `ClientInvoiceLine.caseId` (a column rather than metadata: an indexed join for "Charge on …", survives line edits, SetNull keeps a billed line if a case is deleted). The case shows "Charge on INV-… (draft)" with links and an internal case note; the editor line shows "From case …". Rights: exactly the invoice ones (ADMIN; OPS_MANAGER with finance at manage, checked here because the route is under Cases). Idempotent per request key (receipt `case_charge_v1:*`; a retry after a failure reuses the draft it created). Nothing is approved or sent.

**Laundry clock times** (`lib/laundry/planned-times.ts`, `PATCH /api/admin/laundry/[taskId]/times`, ADMIN and OPS_MANAGER as for editing a run). Planned pickup / drop time of day on the existing days, set in the run panel ("Set planned times", stale-write guard, audited `LAUNDRY_PLANNED_TIMES_UPDATED`); cards show "Driver Tom 14:30" / "Pickup 14:30" / "Drop 09:15" and the panel "Wed 11 Nov 15:00" only when a time was planned. Days, statuses and Calendar laundry counts unchanged.

**Lost and found: New items older than 14 days.** `NEW_ITEM_FLAG_DAYS = 14` (named, separate from the configurable holding period; organisation calendar days from the found date). Row badge "New · 16 d" (warning), chip "New over 14 days N" (`?flag=new-stale`), summary "· N still new after 14 days" when there are any, a panel note, and the Command work-queue row reads "Lost item still new after N days: …".

**Rules added (for the final summary).** Adding a need to a shopping run never changes its stage; a closed run (submitted, approved or later, or settled) takes no new lines. One live "buy" ask per need; only the asked cleaner can answer, only while asked; "Bought" requires the office's reimbursement approval. Operations managers act on urgent stock with the inventory pack at manage (read to see); ~~resolving stays ADMIN-only~~ since 2026-11-12 resolving, bulk Close and reminder settings also need manage (ADMIN unchanged). A form's stock count sets on-hand only on approval, only from the latest submission, never over a newer stock movement. A case charge goes only onto a DRAFT invoice of the case's client, by those who may edit invoices. An item still New 14 days after it was found is flagged.

**Tests.** `tests/db/wave-f.test.ts`, `tests/db/wave-f-case-charge.test.ts` (PostgreSQL), `tests/lib/wave-f-rules.test.ts`, `tests/components/wave-f-ui.test.tsx`, `tests/api/wave-f-routes.test.ts`, new cases in `tests/lib/notification-office-sends.test.ts` and `tests/lib/notification-entity-scope.test.ts`; fixtures updated on purpose in `tests/components/urgent-needs-table.test.tsx` (an acknowledged need now offers "Add to shopping run", as drawn), `tests/lib/admin-attention-urgent.test.ts` and `tests/api/urgent-stock-office.test.ts` (operations managers with the pack), `tests/api/cleaner-submit-form-effects.test.ts` and the rotation tests (approval and submission now touch count observations).

## Parity audit fixes: functions restored after the admin redesign (2026-11-12)

An audit compared the redesigned admin with the pre-redesign baseline (commit `1fb4077`) under the owner's rule that no function that existed before may be lost. No schema change, no migration.

**Finance: "Mark as sent (not emailed)".** The old editor could move a DRAFT or APPROVED invoice to SENT through its status control without emailing. `availableInvoiceActions` (`components/v2/admin/finance/invoices/invoice-model.ts`) offers `mark-sent` on DRAFT and APPROVED invoices (list row menu and editor "more" menu; it waits for unsaved edits like every action on the stored invoice). The confirm dialog (`mark-sent-dialog.tsx`) states that no email is sent and that terms, due date and the edit lock follow; a refusal stays in the dialog. It is `PATCH /api/admin/invoices/[id] { status: "SENT" }` through `patchClientInvoice`, so every rule of the status path still applies: the lifecycle graph, the leave-draft rules ("Before you approve": rates, double billing, totals), started-work review, empty-invoice and credit-note limits, the payment-terms and first-sent (`sentAt`) snapshot, and the content lock once SENT. The status path needs no PIN (only line removal and delete do). Same roles as other status changes (ADMIN; OPS_MANAGER with finance at manage). The PATCH never emails (the send route does), so a move to SENT through it now writes `CLIENT_INVOICE_MARKED_SENT` (`before.status`, `after: { status, emailed: false, note: "Marked sent without email", impersonatedBy }`) in the same transaction as the update; the History card reads "Marked sent without email".

**Finance: "Group by property" saves the order.** On an editable invoice, turning "Group by property" on saves the grouped line order through the existing reorder save (`reorderLineIds`, `propertyGroupedOrder` in `editor/line-sections.ts`: the PDF's own grouping, property sections alphabetically, extras last, order kept inside each), as the old `persistLineOrder` did. Nothing is saved when the order already reads that way; turning it off, or any toggle on a read-only invoice, only changes the view. The toggle waits while a save runs.

**Quality: Rating mix per cleaner.** `CleanerRow.ratingMix` (`lib/quality/cleans.ts` `ratingMixByCleaner`): the cleaner's reviewed cleans in the period by authoritative review, with the old overview's rule (management review flag counts as "Mgmt review" whatever the rating; otherwise EXCELLENT / PASS / NEEDS_IMPROVEMENT / FAILED / MANAGEMENT_REVIEW). A review with no recognised rating is counted as "No rating", never folded into a band. Shown in the cleaner's expanded row as pills (empty bands left out; "No reviewed cleans in this period." rather than zeros).

**Quality: Mgmt review queue.** `QualitySummary.managementReview.count`: reviewed cleans in the period (the hub's clean basis) whose authoritative review is flagged for management review or rated MANAGEMENT_REVIEW, each clean once. A sixth stat card "Mgmt review queue" links to `/v2/admin/approvals?queue=managementReviews`; the Approvals workspace now opens the queue named in `?queue=` (otherwise the first queue with work, as before; the queue buttons expose `aria-pressed`). The card's basis says the Approvals queue lists every flagged review still waiting, which is not limited to the period.

**Quality: review queue counters.** `GET /api/admin/quality/inspections` adds `counters: { awaiting, inProgress, completedToday, timeZone }` (`inspectionQueueCounters`, `lib/quality/inspections.ts`): OPEN or ASSIGNED; IN_PROGRESS; COMPLETED with `completedAt` since midnight in the organisation timezone (`settings.timezone`, fallback Australia/Sydney, daylight-saving safe), all narrowed by the same property and text filters as the list. The Review queue tab shows Awaiting QA, In progress and Completed today above the inspection queue; an answer without counters shows none, never zeros.

**Laundry team metrics for quality-pack viewers.** The ops catalog maps a path to one pack, so "either pack" is a second read route: `GET /api/admin/quality/laundry-team-metrics` (quality pack, live `requireRole([ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER])`, the same `getLaundryTeamMetrics` service and response as `/api/admin/laundry/team-metrics`, which keeps the laundry pack). The Quality hub has a lazy "Laundry team and suppliers" disclosure (`hub/laundry-team-section.tsx`: Our laundry team cards, unattributed stuck tasks, External suppliers; 7 / 30 / 90 days; nothing read until opened; period kept when collapsed; late answers for another period dropped). Its "Laundry statistics" link shows only when the laundry pack allows that page (`HubContext.laundryStatsLink`). QA inspectors do not get the section.

**Accounts KPI strip.** Total staff, Active cleaners and Total clients are back above the role tabs (`AccountsHub` `kpis`, from the page's existing `getAccountsOverview` call). `getAccountsOverview` now lists failed counts in `unavailable`; the page passes those as null and the strip shows "–" with a note, never a fallback 0. "Outstanding receivables" stays removed by owner decision (receivables live in Finance).

**`/urgent-stock`.** Already restored by Wave F: the page redirects an OPS_MANAGER with the inventory pack (server-checked by `urgentStockOfficeAllowed`) to `urgentStockOfficeHref`, keeping the property; ADMIN is redirected as before and cleaners keep their page. Covered by `tests/api/urgent-stock-office.test.ts`; no further change here.

**Dead notification links.** `lib/notifications/accountability.ts`: "Coaching record acknowledged" links to the Quality hub's Coaching tab (`QUALITY_COACHING_HREF`, `/v2/admin/quality?tab=coaching`); the recurring-pattern alert links to the Issues tab for that cleaner and category over its window when the hub has that period (`recurringPatternHref`, button "Open in Quality"). Old emails resolve: `app/admin/accountability/page.tsx` redirects to the hub's Cleaners tab keeping the query, and `app/admin/accountability/coaching/[id]/page.tsx` to the Coaching tab (neither path existed before; the ops catalog keeps gating `/admin/accountability` by the quality pack).

**Tests.** New: `tests/lib/invoice-mark-sent.test.ts`, `tests/db/invoice-mark-sent.test.ts` (PostgreSQL), `tests/api/quality-parity-audit.test.ts`, `tests/components/approvals-queue-deep-link.test.tsx`, `tests/lib/accounts-overview-kpis.test.ts`; new cases in `tests/components/invoice-editor-page.test.tsx`, `tests/components/invoice-row-actions.test.tsx`, `tests/lib/quality-hub-service.test.ts`, `tests/components/quality-hub-actions.test.tsx`, `tests/components/quality-hub-page-access.test.tsx`, `tests/components/accounts-hub.test.tsx`. Changed on purpose: the exact action lists in `tests/components/invoice-row-actions.test.tsx` and `tests/lib/client-invoice-editor-rules.test.ts` include `mark-sent`; summary and cleaner fixtures in `tests/components/quality-hub-actions.test.tsx` and `tests/components/quality-hub-bonus-actions.test.tsx` carry the new fields.

## Owner decisions: urgent stock rights, need-opened notice, applied stock counts on reset (2026-11-12)

Three owner decisions on Wave F, plus a fourth that needs no change. No Prisma schema change, no migration: `StockCountObservation.status` is free text and already kept `previousOnHand` and the applied ledger row (`stockTxId`), which is all a reversal needs.

**1. Operations managers: Resolve, bulk Close, reminder settings.** An OPS_MANAGER whose inventory pack is at "manage" may Resolve a need (`ADMIN_RESOLVED`), use bulk Close, and read and change the urgent-stock reminder settings. Enforced on the server: `actOnUrgentStock` computes `mayResolve` from `urgentStockOfficeAllowed(actor, "manage")` (role and extra roles re-read from the database, pack from the live policy) and passes it to `validateUrgentStockTransition` (the parameter was `isAdmin`); bulk Close runs each need through that same function; `setUrgentStockSettings` and the `settings` field of `listUrgentStock` use the same rule. The settings live only in this service (no settings-catalog screen has its own guard), so nothing else was widened. "read" is refused for all three. While an administrator impersonates a manager, the session user is the target, so the target's own pack applies (never the administrator's rights); `lib/inventory/urgent-stock-actor.ts` adds `impersonatedBy` for the audit. Audit: the reminder settings row (`URGENT_STOCK_REMINDERS_UPDATED`) records `before`, the new values, `actorRole` and `impersonatedBy`; a Resolve event's detail records `actorRole` and `impersonatedBy`. UI: Stock → Urgent needs gets `canResolve` / `canEditReminders` from the page (same server rule) instead of "role is ADMIN"; the panel's Resolve, the bulk bar's Close and the Reminder settings button follow them. Wording changed from "administrator" to "the office" where the action is no longer administrator-only ("Resolved by the office"). ADMIN unchanged.

**2. "Urgent need opened" notice for operations managers.** The notice (catalog type `stock.urgent_need`, event `urgent_stock.open_need`, inbox only, category `shopping`, personal preferences respected) now goes to every active ADMIN and every active OPS_MANAGER whose inventory pack is at least "read", through the intent pipeline with a new entity recipient scope `URGENT_STOCK_OFFICE` (id = the need) in `lib/notifications/recipient-scopes.ts`. The pack is checked when the notice is queued (`urgentNeedOpenedRecipients` in `lib/inventory/urgent-stock.ts`) and again at dispatch (inbox and provider paths call the same scope rule): a pack turned off in between skips the delivery (`recipient_not_in_scope`); a corrupt policy fails closed; a manager who also holds ADMIN has no pack restriction, as everywhere else. One notice per person: candidates are read once per account and the envelope role is the account's stored role (the old code addressed users with an extra ADMIN role as ADMIN, which the dispatcher then skipped). No email, SMS or device push was added. Reminders for unresolved needs are unchanged (administrators only, scope ADMIN_OPERATIONS); not decided.

**3. Applied stock counts when a job is reset.** `lib/inventory/stock-count-reset.ts`. Paths that take an approved job out of Completed, and what each now does:
- Reset job (`/api/admin/jobs/[id]/reset`, dialog `components/v2/admin/jobs/job-reset-dialog.tsx`), QA reset (`/api/admin/jobs/[id]/qa-reset`, classic job page `components/qa/admin-qa-reviews.tsx`), a status edit through `PATCH /api/admin/jobs/[id]`: when the job has APPLIED counts the request must carry `appliedStockCounts: "REVERSE" | "KEEP"`; otherwise 409 `STOCK_COUNT_CHOICE_REQUIRED` with `appliedStockCounts` (item, counted value, on hand now, value before the count) and a message naming them. Checked before the PIN and again inside the reset transaction (a count applied in between is caught). GET on the reset and QA reset routes lists the counts for the dialogs. The status edit's 409 also says to use Reset job (the job edit form shows the message; it has no choice control).
- Bulk status change: a COMPLETED job with applied counts going to any other status is a blocked row naming the reason ("Use Reset job …"); apply refuses the batch with 409 naming the jobs.
- QA verdicts that move a COMPLETED job back to QA_REVIEW (QA inspection fail, admin QA score fail, photo-review deduction fail, the authoritative-review recompute after a score is edited or deleted) are verdicts, not resets: the counts stay applied and each observation records "Kept: the job left approval … without an office choice" (audited `STOCK_COUNTS_KEPT_NO_CHOICE` when a person caused it; the recompute has no actor, so only the observation reason).
- The QA inspection Reopen (`/api/qa/jobs/[id]/reopen`) does not change the job's status and is unchanged.

Dialogs: the Reset job dialog and the QA reset dialog list the counts and ask "Reverse these counts" / "Keep them"; nothing is pre-selected and Reset stays disabled until one is chosen; a 409 that brings new counts shows them and clears the choice; the dialog's state resets when the job changes; the Reset job dialog's context read is cancelled when it closes or the job changes.

Reverse, per item, under the stock row lock: restore `previousOnHand` only when no stock movement was recorded at or after the count's own ledger row and on-hand still equals the counted value (a change without a ledger row also counts as newer); otherwise "not reversed: newer record" and left as it is; an unknown `previousOnHand` is "not reversed" (never taken as zero). A reversal writes an `ADJUSTED` `StockTx` (not linked to the submission, so it survives "Clear form data"), sets the observation to `REVERSED` with the reason and the reversal ledger id, and keeps the applied ledger row and `previousOnHand`. A REVERSED count is never applied again by a later approval. Keep leaves on-hand and status as they are and writes the decision on the observation. Every decision writes `STOCK_COUNTS_RESET_DECISION` (path, choice, outcomes, actor, role, impersonating administrator); the reset's own audit row carries the same outcomes. The response's `stockCounts` lets the dialogs say "N reversed; not reversed (newer record): …".

Reset job with "Clear form data": the choice runs first. The count's ledger row is detached from the submission (kept as the record that explains on-hand) instead of being "restored" and deleted with the submission's usage rows; usage rows of an item whose count stays in force (kept or not reversed) are not added back on top of the counted value; a reversed item returns to its pre-submission value as before. Limitation: the observation rows themselves still go with the deleted submission (`onDelete: Cascade`); their final state is in both audit rows.

Stock → Stock counts → Counts from job forms shows the new state "Reversed when the job was reset".

**4. Lost items still New.** Owner: an item still at New stays a separate count (the "New over 14 days" chip and summary are not merged into another count). No code change.

**Tests.** `tests/db/owner-decisions-stock.test.ts` (PostgreSQL, 13): manager with manage resolves and bulk-closes, read refused; settings by manage with audit role, read refused and sees none; impersonated read-only manager refused, impersonation recorded, a revoked pack takes effect at once; notice recipients (admins, managers with read / manage, not off, one notice for a manager who is also ADMIN); pack re-checked at dispatch and a muted preference skipped; reset requires the choice and names the counts; Reverse restores, newer movement not reversed, ledger kept, reversed count never re-applied; Keep; a path nobody decides; the reset, QA reset and job PATCH routes (409 then success, "Clear form data" with Keep). `tests/lib/stock-count-reset-rules.test.ts`, `tests/components/stock-count-reset-dialog.test.tsx`, `tests/components/qa-reset-stock-count-choice.test.tsx`, `tests/components/urgent-office-manage-controls.test.tsx`; new cases in `tests/lib/notification-entity-scope.test.ts` (scope rule) and `tests/lib/jobs-bulk-status.test.ts` (blocked row). Changed on purpose: `tests/db/wave-f.test.ts` (a manager with manage now resolves), `tests/lib/urgent-stock-policy.test.ts` and `tests/lib/urgent-stock-actions.test.ts` (`isAdmin` renamed `mayResolve`); fixtures given an empty `stockCountObservation` reader in `tests/api/qa-reset-financial-guard.test.ts` and `tests/lib/jobs-bulk-status.test.ts`.

## Owner reports: lost and found photos, laundry partners as suppliers, laundry team requests, account email resends (2026-11-12)

Owner's words: "Lost and found images doesn't show"; "Laundry partners is also suppliers, so need to link them and show them in suppliers list"; "Laundry pickup/drop off is handled by laundry partners/suppliers only so make the changes in the laundry page to ask laundry team buttons etc."; "Also allow re-send any invitations or emails related to new accounts, password resets, etc." One forward migration, `20261112210000_laundry_partner_links` (additive, nullable): `LaundrySupplier.stockSupplierId` (unique) and `User.laundrySupplierId` (FK to `LaundrySupplier`, `ON DELETE SET NULL`, indexed).

**Lost and found photos (root cause and fix).** Photos are stored as `{ key, url }` with `url = S3_PUBLIC_BASE_URL/<key>` (`publicUrl`), and the pre-redesign board (`1fb4077`) rendered that link directly. Everything else in the app treats the bucket as private and signs on demand, so the public link only works where the bucket is public and `S3_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` is set. The redesign's fallback asked `/api/uploads/access`, which refuses every cleaner (job-scoped `jobs/<jobId>/` keys only; lost-found keys are `lost-found/<uploader>/…`) and every role but ADMIN/OPS_MANAGER/QA; the cleaner detail gallery had no fallback at all; the office "Log item" preview used the same public link. Now every place a lost item photo appears (table thumbnail, item panel and its lightbox, the cleaner's item detail, the Log item preview) shows it through `GET /api/lost-found/[id]/photo?key=<key>` (`lib/lost-found/photo-src.ts`): same access rule as opening the item (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER, or the reporting CLEANER while the module is on), the key must be one of the item's own photos, answer is a 302 to a 10-minute signed link (`private, no-store`); if storage cannot sign it falls back to the public link only when `S3_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` is set, else 503 `STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE`. Serialized items carry `photos[].src` (the stored `url` is unchanged). A photo that cannot be loaded shows "Image could not be loaded" with Retry (asks the route again), never a blank; the lightbox says so too. The Log item preview uses a local object URL. Clients have no lost-found screen and the "Tell client" message carries no photo. The classic cleaner page lists no photos. Production checks: see the review record.

**Laundry partners in the Suppliers list.** Inventory › Suppliers reads `GET /api/admin/inventory/suppliers/directory` (`lib/laundry/partners.ts`, read model `lib/suppliers/directory-types.ts`): stock suppliers (AppSetting catalog, unchanged) and laundry partners (`LaundrySupplier`) together, type "Stock supplier", "Laundry partner" or both when linked. A partner row shows its contact, turnaround (hours, "not recorded" when empty), price per kg, its laundry portal team ("No laundry portal users linked" when none) and "Laundry runs (n)" → `/v2/admin/laundry?supplier=<id>` (the Pipeline opens filtered to that partner). "Add laundry partner" and "Edit partner" use the existing laundry supplier endpoints, which now also take `stockSupplierId` ("Same business as": one stock supplier links to one partner, 409 otherwise; 404 when the stock record is gone) and `teamUserIds` (LAUNDRY accounts only; moving someone from another partner is allowed and recorded); both are optional, so Laundry › Suppliers keeps working unchanged. Changes are audited `LAUNDRY_PARTNER_LINKS_UPDATED`. Deleting a stock supplier unlinks the partner (the partner, its runs and team stay); deleting a partner unlinks its people (accounts stay). A link to a stock supplier that no longer exists is reported on the list, never dropped silently.

**Laundry pickup and drop-off go to the laundry partner's team.** `lib/laundry/nudge-rules.ts`: the cleaner is asked only about the bags at the property ("Ask cleaner": NOT_READY, or bags unconfirmed on the pickup day after the clean); the cleaner photo request and the cleaner ask for an overdue pickup the cleaner marked ready are no longer offered. Team requests, each only where the run calls for it: "Ask laundry team to confirm pickup" (pickup due/overdue, bags not reported not-ready), "Ask laundry team to confirm drop-off" (picked up, drop day reached), "Ask laundry team: when will it be back?" (over turnaround or drop overdue), "Ask laundry team for a photo" (delivered without the required drop photo). They go through `POST /api/admin/notifications/actions/laundry-team-request` (ADMIN and OPS_MANAGER, as the cleaner nudge; never while impersonating) → `sendLaundryTeamRequest` (`lib/notifications/office-actions.ts`), catalog `laundry.team_request`, new recipient scope `LAUNDRY_TASK_SUPPLIER_TEAM` (active LAUNDRY account whose `laundrySupplierId` is the run's supplier and who may see the property under the laundry visibility rule; checked when queued and again at dispatch), 30-minute cool-down per run and kind (audit `LAUNDRY_TEAM_REQUEST_SENT`), the server re-applies the Pipeline rule, transports chosen explicitly, truthful per-recipient states. A run whose partner has no such users shows the button disabled with the reason, and "Chase supplier" is then offered for the stored contact even before the turnaround is exceeded, with a draft that asks for the same thing (`supplier-chase-rules.ts` `unansweredTeamRequest`, decided again on the server). Lane cards show the most pressing team request and the cleaner ask; the run panel shows all, the partner as "Laundry partner" and its "Laundry team" names. The route stop label "Driver Tom, stop 2" became "<partner> · Tom, stop 2" ("Laundry team · …" without a partner), and the Pipeline filters read "Laundry team" and "Laundry partner". The laundry portal Today page has "Requests from the office" (`lib/laundry/office-requests.ts`): requests addressed to the signed-in team member in the last 72 hours for runs they can see, "Open" while the run still calls for it, else "Done", with "Answer on the Queue" (existing confirm pickup / drop-off / photo actions); a read failure says so.

**Account emails.** Inventory and rules in `lib/accounts/account-email-rules.ts`. Resendable: the invitation link (UserInvitation), a password reset link (30 minutes, to the person's own account address only) and the sign-up code (self-registered, unverified). Each send issues a fresh token or code that replaces the previous one, has a 5-minute cool-down per person and type (a provider-refused send can be retried at once), goes through the existing mail path (`sendEmailDetailed`, critical/transactional as before) and is audited `ACCOUNT_EMAIL_SENT` with the real sender, role, address and the provider's answer (`lib/accounts/account-email-record.ts`): Accepted (delivery not confirmed), Not sent, Unknown, Not sent (not configured/switched off). Not resendable by design: temporary-password emails (use the reset link instead; a password is never mailed or shown again), sign-in and two-step codes, VA invitations and document requests (their own screens resend). Senders: ADMIN, or OPS_MANAGER with Staff accounts at manage (office-send-rules `account_email`); never while impersonating; never to a person whose role (primary or extra) is above the sender's; not to yourself. The person panel's Activity tab (and the full page) has "Account emails" (`GET/POST /api/admin/users/[id]/account-emails`): the account address, invitation state, the buttons the viewer may use (or why not), confirm before sending, the provider's answer after, "Copy invitation link" for a pending, unexpired invitation (`POST …/invitation-link`, not re-issued, audited `ACCOUNT_INVITATION_LINK_COPIED`), and the history from `ACCOUNT_EMAIL_SENT` plus older records (account created with invitation, administrator temporary password, client portal access, invitation accepted), "Not recorded" where nothing was recorded. "Forgot password" now records its sends and is limited to 3 per address per 15 minutes (it had no limit), and its email escapes the person's name.

**Fixed on the way.** "Resend invite" (person menu, `POST /api/admin/users/[id]/resend-otp`, Accounts bulk) sent a sign-up CODE to people the office invited by LINK, who have no password, so it could not get them in; it now sends a fresh invitation link to a pending invitation and the code only to a self-registered login, with the sender rules, cool-down and audit above (an OPS_MANAGER could previously resend to an administrator, without the pack check). The bulk action is "Resend invitation" and the menu item is shown only for an active person with a pending invitation or unverified email.

## Properties list and property page redesign (2026-11-12)

Built from the approved boards (Properties, Property full page). No schema change. Role lists unchanged: every page and route is ADMIN / OPS_MANAGER and sits under the operations `properties` feature (middleware path mapping), as before.

**Properties list** (`/v2/admin/properties`, `components/v2/admin/properties/list/*`)
- Heading "Properties" with a real summary ("N active properties for N clients · N need attention"), Export and Add property. Four stat cards: Active (clients), Calendar linked (sync errors), Cleans next 7 days (jobs scheduled today and the next six days, cancelled excluded; unassigned = status UNASSIGNED), Jobs, all time.
- `GET /api/admin/properties?shape=list` (`lib/properties/portfolio-list.ts`): server paging, sort (`name`, `size`, `calendar`, `nextClean`, `qa`, `flags`; anything else → 400) and filters (`q`, `clientId`, `suburb`, `chip`, `includeOneOff`; an unknown chip → 400) through `lib/admin/list-query.ts`, with real totals, chip counts, stats, facets and the mapped count. The active portfolio is read whole (as the old page did) because the derived columns are not stored; each fact is one batch query (`lib/properties/portfolio-facts.ts`). Without `shape=list` the original array shape every picker reads is unchanged.
- Columns: Property (suburb · client), Size, Calendar (Not linked / Not synced yet / Syncing / Synced / Failed with the age of the latest failed run), Next clean (next job still to happen, Sydney day, "None booked"), QA, 30 d (average authoritative score of cleans scheduled in the last 30 days; "Not enough data" below 3 reviewed, never 0), Flags, one row action (Open the panel, or Fix sync → the property's Calendar sync card) and the more menu (Open property, New job here, Copy as new property, Edit form fields, Deactivate with PIN or password).
- Flags (`lib/properties/portfolio-rules.ts`): Sync error (integration in ERROR), Key lost (key-lost mode), Linen late (an open laundry run the Laundry pipeline's own detector calls pickup overdue, drop overdue or over turnaround; cancelled jobs and no-pickup runs excluded), Care due (an OPEN property-care JobTask), No laundry (laundry service on and nobody allocated). Chips: Needs attention (any flag), Sync error, Key lost, No laundry partner; "Show one-off sites" keeps `?includeOneOff=1`.
- Views List / Cards / Map in the URL (`?view=`); Cards are the old portfolio cards over the same server page; Map stays the geocoded count with the ops map link (the admin has no embedded map).
- Bulk bar (always visible, "Select properties to"): Set laundry partner, Assign checklist, Sync calendars now (at most 20), Export. One endpoint `POST /api/admin/properties/bulk` (`lib/properties/bulk.ts`) with per-property outcomes (done / no change / refused / failed); refused and failed rows stay selected. Laundry partner = `accessInfo.laundryTeamUserIds` merged through `buildPropertyAccessInfo` in a locked transaction with an audit row (`PROPERTY_LAUNDRY_TEAM_SET`); a legacy plaintext door code still in the JSON is moved to the encrypted column first; laundry-off properties refused. Checklist = the property form-template override for one job type (`lib/properties/form-overrides.ts`, now shared with `PATCH /api/admin/properties/:id/form-overrides`; same validation and audit). Sync = `syncPropertyIcal` per property (unlinked and already-running refused). Export: `GET /api/admin/properties/export` (filters, or `ids=`).
- Preview panel on `?open=` (`GET /api/admin/properties/:id/preview`, `lib/properties/preview.ts`): name, address, client, Open property, more menu, cover photo, tabs Overview / Jobs / Care / Access / More (links to the page's tabs). Overview: size, checklist (the template a turnover would use, by `resolveJobFormTemplate`), laundry partners, calendar, access (whether a code is recorded; the code itself is not sent), Next cleans, Property care due, Recent (latest QA review, latest laundry delivery, latest property audit entry).

**Property page** (`/v2/admin/properties/[id]`, `components/v2/admin/properties/property-detail.tsx` + `detail/*`)
- Header: ← Properties, name with Active pill (and Key lost mode), address · client link · check-in / checkout; Sync calendar now (linked calendars only), primary New job here (`/v2/admin/jobs/new?propertyId=`, which the New job form already reads), More (Copy as new property, Edit form fields, Deactivate with PIN or password).
- Key facts: bedrooms, bathrooms, sofa beds, jobs, reservations, last clean (latest submitted / QA / completed / invoiced job, linked), average rating and lifetime value (existing stats endpoint; paid invoice lines only).
- Tabs (`lib/properties/detail-tabs.ts`, URL `?tab=`): Overview, Checklist and forms, Access guide, Stock, Jobs and history, Care and planning, Billing rates. Old links: `profile` → Overview, `inventory` → Stock; `jobs`, `checklist`, `access`, `billing` unchanged. Deep-clean planning and the property memory and care workspace moved from Jobs to Care and planning.
- Overview: "Coming up, from the calendar and jobs" (`GET /api/admin/properties/:id/overview`, pure join `lib/properties/coming-up.ts`): each day in the next 21 days with a job or a checkout — guest out, guest in ("No booking yet", Same day), job and status ("Job created at next sync" only when the calendar is linked with turnover creation on, otherwise "No job"), cleaners or Assign (opens the job's peek on Jobs), laundry (pickup day/time, Picked up, Delivered, No pickup needed, No laundry service). The old one-page Profile form is split into cards with their own Edit, each sending only its keys: Details (name, address with map pin, rooms incl. sofa beds, times, cover photo), Service settings (five switches saving on change; key lost mode still confirms first), Cleaning standard and pay, Setup guide, Next-job checklist (also on Checklist and forms; same idempotent request id), Notes and onboarding (notes can now be cleared; onboarding stays read only), Performance (existing stats strip), Documents and additional details (only when `accessInfo.attachments` or intake custom fields exist; Add file uploads through `/api/uploads/direct` and appends to the access files).
- Rail: Client (contact, active properties, terms; amount owing only for ADMIN or an operations manager with finance, omitted on the server otherwise, from the receivables helpers), Access (lockbox, door code masked with Show — read from the encrypted column, edited into it —, parking, other, instructions, NFC tags and whether required, link to the Access guide tab), Calendar sync (switch, URL, state, Save, Sync now, recent runs with Undo; the URL draft survives a reload of the record), Laundry (partners with their partner business, bag colour and label, driver access, Edit for partners / bag), Stock (tracked, at or below reorder), Billing rates.
- Stock tab: the old Inventory tab moved unchanged in rule (staged rows, Save levels through set-levels).

## Cleaner portal redesign: five tabs, Today, Supplies, Pay, More and the job screen (2026-11-12)

Approved boards "Cleaner portal" (`CleanerPortal.dc.html`) and "One job, four moments" (`CleanerJobFlow.dc.html`). Presentation and composition only: no schema change, no migration, no new or changed API route, no change to save, submit, draft, offline, upload restoration, clock or laundry commands.

- **Navigation.** Phone tabs are Today, Jobs, Supplies, Pay, More (`components/v2/cleaner/portal/portal-nav.ts`, plain module; `lib/navigation/mobile-tabs.ts`). `/v2/cleaner/more` is the labelled, role-filtered More menu again (Work: route and driving mode, schedule month view, QA feedback, lost and found, maintenance only while assigned; You: availability and leave, documents `hub?tab=documents`, team hub, profile, settings; Account: switch role, classic look, sign out; notifications stay in the header bell). Module gates (calendar, lost and found) are read on the server; maintenance uses the live assignment check. The phone menu button is hidden for cleaners (More replaces the drawer); the desktop rail keeps every destination grouped. `PortalShell` gained optional `mobileOnly`/`tabActivePrefixes` on nav items and `immersive`/`hideMobileMenuButton` props; other portals are unchanged. Tab badges: Today attention, Jobs offers, Supplies waiting buy asks (`GET /api/cleaner/urgent-stock/asks`, state ASKED; unreadable = no badge), Pay pending pay requests, More QA fails plus maintenance.
- **Jobs.** List / Month switch on `/v2/cleaner/jobs` and `/v2/cleaner/calendar` (hidden when the calendar module is off). Both URLs unchanged.
- **Today.** Greeting and "N accepted jobs today · M offers waiting"; clock state card from the open `TimeLog` (Not clocked in / Clocked in since / unknown when the read fails — never assumed); Today's brief; next job card (pay from `computeJobPayForCleaner`, Open job, Navigate to `/route` for today's job, Google Maps otherwise); offer cards with Accept/Decline and "Access details appear once you accept"; Needs attention (existing feed) with coaching and QA cards; My day (route summary with saved order plus today's jobs) and the 7-day count. Stat tiles removed (the count lines replace them).
- **Supplies.** To do · Shopping · Counts · Urgent · My stock. To do lists office buy asks, the cleaner's open shopping runs (receipt missing, bought so far from recorded line costs), stock counts asked of them (Quick scan / Full count) and urgent needs they reported with the recorded stage, each read failing separately as "could not be loaded"; "A report tells the office what is needed. It does not order anything or prove delivery." Counts holds Stock counts (module), Quick scan and Restock; `?tab=quick-scan|restock|stock-runs` still open those tools; `/restock`, `/stock-runs`, `/shopping` still redirect.
- **Pay.** This period · Invoices · Requests. This period shows the server's `estimatedPay` with its parts (jobs, shopping time, reimbursements, approved extra pay, QA inspections, transport — each a server total; lines add up to the estimate), the default rate in force from `UserPayRate` and its start date, the period's jobs with recorded status, pending extra pay (not in the total) and the gap-jobs warning. `/v2/cleaner/invoices` and `/v2/cleaner/pay-requests` redirect to the tabs keeping `preset` and `jobId`.
- **Job screen.** On a phone the portal header and tabs hide inside one job. Header: back to Today, property name, job number and a live "2 h 12 m to guest arrival" (Sydney day of the job only), Property info; status, timer, pause/resume, Navigate, Call, date and copy address kept. Five-stage rail with Accept shown done. Offline banner reads "Saved on this phone, not sent yet" and says nothing counts as sent until the draft save is confirmed. Sticky bottom bar: Back / Next; on Set up the clock-in button ("Clock in and start" / "Resume timer") with the reason it is unavailable (boxes not ticked, or no connection) — the clock card no longer repeats it until the job has started. Wrap up opens with "Before you submit" (only blockers, each a jump link; items still on the phone listed apart), the laundry outcome is a segmented Ready / Not ready / No pickup choice over the same stored values, Submit sits in the bottom bar with "Fix N items first" and the optional "Clock out (finish form later)". After submit: status pill, time on site, server pay for the job, adjustments, laundry "Saved" (delivery tracked separately). The + sheet adds "Urgent stock for this property" (the existing urgent stock workspace locked to the job's property).
- **Not built (needs a rule or data that does not exist):** a QA correction that unlocks one item as revision 2; a submission receipt number and photo/answer counts after submit; the rate snapshot breakdown (hours × rate) on the job; "photos sent · answers" sync counts; the invoice opening date; an early-start block while guests are inside.

## People: clients and cleaners in one directory, client page, person page, client assistants, account emails (2026-11-12)

Approved boards `PeopleDirectory.dc.html`, `ClientDetail.dc.html`, `CleanerDetail.dc.html`, `AssistantWorkspace.dc.html`, `Invitations.dc.html` ("build all designs as drawn"; owner answers 2026-11-12: nav label "People"; suppliers edited only in Inventory › Suppliers; client assistants stay under their client and have their own People tab; "Paid this month" in header figures and Columns; "Copy invite link" wherever a still-valid link exists). No schema change, no migration.

- **People (`/v2/admin/accounts`, route kept).** One h1 "People", a real summary line, Export (per tab), Invite person (ADMIN), New client. Tabs: Clients · Cleaners · Office and other staff (ADMIN, OPS_MANAGER, QA_INSPECTOR, **MAINTENANCE**) · Laundry partners and suppliers (LAUNDRY logins + read-only supplier records linked to Inventory › Suppliers) · Assistants · Invitations; Everyone / Needs attention (/ Client logins) are chips. Tab in `?tab=`; each tab keeps its own URL state, columns (per-tab saved Columns), chips, bulk bar and side panel. Old values: `team|staff` → Office, `vas` → Assistants, `emails` → Invitations, `logins` = client logins; `?tab=clients` (formerly client logins) now opens the Clients tab, whose panel lists each client's logins. `lib/people/tabs.ts` (plain).
- **Clients tab.** `GET /api/admin/clients/directory` (`lib/people/clients/*`): server filter (`filter=all|active|inactive|owing|overdue|no-login|assistants|attention`, `q`), sort (name, properties, logins, invoicesOpen, owing, lastClean, status; missing figures last), paging through `lib/admin/list-query.ts`, chip counts, header figures (Total clients, Outstanding receivables with overdue, Paid this month, Active properties) and CSV (`format=csv`, `ids=` for Export selected). Owing/overdue/paid reuse `lib/finance/receivables` and the invoice list's overdue and fully-credited ids; unreadable money is null ("–"), never $0. Columns as drawn; "Paid this month" in Columns. Money visibility unchanged: everyone admitted to Clients saw these figures before. Bulk `POST /api/admin/clients/bulk`: Send update (custom message through `sendLifecycleEmail`, manual mode, one outcome per client) and Mark inactive (same effect and audit as Delete client, PIN or password and the `clients.delete` grant). Send portal invite stays one client at a time (it resets that client's portal password). Side panel (`GET /api/admin/clients/[id]/summary`, `lib/people/clients/summary.ts`): Summary / Money / Logins / Activity.
- **Cleaners tab.** The Accounts list (`/api/admin/users?paginated=1`) with two new figures: **Jobs this week** (assignments scheduled Monday–Sunday Sydney, cancelled excluded) and **Owed** (approved pay adjustments, as the old roster summed; pay visibility only); new sorts `jobsWeek`, `owed`. Header figures carry the Workforce overview and roster KPIs (`lib/people/cleaner-figures.ts`); links to Compliance, Coaching, Recognition, Roster (workforce pack) and Home addresses (ADMIN). `?tab=cleaners&rate=none` (Finance "no pay rate" link) still works.
- **Assistants tab.** `GET /api/admin/va-teams` (`lib/people/assistants.ts`; grants and scope through the client-portal parsers): table grouped by client team (Group by team switch), status chips, client filter, one button per row (Open / Resend / New invite / Reactivate), row menu (profile, team, Resend invite, Copy invite link, Remove from team) plus the account actions menu for ADMIN; bulk Resend invite and Suspend (existing `users/bulk` actions); team panel: Team active switch, permissions, never-allowed money rule, property scope (PATCH `/api/admin/va-teams/[id]`, audited), Members, Invite (up to 20, `POST /api/admin/va-invites`), Activity (members' audit rows). Creating a new team keeps the existing manager in a disclosure. Editing stays ADMIN as before.
- **Invitations tab (account emails).** `GET /api/admin/users/account-emails` (`lib/people/account-emails/*`): one row per person with their latest account email (from `ACCOUNT_EMAIL_SENT`, `CREATE_USER` invite, `RESET_USER_PASSWORD`, `SEND_CLIENT_INVITE` audit rows and each person's invitation), recorded delivery only (Accepted / Not sent / Unknown / Not recorded; Delivered, Opened and Bounced are not stored for account emails, so the Bounced figure reads "Not recorded"), expiry, signed in; type and state chips, sender, period; per-row `canResend` / `canCopyLink` decided on the server by the same rules as the person panel (ADMIN, or OPS_MANAGER with accounts at manage; never upward; never while impersonating; never yourself). Detail panel reuses the person's Account emails section (timeline, Resend with the "old link stops working" warning, Copy link). Bulk Resend uses `users/bulk`. Copy link: `POST /api/admin/users/[id]/account-emails/invitation-link` (not re-issued, audited) from the list, the panel and assistant rows.
- **Client page (`/v2/admin/clients/[id]`).** Header with the status-dependent primary action (an overdue invoice → "Chase overdue invoice", opening it; else "Send update"), New quote, More (Edit client details, Send portal invite, Re-send a document, Add property, Open in Approvals; Danger zone: Delete client, PIN or password — the existing ClientActions dialogs via a new `renderTrigger` prop); key facts; tabs in `?tab=` (Overview, Properties, Jobs, Invoices, Profit and pricing, Portal access, Messages, Documents, History); main cards (Needs attention, Properties with next/last clean, turnover rate and calendar sync, Outstanding invoices with due days, Quotes, Recent jobs, Cases and leads, Activity); rail (Contact and notes, Portal logins and teams, Billing, What the client sees, Messages and automation, Documents). Payment terms editor, profit, communications, automation rules, documents and timeline are the existing components. Data loader moved to `lib/people/clients/client-page-data.ts`.
- **Person page (`/v2/admin/accounts/users/[id]`).** As drawn on the Cleaner board for every person: Test as, Message (staff message route, when allowed), primary action from the most urgent document, More (Person: edit, Add extra payment — ADMIN, the roster's endpoint —, change rate, upload document, coaching, kudos; Sign-in; Danger zone, existing PIN rules); key facts from the profile; tabs (Overview, Jobs, Pay and invoices, Documents, Leave and availability, Quality and coaching, Access, Activity; opened tabs stay mounted so drafts survive); rail (Contact, Device and last seen — Device "Not recorded" —, Payroll and bank, Office notes).
- **Old list URLs.** `/v2/admin/clients`, `/v2/admin/cleaners`, `/v2/admin/workforce` redirect to the matching People tab keeping the query string, for viewers who may open People (ADMIN, or OPS_MANAGER with the accounts pack). A manager whose packs admit the old page but not People keeps the old page (no lockout) until the middleware admits People tabs by their own pack. `/v2/admin/users` redirects keeping its query and old tab. Sub-pages (compliance, coaching, recognition, roster, home addresses, performance) are unchanged and linked.
- **Not built (reported):** Revoke invitation (the token has no revoked state; expiring it would read as "expired"); Send by SMS instead (no SMS path for account emails); a stored copy of the email sent; Move an assistant to another team (no path moves a login between teams; re-inviting another team's assistant is refused by design); bulk Add payment (no bulk pay-adjustment endpoint; per person in More); bulk invitation Export; partners' "properties served" and "on-time pickups" (not in the directory read model); Document requests in the account emails list (they stay in Documents and Compliance); device tracking.

## Change Log

- **2026-11-12 (Notification types and wording):** "Types and wording" tab as drawn: types grouped by audience with existing switches and transports, details, wording editor per transport with variables, required-variable check, SMS length, live preview, versions (compare, restore, reset), Regenerate wording as a proposal from the configured AI provider, Send test to me with explicit transports including SMS. Wording layer with one render function (byte-identical without overrides), override store with versions, stale-write guard and audit; email-template parts edit the shared templates. Access: ADMIN or operations manager with notifications at Manage, never while impersonating. Fixes: stale reads across requests, organisation timezone on log/health times and log date filter. No schema change.

- **2026-11-12 (Access and permissions):** Settings › Access and permissions replaces Roles and permissions (old URL redirects): per-person levels Off/View/Manage/Full over every ops area, one capability catalog with server enforcement through a shared `can()` evaluator (defaults reproduce earlier access, parity-tested), office-assistant presets, copy access, plain-language summary, PIN-confirmed save with conflict view, history from audit rows, client assistant teams via existing VA routes, and individually grantable administrator capabilities (password/two-step reset, Test as, edit access) with server guardrails. No schema change. See A3b.

- **2026-11-12 (Cleaner portal redesign):** Five phone tabs (Today, Jobs, Supplies, Pay, More) with a role-filtered More menu at `/v2/cleaner/more`; Today with clock state, next job, offers, needs attention and my day; Supplies To do / Shopping / Counts / Urgent / My stock; Pay This period / Invoices / Requests (old invoice and pay request URLs redirect); job screen header, five-stage rail, sticky bottom action, "Before you submit" jump links, after-submit summary and urgent stock in the + sheet. Presentation only; no schema or API change. See "Cleaner portal redesign: five tabs, Today, Supplies, Pay, More and the job screen (2026-11-12)".

- **2026-11-12 (Properties list and property page redesign):** Properties list as drawn (server paging/sorting/filters with real counts, stat cards, flags from existing data, List / Cards / Map, preview panel on `?open=`, bulk Set laundry partner / Assign checklist / Sync calendars / Export with per-property outcomes) and the property page as drawn (New job here, key facts, seven tabs with old `?tab=` links mapped, Coming up read model, sectioned Overview cards and rail). No schema change. See "Properties list and property page redesign (2026-11-12)".

- **2026-11-12 (Owner reports: photos, laundry partners, laundry team, account emails):** Lost and found photos show through `GET /api/lost-found/[id]/photo` (access re-checked, signed redirect, honest "Image could not be loaded" with Retry) everywhere they appear; laundry partners appear in Inventory › Suppliers beside stock suppliers (link to the stock record of the same business, team of laundry portal users, turnaround, runs link; migration `20261112210000_laundry_partner_links`); Pipeline pickup / drop-off / return / photo requests go to the partner's laundry team (scope `LAUNDRY_TASK_SUPPLIER_TEAM`, catalog `laundry.team_request`, disabled with the reason and Chase supplier offered when the partner has no portal users; cleaners asked only about the bags; laundry portal "Requests from the office"); Accounts "Account emails" resends invitation link, password reset link and sign-up code with fresh tokens, cool-down, audit and recorded provider states, plus Copy invitation link; "Resend invite" fixed to send the invitation link to invited people; forgot password rate-limited and recorded.

- **2026-11-11 (Quality, Wave C):** Quality Cleaners rows can "Propose bonus" by hand (`POST /api/admin/quality/bonus-proposals`: a PENDING pay adjustment written as the admin create route writes one, $5–$200, reason required, idempotent, 409 with the list when a bonus is already pending; ADMIN, or OPS_MANAGER with payroll and quality at manage; paid only after the existing approval); one visible row action with the others in a row menu; severities read Low / Medium / High through `lib/quality/severity.ts` (stored and query values unchanged; CSV adds "Severity label"). No schema change. See "Quality design completion: manual bonus proposal, both row actions, Low / Medium / High (Wave C, 2026-11-11)".

- **2026-11-11 (Live ops follow-up):** Live ops "Notify client" (ADMIN; confirm with recipients, wording and ETA; idempotent per day with explicit "Send another"; row shows who requested and when) and "Pause clock" (ADMIN; `POST /api/admin/jobs/[id]/pause-clock` through the shared `clockOutCleaner`, audited, cleaner notified, resumes with the existing Resume clock); declining a continuation requires a reason (400 without; Live ops, Command, job pages); Cases "Raise QA issue for cleaner" (`POST /api/admin/quality/issues`, marked `case:<id>` in `itemKey`, no deduction) with "Raised from case" in Quality; Laundry Ask cleaner / Request photo with a 30-minute server cool-down; Accounts bulk Message; Cases manual Refresh restored. No schema change. See "Live ops follow-up: delay notice, clock pause, decline reason, QA issue from a case, laundry nudges, bulk message (2026-11-11)".

New catalog of all 45 notification types with a completeness test; `/v2/admin/notifications` (former Comms center) becomes the one area: Types, Delivery log (pipeline + direct + provider events, masked), Health, Preferences; nav entry and settings destination added, old settings tabs kept. Composer sends recorded. See "Notifications in one place (2026-11-11)".

### 2026-09-09: Jobs columns and form-revision prerequisite

Added optional Jobs columns with strict URL/snapshot validation and read-only compatibility for older stored views; five local PostgreSQL tests left no retained fixtures. Added a server-only canonical form-revision helper, verified alongside real assembly/reference resolution in 58 tests with mocked signing. Submission/recovery wiring and admin visual verification remain open.

Post-integration verification: full TypeScript passed; the full Vitest suite passed 3,714 tests across 288 files, with 14 tests skipped in four files. Device/provider and remaining browser gates are tracked separately.

### 2026-09-09: Cleaner Property info keyboard focus

Property info and access imagery now use the existing Radix dialog primitive for accessible naming, independent focus containment and opener restoration. Escape closes nested access imagery before the drawer. Fifteen component tests passed; visible-browser outer-drawer Escape/Close restored the trigger with no console errors. Nested image viewer browser checks remain separate.

### 2026-09-09: Personal admin Jobs views

Added named personal views, Jobs-only density, URL/default precedence and acknowledged revision-protected preference writes. Seven focused test files passed 104 tests, TypeScript passed, and three local PostgreSQL checks rolled back all test records; admin browser verification remains pending an authorized test session.

### 2026-09-09: Shared cleaner form assembly

Read and submit now share optional-additional construction and schema normalization, including generated rework forms. Submit snapshots preserve the extra fields visible to cleaners. The combined form/reference/report regression run passed 115 tests; route execution, actual persistence and browser/provider gates remain separately tracked.

Eleven real POST-handler tests now cover the assembled schema and stored snapshot contract with mocked database/services. Route date/travel controls gained accessible names; visible-browser checks confirmed empty-state route/calendar interaction without job/GPS mutations.

### 2026-09-09: Nested form reference images

Child-field reference images now receive the same URL resolution as top-level fields, preserving keys and sibling progress on signing failure. Nine focused mocked-provider cases cover nesting, no-parent-reference cases, unchanged external URLs, immutability, partial failure and malformed inputs. Shared form assembly work and remaining parity gates are tracked separately.

### 2026-09-09: Actor-bound cleaner local drafts

New local drafts use validated identity-scoped envelopes, derived from real actor/effective cleaner/job on the server. Page and form identities must match before restoration; draft GET/PATCH/DELETE checks supplied identity against the resolved session. Old unscoped records stay untouched with an ownership warning. Shared server behavior and classic headerless compatibility remain unchanged. Verification and remaining form-version/browser/upload boundaries are recorded in the tracker.

Post-integration TypeScript passed. The full Vitest run passed 3,524 tests across 276 files, with nine tests in three files skipped (explicit database gates and the legacy draft-store test). These automated results do not certify browser, device, or provider workflows.

### 2026-09-09: Cleaner draft acknowledgement and mutation locking

Draft PATCH performs active-assignment and existing job-lock checks plus shared read/merge/save under a per-job READ COMMITTED transaction/advisory lock. Save and clear helpers participate in the same protocol, including submit cleanup. Same-editor replacement and cross-editor merging remain unchanged. The broader submit workflow is not made atomic by this change. Draft API responses are private/no-store with sanitized unexpected errors.

The v2 workspace validates server save acknowledgements and displays pending, acknowledged or error states with an explicit retry. Immutable saves queue FIFO; edits invalidate old indicators, submit clears pending work, and unmount suppresses queued dispatch. Local edits mirror before network debounce. Workspace mounts are keyed by effective cleaner and job. Local recovery remains job-keyed and is not yet a durable actor/form-scoped upload queue; interrupted server outcomes and full submit integration remain open verification gates in the execution tracker.

Initial restoration now fails explicitly without enabling autosave when the shared draft read fails or is malformed. Stale responses cannot hydrate over newer state. Refresh failures preserve mounted controls only for transient errors; authorization denial clears content and pending saves. Verification includes 131 focused tests and four rollback-only checks against local PostgreSQL; browser/device and durable queue gates remain separate.

### 2026-09-09: Evidence upload receipt integrity

The shared v2 capture pipeline preserves successful receipts when temporary video cleanup fails, validates direct/multipart key and URL strings before returning attachments, and does not automatically retry failed videos based on their compressed size. Explicit recovery is still required for incomplete receipts. The existing IndexedDB draft helper is not yet wired into v2 capture; durable cross-navigation recovery remains unfinished.

### 2026-09-09: Access-guide audience isolation

Per-job briefing reference history excludes jobs scheduled on or after the current job date. This is prior scheduled-job context, not proof of a cleaner's physical visit. Briefing and property-access reads explicitly return private/no-store responses with Vary: Cookie; unexpected server exceptions are generic 503 responses without internal error text.

Property access reads filter stored entries by the existing cleaner/laundry audience rules before projecting display fields. This prevents projection from turning restricted entries into implicitly shared entries. The daily cleaner briefing fallback and image-presence indicator use the same predicate. Existing property assignment/team authorization and legacy shared-entry behavior are unchanged. Pre-arrival change summaries remain pending a reliable historical baseline; current values are not evidence of changes since a visit.

### 2026-09-09: Cleaner timing hierarchy and Sydney route dates

Route Timeline date loads cancel superseded requests and ignore stale completions, including delayed JSON parsing. Successful responses are validated for matching calendar date and unique complete stop records. Previous stops and their actions are withheld during loading/error or scope changes; failed requests can be retried with Refresh. Empty-state claims require a validated successful load.

Shared cleaner segmented buttons expose their selected state through `aria-pressed`. Route mode switching has isolated component coverage; the live-browser switching discrepancy remains tracked separately and is not certified fixed.

Calendar compact clock labels and Timeline planned-time labels now validate clock strings before presentation, while retaining the shared invalid-time warning. Calendar keeps valid finish-only schedules visible.

Review corrections keep invalid early-arrival overrides unconfirmed instead of substituting a later standard arrival, validate detail-banner times, show finish-only planned times in Jobs, and include timing on the Drive next-stop panel. Route estimation anchors on first-stop access where known; reordering rejects a newly affected deadline even if the total missed-deadline count stays unchanged. These are advisory local route calculations, not scheduling mutations or verified travel-provider estimates.

Cleaner Today, Jobs, Calendar, job-detail banners, route timeline and inline Drive mode use a shared planned/guest timing summary. Explicit early arrival overrides standard same-day arrival, matching existing arrival rules. Late checkout and guest deadlines constrain route estimates; contradictions are advisory and do not rewrite schedules. Route page/API now use Sydney day boundaries with strict explicit-date validation, private API errors and an explicit page retry state. Assignment ownership remains enforced and raw internal notes remain server-side. See wave 17 of the execution tracker for tests and incomplete visual/type-check gates.

- 2026-09-09: Jobs assignment/bulk dialogs now use a scoped Radix component with modal semantics, focus management, viewport scrolling and busy dismissal protection. Opacity-only entry preserves centering and reduced-motion settings; mobile/desktop/keyboard and mocked busy-request browser checks passed without business mutations.
- 2026-09-09: Upload drafts recover failed/blocked IndexedDB opens and invalid connections, reject transaction aborts and resolve only on completed transactions; no automatic write replay. Integrated 44 tests and TypeScript passed, with actual isolated Chromium persistence/failure-recovery checks. Whole offline queue and real-phone quota/provider scenarios remain pending.

- 2026-09-09: Jobs search/invoice filters now apply server-side before pagination to both counts and results, and are reused by export. Search is bounded, wildcard-literal and debounced; refinements reset page one. Actual browser search found a QA job outside the initial page; invoice partition, page reset, mobile layout and filtered CSV verified. Export's existing 5,000-row cap remains.
- 2026-09-09: Database schema tests require explicit local test URL and roll back self-owned fixtures, including assertion failures; four real PostgreSQL checks passed with no retained fixtures. Added isolated Chromium upload-draft persistence verification, covering blob save/reload/update/delete; real-phone quota/offline conditions remain unverified.

- 2026-09-09: Fixed-inventory single-worker non-DB regression run passed 2,864 tests with one skipped; all 244 files accounted for and test/config hashes unchanged. TypeScript and scoped browser workflows passed separately. Database schema tests, production build and provider/device checks remain outside this evidence; earlier silent test exits were not reproduced or attributed to an unproven cause.

- 2026-09-09: Admin Jobs list state is URL-backed and restored before fetching, including page, date/filter/sort and list/board selection. Stale requests cannot overwrite the current query; malformed or failed results render explicit Retry without fabricated empty/zero results. Browser Back/reload and 503 recovery verified; 79 combined Jobs/notification tests and TypeScript passed. Search/invoice refinement remains page-local; queue navigation/team views are not yet implemented.

- 2026-09-09: Approval decisions require the client-displayed version and reject stale reviews under the existing transaction lock; classic and v2 clients refresh before another decision. Local isolated PostgreSQL concurrency verification passed without changing application history. Read-only AI configuration reports provider/model/key presence and explicitly untested connectivity; composition has validated output and bounded generic failures, with no live provider verification.
- 2026-09-09: Notification history adds opt-in recipient-scoped timestamp/id cursor pages; the existing nonpaginated array contract remains. Shared inbox loads older pages, deduplicates records and retries failures without discarding loaded rows. Focused route/component and mobile browser checks passed; read/unread lifecycle and provider/device delivery remain pending.

- 2026-09-09: Admin Jobs rows now adapt to available panel width rather than viewport width, preventing property names from collapsing beside the sidebar. Compact rows preserve metadata/timing badges and visible actions; nested keyboard controls do not navigate the parent row. Three regression tests, TypeScript and six browser viewport checks passed; Manage destination verified without business-record writes.

- 2026-09-09: Agent-assisted wave 6 adds cleaner active-job continuity, GPS queue identity isolation, approval UI reliability and atomic approval-store writes; 359 tests passed, with scoped local browser and advisory-lock checks.

- 2026-09-09: Agent-assisted wave 5 adds remembered navigation groups, truthful client dashboard failures, and session-isolated shared badge/maintenance refresh; 253 integrated tests and TypeScript passed, with cleaner navigation and client mobile browser checks.

- 2026-09-09: Agent-assisted wave 4 adds the shared recent-notification inbox, private history error handling and live-listener identity cleanup; 211 integrated tests passed, with cleaner desktop/mobile browser checks and explicit remaining delivery/lifecycle gates.

- 2026-09-09: Agent-assisted wave 3 fixes login CSRF-response/hydration handling, client/VA navigation refresh and scoped badges, and Laundry dashboard failure states. Integrated suite: 165 tests; tracker includes browser evidence and remaining gaps.

- 2026-09-09: Agent-assisted wave 2 adds per-user navigation favorites and QA dashboard error/DST fixes; notification stream hardening is verified separately in the UI/UX execution tracker.

- 2026-09-09: First UI/UX execution slice: shared searchable/accessibility-improved navigation, stable mobile tabs, Maintenance count/error/date fixes, safer role/version notification destinations and honest unsupported template-push status. Full 106-item backlog tracked separately; not a whole-platform completion.

One line per shipped wave. Newest first. (Add to this list with every future wave — see the maintenance protocol at the top.)

- **2026-08-24** — **Assistants become first-class accounts in the admin portal: a directory, admin-editable permissions, and an activity trail that actually has entries.** Three gaps, one shape. (1) The accounts page could CREATE assistants but never LIST them — the assistants tab held only the client-scoped invite manager, so a VA onboarded last month appeared nowhere unless you remembered which client they belonged to, while every other account type got a flat list. `components/v2/admin/accounts/va-directory.tsx` is that list: every VA across every client with client/team/grant context, the SAME account menu every other row gets (edit, reset password, reset 2FA, delete — `/api/admin/users/[id]/*` was always role-agnostic), a Profile link, and a stranded-login warning for a VA with no team (an input with no output: a login that can access nothing). The users GET now returns `vaTeam` (null for everyone else). (2) **An assistant's grants were editable only by the CLIENT they act for** — admin va-invites had GET+POST only, so the admin who onboarded a team could not correct its permissions without asking the client. New `PATCH/DELETE /api/admin/va-invites/[teamId]` reuses `updateVaTeam`/`deleteVaTeam` (one implementation, three doors: client portal, admin, and the invite flow) and writes an AuditLog row with the before-image — "who widened this team, from what" is the question an audit exists to answer. The invite-flow rule stands: adding a member still never rewrites grants. The directory carries the editor (grants, property scope, team active), and the admin profile page gains a "Delegated access" card for VAs — previously it rendered a VA as a strangely empty cleaner. (3) **The activity feed existed but VA rows were near-empty**: of 28 mutating client-portal handlers, only the four va-team routes audited. Every VA-REACHABLE mutating handler now writes an AuditLog row through `auditClientPortalAction` — booking, cases (create + update), maintenance, messages, cancel/reschedule/skip requests (+ skip withdrawal), light requests, task requests (create/update/withdraw), preferred cleaner (set/clear) — 14 handlers across 12 routes, each after the mutation succeeds, each carrying the actor label so the feed reads "did X for client Y". The 12 `requireRole([CLIENT])`-only mutating routes were deliberately NOT converted: a VA cannot reach them (paying invoices is on the VA forbidden list), and widening guards is a permission decision, not a logging one.
